# 万物生｜主篇中段·大漠沙猎机械追逐、黑白武侠与全套美术设定

> 拆分为「视频动作分镜」与「四视图人设／载具武器／手绘故事板／13 色复合色卡／电影海报」，已剥离原始资产编码与局部修图短指令，并去重。


## 一、大漠机械追逐与黑白武侠分镜


### 第1条｜飞船 甲板上的纯写实空场景+无任何角色人物入画+无任何动作发生+所有物件保持

Shot on 35mm Kodak Vision3 500T film stock + analog film grain visible + practical light only + Christopher Doyle cinematography + Wong Kar-wai painterly desaturation + photochemical film texture + slight halation on highlights + organic film noise + heavy atmospheric haze + dust particles in light beams + tactile real-world materials + lived-in worn surfaces + film print look not digital cinema look. Photorealistic film still aesthetic+real physical scene captured through a camera lens+not a 3D render.

@图1 作为飞船甲板与船体细节的视觉锚定+船体所有结构/木质木纹质感/铜青绿色调/机械装置/古代红色金色装饰/布幡铜钟严格按此图渲染+不可改造。

整段一个连续长镜头+no cuts no transitions+严格single take。

【镜头与机位设置】
镜头:50mm prime lens标准焦段+rectilinear lens projection直线投影+no barrel distortion+no fisheye+no wide-angle warping at edges+no edge stretching+几何线条在画面任何位置都保持笔直+方形物体在画面边缘不扭曲为梯形+人眼最自然视角+王家卫《2046》《阿飞正传》Christopher Doyle 50mm prime使用风格。

机位:nodal pan locked tripod shot+相机以三脚架方式固定锚定在飞船甲板正中心一个不可移动的物理锚点上+三脚架插在木质甲板地板上+相机位置坐标在整段视频中保持完全不变+相机仅绕自身的垂直Y轴做纯轴心旋转+camera spins on its own vertical axis like a security camera or a lighthouse beam+360度完整旋转一圈+回到起始朝向+匀速旋转无加速无减速。

相机高度:站立人眼高度约1.6米+水平视线+不仰拍不俯拍。

50mm焦段视角约40度+一次只能看到甲板的一个局部方向+这是预期效果+不需要一帧塞下整个甲板+整段视频通过360度旋转扫过所有方向+每一个方向的甲板局部都被记录下来作为后续静帧素材使用。

【画面内容 空场景写实】
飞船@图1 甲板上的纯写实空场景+无任何角色人物入画+无任何动作发生+所有物件保持静态+只有相机自转。

甲板可见元素:风化木质甲板地板+做旧古代红色金色装饰板+破旧布幡在风中自然飘动+铜钟悬挂在某处+古代机械装置+古代符号雕刻+铜青绿主色调+暗金装饰+夕阳金色侧光+船舱结构+木质栏杆+麻绳+小物件+真实的尘土与磨损痕迹+整体氛围沧桑破旧机神教感+film stock捕捉到的真实物质质感。

旋转一圈过程中相机依次扫过甲板四个方向:甲板前方船头方向+甲板右舷+甲板后方船尾方向+甲板左舷+回到甲板前方。每一面都展示该方向的甲板环境与船体结构静态细节。

【光线】
golden hour夕阳侧光+#F5C870夕阳金+#C8581F落日橙背景天空+#1A1610深阴影。光线从某一固定方向打入甲板(光源在世界坐标系中位置不变)+随相机旋转+夕阳光从画面不同侧入画+相机转向光源时正面逆光lens flare+转背时背光。空气中可见#F5C870金色光柱条纹+尘土漂浮+atmospheric haze。

【声音设计】
环境音:风声呼啸+布幡rip-flap飘动+铜钟微弱clink+船身木质吱呀声+远处低频脚步震动感+无对白+无bgm。

【关键约束】
镜头铁律 50mm rectilinear:50mm prime lens严格直线投影+所有直线几何在画面任何位置都保持笔直+船体甲板上的木板缝直线+栏杆直线+船体边缘直线在画面边缘绝对不弯曲+方形物体在画面任何位置都不扭曲变形+absolutely no barrel distortion+absolutely no pincushion distortion+absolutely no fisheye effect+absolutely no wide-angle edge warping。

机位铁律1 nodal pivot only:相机机身位置坐标在整段视频中绝对不变+相机仅绕自身垂直轴旋转+像保安监控摄像头spinning on its mount+像灯塔光源旋转+camera body does not translate or move through space+only rotates around its own vertical axis。

机位铁律2 no orbit:绝对禁止环绕拍摄+绝对禁止围绕飞船的orbit shot+绝对禁止相机绕飞船外部转一圈展示船的外观+相机始终在甲板内部中心点+永远不离开甲板中心。

机位铁律3 no translation:no dolly+no track+no crane+no jib+no boom+no zoom+no push in+no pull out+no lateral movement+only pure on-axis rotation。

机位铁律4 no parallax:旋转时画面中近景物体不应该相对于远景物体移动位置+视差应该是纯旋转视差+不是位移视差+背景物体在水平方向匀速划过画面但不远近变化。

主体铁律:相机的视野中始终是甲板内部环境+永远不出现飞船外部全景+永远不离开甲板。

静态铁律:所有甲板物件保持静态+船舵不旋转+无角色操作+无机械运动+只有布幡飘动与铜钟轻晃这种被风带动的自然小幅摆动+所有大型物件物理位置完全不变。

风格铁律:35mm film stock photography texture+analog film grain+photochemical look+NOT 3D rendered+NOT CGI+NOT video game graphics+NOT Unreal Engine render+NOT Blender render+NOT clean digital surface+NOT plastic look+NOT smooth artificial texture+all surfaces must show real physical wear and weathering+film print quality not digital cinema clean。

NOT wide angle lens+NOT fisheye+NOT barrel distortion+NOT 24mm or shorter focal length+NOT orbit shot+NOT 360 around the ship+NOT external view of the ship+NOT camera flying around the ship+NOT drone shot+NOT crane shot+NOT dolly+NOT tracking shot+NOT character action+NOT slow motion+NOT cyberpunk neon+NOT cartoon+NOT illustrated+NOT 3D animation+NOT video game cinematic+无字幕+无水印。

### 第3条｜全程使用白噪音作为bgm

全程使用白噪音作为bgm。**起始约0-2秒**：从near total whiteness渐显（承接段3尾白云遮罩）+摄影机在云海上方+上帝视角top-down bird's-eye view俯瞰。云海#F0EBE0+暮蓝灰#B8C4D4阴影面+朝晖#D4884A+粉霞#E8A878从画面边缘+一架墨家机关飞行器Mojia mechanical flying machine在云海中飞行。飞行器是古代中国木造机关结构（视觉参考《长城》木造攻城器+《天空之城》Robot Soldier+《风之谷》空中战舰）+木暖褐#6E4A28主体+铜暗金#8E6B3A齿轮+金属灰#4A4438金属节点+木制机关翅膀缓慢拍动wooden wings flapping。
 
**约2-5秒**：摄影机开始orbit shot绕飞行器+camera orbiting in sweeping arc+从俯瞰top-down view→3/4高角度→侧面side angle→飞行器前方in front of the machine+视角逐渐从俯瞰转为正面。视觉参考《Top Gun: Maverick》飞机绕弧+《奇异博士》空间旋转。
 
**约5-7秒**：摄影机位于飞行器前方+对着驾驶员。揭示墨家子弟Mojia disciple——古代中国男性工匠+30岁+黑色外袍#2A2A2A+青灰内衫#4A5258+束发+**额头戴着青铜防风眼罩bronze leather aviator goggles pushed up on forehead**（青铜框+皮革带+圆形镜片+古代飞行员护目镜）。**关键构图：荷兰角45度倾斜Dutch angle 45 degrees + 墨家子弟在画面偏右1/3不居中off-center**。**表演开始**：墨家子弟悠闲哼着小曲humming a folk tune casually+嘴部跟旋律微动+面部松弛+自得微笑relaxed self-satisfied expression。
 
**约7-9秒**：墨家子弟随手从怀里掏出邀请函casually pulls out invitation from his robe+漫不经心地举到眼前+带着小得意的微笑斜睨邀请函self-satisfied smirk eyes glance at the card with smug expression+回味拿到邀请函的得意。邀请函深蓝底#1F2840+art deco金色几何纹路#C9A050+古代封印字符。
 
**约9-10秒**：邀请函突然被强风从手中卷走a sudden gust of wind catches the card+invitation slips from his fingers+caught off guard。
 
**约10-11秒**：**墨家子弟表情瞬间反转**face shifts dramatically from relaxed to shocked+eyes widen+mouth opens in surprise+伸手猛地去抓邀请函lunges forward to grab the card。
 
**约11-12秒**：邀请函向镜头飞来+越来越大approaching the camera+最终糊到镜头上invitation slams flat against the camera lens+完全占满画面filling the entire frame+邀请函深蓝底#1F2840填满画面+near total darkness。整段在邀请函糊镜头近全黑状态下结束。
 
【声音设计】
全程：BGM v5万国来朝版（climax层）+Hans Zimmer风+rich layered tutti。
0-5秒：高空风声+飞行器机关声+木质吱嘎wood creaking+风声渐强wind whoosh as camera orbits。
5-9秒：风声+墨家子弟哼小曲humming softly+邀请函抽出纸张摩擦声paper friction。
9-12秒：邀请函脱手纸张拍打声paper flapping wildly+撞镜头瞬间sound becomes muffled。
 
【关键约束】
- 整段必须是1个连续镜头+orbit shot绕飞行器+无切镜
- 起始约2秒必须从near total whiteness渐显（承接段3尾白云遮罩）
- 约2-5秒摄影机必须做orbit shot从俯瞰绕到飞行器前方+camera path top-down → 3/4 → side → front
- 约5-7秒必须荷兰角45度倾斜Dutch angle 45 degrees+墨家子弟在画面偏右1/3不居中off-center
- 结尾约1秒必须邀请函糊镜头+near total darkness
- 飞行器是古代墨家木造机关飞行器Mojia ancient Chinese wooden mechanical flying machine+NOT modern aircraft+NOT helicopter+NOT spaceship+NOT industrial revolution airplane+NOT steampunk
- 墨家子弟是古代中国男性+黑衣古装+NOT modern person+NOT Western character+NOT samurai
- 邀请函必须深蓝底#1F2840+art deco金色#C9A050几何纹路+和段3邀请函视觉一致
- NOT graphic violence
 
【通用约束】
真人电影实拍质感 cinematic realistic film。NOT cartoon, NOT illustrated, NOT 3D render, NOT CGI, NOT anime。NOT bright kelly green, NOT cheerful saturated colors。only BGM音乐+无人声no voice-over no narration。no subtitles, no watermark。

### 第5条｜万物生第1集 沙猎部落 机械赛加羚羊 兽形态设计图 v1

**万物生第1集 沙猎部落 机械赛加羚羊 兽形态设计图 v1**
【格式】
- 16:9横版+最高分辨率
- 4格均等2x2 = SIDE + FRONT + TOP + 3/4 VIEW
- 同studio backdrop+single-side rim backlight环境（继承沙猎部落photoshoot DNA）

【兽设定】
精英沙匪搭档——**机械赛加羚羊（Mechanical Saiga）**——独立形态。本张是兽自身的兽形态人设，合体外骨骼形态后续做。

- **种属**：纯机械（古代失落科技遗物+无生物组织+半智能AI）
- **战术**：群体冲撞+蹄踏+gallop颠簸
- **signature视觉记忆点**：奇特bulbous长鼻（机械化）+螺旋竖角
- **体型**：肩高1.2米+体长1.5米（真实赛加放大约1.5倍——保留比例不变成马）
- **DNA**：和合体外骨骼+人物机改+沙艇全部同青铜patina

---

A creature design reference sheet. **Horizontal 16:9 aspect ratio. Maximum resolution output.** Pure solid neutral light-warm-grey studio backdrop. Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45 degrees behind the subject, no fill light, no front light, no other light sources—dramatic cinematic studio lookbook lighting. Same studio environment as the Sand Hunter character sheets and sand-skiff design sheet—reads as part of the same photoshoot session.

【Layout】

**2x2 equal grid**:
- Top-left: SIDE VIEW (full broadside profile, the primary view)
- Top-right: FRONT VIEW (head-on, showing facial features prominently—the bulbous nose and horns)
- Bottom-left: TOP-DOWN VIEW (god's-eye view from directly above showing footprint and dorsal mechanical layout)
- Bottom-right: 3/4 PERSPECTIVE VIEW (three-quarter angle showing depth and dimensionality, hero shot)

Thin neutral light-grey dividing lines between all four panels. Creature centered in each panel with even space margin.

【Subject — same creature across all 4 panels】

A **mechanical Saiga antelope**—an ancient lost-technology mechanical war-beast, modeled after the Central Asian Saiga antelope species, retrofitted as a war-mount for the Sand Hunter tribe's elite raiders. NOT a biological creature, NOT a cyborg with biological tissue—**fully mechanical construction with no organic components**, but with semi-sentient AI consciousness reactivated by the tribe (a relic of a long-lost mechanical civilization, now serving new masters).

**SPECIES IDENTIFICATION (CRITICAL)**:
**Saiga antelope** (Saiga tatarica)—the Central Asian steppe-desert species native to the Sui-Tang frontier territories. Highly specific anatomy:
- **Bulbous oversized proboscis-like nose** hanging down past the mouth, soft and drooping in life (in this mechanical version: rendered as a bronze mechanical proboscis with visible air-filtration intake slits and small exhaust ports along its length—the signature feature of the species, the most critical recognition marker—if this nose is missing or replaced with a regular antelope muzzle, the design fails)
- **Vertical spiraled horns** on males—straight-up-pointing horns with translucent ringed spirals (in this mechanical version: rendered as bronze mechanical horns with the original spiraled ringed structure, sharpened at the tips for combat use)
- **Compact stocky body** with relatively short legs (NOT long-legged like a deer or gazelle, NOT slim like a normal antelope—the Saiga is built like a muscular sheep-antelope hybrid)
- NOT a regular antelope, NOT a deer, NOT a gazelle, NOT a goat, NOT a sheep—the Saiga has a unique unmistakable silhouette dominated by the bulbous nose

**Scale**: Approximately 1.2 meters at the shoulder, 1.5 meters body length nose-to-tail. Slightly larger than a real Saiga (about 1.5x scale) for war-mount viability, but maintaining authentic Saiga body proportions—NOT scaled up to horse-size (must read as Saiga, not horse-shaped antelope).

**Construction (CRITICAL)**:
**Fully mechanical, no biological tissue.** The body is constructed of:
- **Bronze mechanical bone-and-muscle armature**: visible mechanical "skeleton" of bronze rods, joints, gears, hydraulic-style pistons (clockwork-driven not steam) where the biological skeleton would be—giving the creature its anatomical proportions
- **Bronze panel armor "fur"**: the surface is covered in overlapping bronze panel-plates that approximate the silhouette of fur—not actual fur, NOT biological skin—segmented bronze panels with weathered patina, joint-line gaps showing mechanical movement underneath
- **Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845 + Aged Gold #846035 patina** with verdigris in seam recesses—exactly matching the Sand Hunter tribe's combat exoskeletons, prosthetics, sphere-companion, and skiff drive units (consistent universe DNA)
- **Tribal geometric runic glyphs** etched into the larger bronze panels (matching the Qingtong Sphere and tribe equipment glyph aesthetic—abstract pre-civilization mechanical-runic geometry, NOT Chinese decorative motifs, NOT real-culture runes)

**Bulbous mechanical nose (CRITICAL signature feature)**:
A large pendulous bronze proboscis hanging down past the mouth line, approximately 25cm long, reproducing the iconic Saiga nose silhouette in mechanical form:
- Made of segmented bronze rings tapering toward the tip
- Visible **slatted air-intake vents** along the upper surface (the Saiga's biological function was filtering desert dust—the mechanical version retains this purpose with mechanical filters)
- Small **exhaust ports** at the rear sides emitting faint visible heat-shimmer or steam-trace
- Slight downward droop matching biological Saiga posture
- Verdigris patina in deep recesses

**Horns**:
A pair of vertical spiraled mechanical horns mounted on the head, approximately 30cm long, pointing nearly straight up with slight forward angle. Each horn is bronze with the characteristic Saiga ringed-spiral pattern preserved (the ridged twist visible). Sharpened metal tips for combat use. Slight battle-wear visible on the spiral edges.

**Eyes**:
Small round mechanical eyes with **glowing cyan inner light #4FC3D9** (matching the Qingtong Sphere companion's eye glow—a deliberate visual hint at shared ancient lost-technology origin across all the universe's mechanical creatures). Bronze rim around each eye with thin glass-like lens cover. Eyes appear alert, semi-sentient, watching.

**Legs and hooves**:
Four mechanical legs with stocky proportions matching biological Saiga (relatively short legs for the body—NOT long-legged like a horse or deer). Each leg has visible bronze joint pivots at hip, knee, and ankle. **Cloven mechanical hooves** at the feet—two-toed bronze hoof shoes designed to grip sand. Visible wear and sand-pitting on the hoof surfaces. Lower legs show fine mechanical detail of the gallop-driving pistons.

**Tail**:
Short stubby mechanical tail (about 10cm)—matching biological Saiga's small tail. Bronze segmented construction.

**Cockpit/合体接口 (CRITICAL — visible in TOP and 3/4 panels)**:
On the **dorsal surface (back)** between the shoulder blades, a clearly visible **mechanical cockpit hatch / pilot integration interface**—a roughly human-torso-sized rectangular-oval seam with bronze locking clasps around its perimeter, visible hinges along one edge, and small step-rungs on the side flank for mounting access. This is where the elite raider partner climbs in and integrates with the war-beast. The hatch is currently CLOSED in this character sheet (showing the design but in dormant/standalone configuration). Below the hatch line, faintly visible internal mechanical structure suggests the harness/cradle inside.

**Tribal trophy decorations and partner-binding tokens**:
Moderate quantity of trophy decorations affixed to the war-beast:
- Leather-strap harness around the neck/chest with woven beads, polished bone fragments
- A small leather pouch tied to the saddle-area near the cockpit hatch (containing the partner-raider's personal token—a small carved bone amulet visible peeking out)
- 3-5 small trophies (defeated enemies' equipment fragments) tied to flanks via leather cords
- Tribal pattern dyed-cloth banding around the lower legs and around the base of the horns
- Mecha Crimson Bronze + Aged Gold + dried-blood-red color accents

**Weathering**:
War-torn weathered—heavy verdigris patina in deep recesses, sand-pitting on bronze surfaces, small dents from past combat, mismatched repair patches in slightly different bronze tones (the tribe has maintained this ancient relic for many generations using whatever bronze they could find). NOT clean, NOT polished, NOT factory-new.

【4 Panels detail】

**Top-left panel — SIDE VIEW**: Pure broadside profile view. Beast facing camera-right (head to the right). Full body visible nose-tail. The bulbous mechanical proboscis prominently visible drooping down from the head. Vertical spiraled horns visible. Stocky leg proportions visible. Cockpit hatch on the back visible from this angle. Dorsal trophy decorations visible. Side-rim backlight catches the upper back, horn tops, and rear flank.

**Top-right panel — FRONT VIEW**: Beast head facing camera directly. **The bulbous mechanical proboscis nose is the dominant feature in this panel**—centered, drooping, with air-intake vents and exhaust ports visible. Horns visible rising vertically with slight forward angle. Cyan eyes glowing on either side. Front legs visible flanking the chest, cloven hooves on the ground.

**Bottom-left panel — TOP-DOWN VIEW**: God's-eye view from directly overhead looking down at the beast. Shows the full footprint and dorsal mechanical layout. **The cockpit hatch on the back between the shoulder blades is most clearly visible in this view**—the rectangular-oval seam with locking clasps. Tribal pattern bronze panels visible in their full layout. Horns visible from above as paired vertical spires. Stocky body proportions clearly readable.

**Bottom-right panel — 3/4 PERSPECTIVE VIEW**: Three-quarter angle showing depth, approximately 30-45 degrees from front-side, slightly elevated viewpoint. Side profile + front + slight top all visible. The signature bulbous nose visible at this angle. Dorsal cockpit hatch visible from the elevated angle. Cyan eye glow catches the rim light. The "hero shot" of the four views—dynamic stance with one foreleg slightly raised as if mid-stride.

【Critical consistency】
SAME individual creature in all 4 panels—identical body proportions (stocky Saiga build), identical bulbous proboscis, identical horns, identical cockpit hatch position and design, identical trophy decoration arrangement, identical bronze panel pattern, identical weathering pattern. Same studio backdrop, same single-sided side-rim backlighting setup across all 4 panels.

【Lighting - critical】
**Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45-degrees behind subject. NO fill light. NO front light. NO ambient. NO other light sources.** Strong dramatic edge-light catches the right side of the creature silhouette in every panel, leaving the left side in deep falloff shadow with subtle ambient bounce. Cyan eye glow self-illuminates faintly on bronze panels adjacent to the eyes.

【Style】
Hyperrealistic photographic creature design rendering—shot like a high-end practical animatronic creature photographed in studio for a film production design board. NOT illustration, NOT obvious 3D CGI render.

**Reference aesthetics**:
- **Real-world Saiga antelope wildlife photography** (for accurate species anatomy—the bulbous nose, vertical spiral horns, stocky proportions are mandatory)
- **Studio Ghibli Princess Mononoke spirit-beast aesthetic** (for the ancient mythical-mechanical creature feel)
- **Ancient bronze Chinese ceremonial vessels and Han dynasty tomb mechanical figures** (古代青铜机关——for the lost-tech bronze patina and ringed-glyph aesthetic)
- **Pacific Rim mecha weathered combat aesthetic** (for the war-torn salvage maintained-by-primitive-hands feel)
- **Monster Hunter Wilds / Horizon Zero Dawn mechanical desert beasts** (for the contemporary mechanical-fauna aesthetic reference)

Light Kodak Vision3 35mm film grain texture overlay.

【Quality】
Maximum resolution output. 8k ultra clean rendering. Sharp clean edges. Every bronze panel seam, every gear tooth on visible joints, every air-vent slat on the proboscis, every spiral ring on the horns, every leather strap on the trophy decorations clearly defined. Real practical-creature animatronic photography aesthetic.

【Panel Labels】
Small clean monospace sans-serif black text below each panel:
- Top-left: "SIDE"
- Top-right: "FRONT"
- Bottom-left: "TOP"
- Bottom-right: "3/4 VIEW"

Title at top center of canvas (small clean black sans-serif): "MECHANICAL SAIGA — WAR-MOUNT DESIGN SHEET"

【Constraints】

**Species accuracy (MOST CRITICAL — high failure risk)**:
MUST be a Saiga antelope—the bulbous proboscis-like nose is the absolute mandatory recognition feature. NOT a regular antelope, NOT a deer, NOT a gazelle, NOT a goat, NOT a sheep, NOT an ibex, NOT a horse, NOT a cow. Vertical spiraled ringed horns (NOT curved horns, NOT spiral-curved like a kudu, NOT branched like deer antlers). Compact stocky body proportions (NOT long-legged like a horse or deer).

**Mechanical purity**:
Fully mechanical construction with NO biological tissue—NOT a cyborg, NOT biological-with-armor, NOT fur-and-flesh-with-mechanical-parts. ALL surfaces must be mechanical bronze-and-metal panels and components. NO real fur, NO real skin, NO real muscle, NO real eyes (mechanical eyes with cyan glow only).

**Aesthetic anchoring**:
NOT precision modern robotics, NOT clean factory-new metal (war-torn weathered patina only), NOT shiny chrome (aged bronze with verdigris only), NOT steampunk Victorian (this is ancient lost-tech NOT industrial-revolution), NOT cyberpunk neon, NOT obvious CGI 3D render, NOT cartoon, NOT anime, NOT fantasy elf-craft elegance, NOT pure militaristic mecha (must retain organic creature silhouette underneath the mechanical surface).

**Cockpit hatch presence**:
The dorsal cockpit hatch between shoulder blades MUST be visible in TOP-DOWN and 3/4 panels (this is the key story-element of the war-mount system). NOT a fully sealed creature—the partner-integration interface must be readable.

**Layout and lighting**:
NOT 1:1 square aspect (must be horizontal 16:9), NOT 9:16 vertical, NOT colorful background (solid neutral light-warm-grey only), NOT soft even neutral lighting (must be single-side rim backlight only), NOT front light, NOT fill light, NOT colorful saturated palette (muted desert bronze tones only), NOT any text other than the four panel labels and the title.

【实操注意】

1. 跑出来重点检查（按风险优先级）：
   - **奇特bulbous nose（MAX风险）**——Saiga的灵魂特征，AI模型很可能跑成普通羚羊muzzle。如果没跑出明显下垂的proboscis-like鼻，整图失败重做+按"the bulbous proboscis is the mandatory recognition feature, MUST be visibly oversized and pendulous, NOT a normal antelope muzzle"加强
   - **vertical spiraled竖直螺旋角**——容易跑成弯曲羚羊角（如kudu）或鹿角分叉。必须是vertical+spiraled+ringed
   - **stocky compact体型**——容易跑成长腿马或苗条鹿
   - **纯机械（无生物组织）**——容易跑成"fur-with-armor"生物机改。必须all bronze panels
   - **背部cockpit hatch**——这是和合体外骨骼系统的视觉钩子+必须在TOP/3-4 panels可见
   - **同沙猎部落青铜DNA**（#7A6845/#846035 patina+verdigris+部族纹）
   - **眼睛青色发光#4FC3D9**——同青铜球体小宠物的同源科技钩子
   - **16:9横版+4格均等**+SIDE/FRONT/TOP/3-4 VIEW

2. 跑歪兜底加强：
   - 跑成普通antelope/deer/gazelle → "Saiga tatarica species, distinguished by oversized bulbous proboscis-like nose hanging past mouth (THIS IS MANDATORY), vertical spiral-ringed horns straight up, stocky compact body, NOT a regular antelope/deer/gazelle"
   - 跑成生物机改（fur+armor）→ "fully mechanical construction, all bronze panel armor surface in place of fur, NO biological tissue, NO real fur, NO real skin"
   - 跑成现代精良机器 → "ancient lost-technology bronze relic, weathered war-torn patina, salvage-maintained by primitive tribal hands, NOT modern robotics"
   - 鼻子跑歪了 → "the bulbous mechanical proboscis is approximately 25cm long, hangs visibly past the mouth line in a soft droop, with slatted air-intake vents along upper surface and small exhaust ports at rear sides"
   - 角跑成弯曲 → "vertical straight-up-pointing horns with characteristic spiraled-ringed surface (like the biological Saiga horns), NOT curved horns like kudu or impala, NOT branched antlers like deer"
   - 看不到cockpit hatch → "dorsal mechanical cockpit hatch between shoulder blades clearly visible in TOP-DOWN and 3/4 perspective views, rectangular-oval shape with bronze locking clasps and hinges"

3. **关于"兽形态"vs"合体外骨骼形态"**：
   - 这张prompt是兽独立形态——cockpit hatch CLOSED+在dormant/standalone configuration
   - 合体外骨骼形态（沙匪钻进去之后）后续单独做+基于这张兽形态做参考


### 第6条｜主角船甲板上+一个大型青铜储物箱在画面中央偏下+箱盖完全打开

机位:medium shot+相机位于船甲板侧面+50mm prime lens+rectilinear lens projection+水平视线human eye level。
画面:
@图2 主角船甲板上+一个大型青铜储物箱在画面中央偏下+箱盖完全打开+
@图1 江流的整个上半身(头/胸/双臂)完全没入箱内消失+只露出他的下半身:腰部以下/屁股/双腿在画面里+腿在空中乱蹬乱踢flailing+#4A1F1F深酒红外袍后摆+#2D2A28深灰金属颈部机械义体的下半部分可见+江流的姿势是头朝下倒栽进箱子+整个上半身扎得太深拔不出来。

### 第7条｜万物生第1集 沙猎部落 机械奔跑鸟 兽形态设计图 v1

**万物生第1集 沙猎部落 机械奔跑鸟 兽形态设计图 v1**
【格式】
- 16:9横版+最高分辨率
- 4格均等2x2 = SIDE + FRONT + TOP + 3/4 VIEW
- 同studio backdrop+single-side rim backlight环境（继承沙猎部落photoshoot DNA）

【兽设定】
精英沙匪搭档——**机械奔跑鸟（Mechanical Runner-Bird）**——独立形态。本张是兽自身的兽形态人设，合体外骨骼形态后续做。

- **种属**：纯机械（古代失落科技遗物+无生物组织+半智能AI）
- **战术**：单独sprint追猎+喙刺+爪抓+猛禽侦察
- **signature视觉记忆点**：尖矛喙（末端微钩）+长机械尾+精瘦sprinter silhouette
- **体型**：高1.8-2米+喙到尾长2米（精瘦sprinter+不是恐鸟级巨型）
- **杂交参考**：恐鸟（Phorusrhacidae，尺寸+掠食喙）+ roadrunner（精瘦+sprint速度）
- **DNA**：和合体外骨骼+人物机改+沙艇+赛加全部同青铜patina

---

A creature design reference sheet. **Horizontal 16:9 aspect ratio. Maximum resolution output.** Pure solid neutral light-warm-grey studio backdrop. Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45 degrees behind the subject, no fill light, no front light, no other light sources—dramatic cinematic studio lookbook lighting. Same studio environment as the Sand Hunter character sheets, sand-skiff, and mechanical Saiga design sheets—reads as part of the same photoshoot session.

【Layout】

**2x2 equal grid**:
- Top-left: SIDE VIEW (full broadside profile, the primary view)
- Top-right: FRONT VIEW (head-on, showing predatory beak and frontal silhouette)
- Bottom-left: TOP-DOWN VIEW (god's-eye view from directly above showing footprint and dorsal mechanical layout)
- Bottom-right: 3/4 PERSPECTIVE VIEW (three-quarter angle showing depth and dimensionality, hero shot)

Thin neutral light-grey dividing lines between all four panels. Creature centered in each panel with even space margin.

【Subject — same creature across all 4 panels】

A **mechanical predatory runner-bird**—an ancient lost-technology mechanical war-beast in the form of a flightless desert sprinter, retrofitted as a war-mount for the Sand Hunter tribe's elite raiders. NOT a biological creature, NOT a cyborg with biological tissue—**fully mechanical construction with no organic components**, but with semi-sentient AI consciousness reactivated by the tribe.

**SPECIES IDENTIFICATION (CRITICAL)**:
A **hybrid silhouette of a Phorusrhacidae terror-bird crossed with a sprinting roadrunner**—a flightless predatory ground-bird:
- **From terror-bird (Phorusrhacidae)**: predatory hooked beak, ground-hunting carnivore stance, large powerful neck, scale-skin reptilian leg aesthetics
- **From roadrunner**: lean elongated horizontal body silhouette, long sprint-adapted legs, low-slung horizontal posture during running, long stabilizing tail
- The result is a lean fast predatory ground-bird, NOT a giant ostrich (NOT bulky, NOT plump), NOT a chicken, NOT a vulture, NOT a generic eagle. Lean predatory sprinter aesthetic.

**Scale**: Approximately **1.8-2 meters tall at the head when standing alert, 2 meters total length from beak tip to tail tip in horizontal running posture**. Slimmer than a real Phorusrhacidae (which were bulkier)—proportions favor a **lean fast sprinter** built. NOT a giant bird, NOT a small bird—mid-range war-mount scale.

**Construction (CRITICAL)**:
**Fully mechanical, no biological tissue.** The body is constructed of:
- **Bronze mechanical bone-and-muscle armature**: visible mechanical "skeleton" of bronze rods, joints, gears, hydraulic-style pistons (clockwork-driven not steam) where the biological skeleton would be
- **Bronze panel armor "feathers"**: the surface is covered in overlapping bronze panel-plates that approximate the silhouette of feathers—NOT actual feathers, NOT biological skin—segmented bronze panels arranged in feather-like overlapping rows along the body, neck, and wing stubs. Some panels are longer/thinner like flight feathers, others are smaller/scaled like body feathers, but ALL are bronze metal—NOT real feathers
- **Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845 + Aged Gold #846035 patina** with verdigris in seam recesses—exactly matching the Sand Hunter tribe's combat exoskeletons, prosthetics, sphere-companion, skiff drive units, and mechanical Saiga (consistent universe DNA)
- **Tribal geometric runic glyphs** etched into the larger bronze panels (matching the Qingtong Sphere and tribe equipment glyph aesthetic)

**Beak (CRITICAL signature feature — predatory weapon)**:
A long sharp predatory beak approximately 30-40cm long:
- **Primary form: spear-shape**—long, straight, tapering to a sharp piercing point (designed to thrust and stab)
- **Secondary feature: subtle hook at the tip**—the very end curves slightly downward into a small raptor-style hook for tearing flesh after the spear-thrust pierces
- Construction: solid dark bronze, edges sharpened, with visible mechanical hinge at the base where the upper and lower mandibles meet (jaw articulation)
- Slight battle-wear and chips visible on the beak edges—this is a working weapon
- NOT a flat duckbill, NOT a short chicken beak, NOT a curved fully-hooked vulture beak—primarily a spear with a small terminal hook

**Eyes**:
Forward-facing predatory mechanical eyes (the bird is a hunter—eyes set forward like a raptor, NOT side-set like a chicken/sparrow). **Glowing cyan inner light #4FC3D9** matching the Qingtong Sphere companion and mechanical Saiga (shared ancient lost-technology origin marker). Bronze rim around each eye with thin glass-like lens. Cold predatory gaze.

**Neck**:
Long muscular mechanical neck with multiple bronze segment plates allowing flexible curving—neck can extend forward when running for sprint efficiency or arch up alert. Visible mechanical vertebrae structure beneath panel plates.

**Wings — vestigial but visible**:
Wings are **not capable of flight** but **NOT removed**—they are **vestigial mechanical wings** folded against the body sides, with bronze panel "feathers" suggesting wing structure. Wings can extend partially in **threat-display posture** for intimidation. In the SIDE/FRONT/TOP panels: wings folded against body. In the **3/4 VIEW**: one wing partially extended in threat display, showing the bronze feather-panel structure spreading. NOT giant flight wings, NOT fully removed stubs—about 2/3 the size of functional flight wings, ornamental-defensive only.

**Legs and feet**:
Two long mechanical sprint-legs—proportionally LONG (built for fast running, NOT short like a chicken). Each leg shows visible bronze joint pivots at hip, knee, and ankle, with hydraulic-style sprint pistons running along the bone-armature. **Feet: three-toed predatory raptor feet** with two forward toes and one rear toe, ending in **large curved bronze talon-claws** designed for slashing and gripping prey. Talons show wear and pitting from sand and combat.

**Tail (CRITICAL — long mechanical sprint-balance tail)**:
A long mechanical tail extending behind the body, approximately 80cm-1m long, made of segmented bronze plates ending in a tuft of longer thin bronze tail-feather-panels:
- **Functional purpose**: sprint balance (like roadrunner/raptor) and high-speed cornering
- **Construction**: 8-12 bronze segmented vertebrae plates with flexible joints between them, allowing the tail to whip side-to-side for balance
- **Bonus feature**: small concealed bronze blade-edges along the tail underside—the tail can be used as a slashing weapon when whipped sideways
- Tail held nearly horizontal in running stance, slightly raised at rest

**Cockpit/合体接口 (CRITICAL — visible in TOP and 3/4 panels)**:
On the **dorsal surface (back)** between the wing roots, a clearly visible **mechanical cockpit hatch / pilot integration interface**—a roughly human-torso-sized rectangular-oval seam with bronze locking clasps around its perimeter, visible hinges along one edge, and small step-rungs along the side flanks for mounting access. This is where the elite raider partner climbs in and integrates with the war-beast. The hatch is currently CLOSED in this character sheet (showing the design but in dormant/standalone configuration). Same design language as the mechanical Saiga's cockpit—part of a unified war-mount system.

**Tribal trophy decorations and partner-binding tokens**:
Moderate quantity of trophy decorations:
- Leather-strap harness around the neck/upper-chest with woven beads, polished bone fragments
- A small leather pouch tied to the saddle-area near the cockpit hatch (containing the partner-raider's personal token—a small carved bone amulet visible peeking out)
- 3-5 small trophies (defeated enemies' equipment fragments) tied via leather cords along the body sides
- Tribal pattern dyed-cloth banding around the upper legs and around the base of the beak
- Small feather-style ornaments tied to the wing tips (real feathers from defeated enemy raiders' headdresses—a kill-trophy) (the only biological elements on the entire creature, and they are TROPHIES not the creature's own feathers)
- Mecha Crimson Bronze + Aged Gold + dried-blood-red color accents

**Stance**:
Default standing alert posture in SIDE/FRONT/TOP views: standing on both legs, neck slightly arched up, beak forward, wings folded, tail held horizontal-back. In 3/4 VIEW: dynamic stance with one leg slightly raised mid-stride, one wing partially extended in threat display, head turned slightly toward camera—the "hero shot."

**Weathering**:
War-torn weathered—heavy verdigris patina in deep recesses, sand-pitting on bronze surfaces, small dents from past combat, mismatched repair patches in slightly different bronze tones. NOT clean, NOT polished, NOT factory-new.

【4 Panels detail】

**Top-left panel — SIDE VIEW**: Pure broadside profile view. Beast facing camera-right (head/beak to the right). Full body visible beak-tip to tail-tip. Long predatory beak prominently visible. Long horizontal tail trailing behind. Lean horizontal sprint silhouette readable. Cockpit hatch on the back visible from this angle. Side-rim backlight catches the upper neck, beak, and tail tip.

**Top-right panel — FRONT VIEW**: Beast head facing camera directly. **The long predatory beak is the dominant feature in this panel**—centered, pointing forward at camera. Forward-facing cyan eyes glowing on either side of beak base. Body visible behind the head with vestigial wings folded at sides. Long legs visible flanking the body, raptor talons on the ground.

**Bottom-left panel — TOP-DOWN VIEW**: God's-eye view from directly overhead looking down at the beast. Shows the full footprint and dorsal mechanical layout. **The long horizontal tail trails behind in a clear line**, **the beak points forward**, **the wings folded against body sides**. **The cockpit hatch on the back between the wing roots is most clearly visible in this view**—the rectangular-oval seam with locking clasps. Tribal pattern bronze panels visible in their full layout.

**Bottom-right panel — 3/4 PERSPECTIVE VIEW**: Three-quarter angle showing depth, approximately 30-45 degrees from front-side, slightly elevated viewpoint. **One wing partially extended in threat display**—showing the bronze feather-panel structure spreading. Beak prominent. Long tail trailing. One leg slightly raised mid-stride. Cyan eye glow catches the rim light. The "hero shot" of the four views—aggressive predator stance.

【Critical consistency】
SAME individual creature in all 4 panels—identical body proportions (lean sprinter build), identical beak shape (spear with terminal hook), identical wing size (vestigial 2/3 size), identical tail length and segment count, identical cockpit hatch position and design, identical trophy decoration arrangement, identical bronze panel pattern, identical weathering pattern. Same studio backdrop, same single-sided side-rim backlighting setup across all 4 panels.

【Lighting - critical】
**Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45-degrees behind subject. NO fill light. NO front light. NO ambient. NO other light sources.** Strong dramatic edge-light catches the right side of the creature silhouette in every panel. Cyan eye glow self-illuminates faintly on bronze panels adjacent to the eyes.

【Style】
Hyperrealistic photographic creature design rendering—shot like a high-end practical animatronic creature photographed in studio for a film production design board. NOT illustration, NOT obvious 3D CGI render.

**Reference aesthetics**:
- **Phorusrhacidae (terror bird) paleoart and skeleton reconstructions** (for predatory ground-bird anatomy and beak)
- **Roadrunner and secretary-bird wildlife photography** (for lean fast sprinter silhouette and horizontal posture)
- **Velociraptor / Deinonychus dromaeosaurid paleoart** (for the predatory bipedal hunter aesthetic with long tail and raptor stance)
- **Studio Ghibli Princess Mononoke spirit-beast aesthetic** (for ancient mythical-mechanical creature feel)
- **Ancient bronze Chinese ceremonial vessels and Han dynasty tomb mechanical figures** (古代青铜机关——for lost-tech bronze patina aesthetic)
- **Pacific Rim mecha + Horizon Zero Dawn mechanical fauna** (for war-torn maintained-by-primitive-hands mechanical-creature feel)

Light Kodak Vision3 35mm film grain texture overlay.

【Quality】
Maximum resolution output. 8k ultra clean rendering. Sharp clean edges. Every bronze feather-panel, every gear tooth on visible joints, every talon edge, every tail segment, every leather strap on the trophy decorations clearly defined. Real practical-creature animatronic photography aesthetic.

【Panel Labels】
Small clean monospace sans-serif black text below each panel:
- Top-left: "SIDE"
- Top-right: "FRONT"
- Bottom-left: "TOP"
- Bottom-right: "3/4 VIEW"

Title at top center of canvas (small clean black sans-serif): "MECHANICAL RUNNER-BIRD — WAR-MOUNT DESIGN SHEET"

【Constraints】

**Species type accuracy (high failure risk)**:
NOT a giant ostrich (NOT bulky NOT plump—lean sprinter only), NOT a chicken (NOT short-legged NOT round-bodied), NOT an eagle or vulture (this is a flightless ground predator), NOT a peacock (NOT decorative tail feathers), NOT a generic fantasy bird. MUST read as a flightless predatory sprint-bird = terror bird × roadrunner × dromaeosaur silhouette. Lean horizontal posture, long sprint-legs, predatory forward-facing eyes, spear beak with terminal hook, long horizontal tail.

**Mechanical purity**:
Fully mechanical construction with NO biological tissue—NOT a cyborg, NOT biological-with-armor, NOT feather-and-flesh-with-mechanical-parts. ALL surfaces must be mechanical bronze-and-metal panels and components in feather-imitation arrangements. NO real feathers (except the small TROPHY feathers tied to wing tips as kill-decorations—these are explicitly trophies from defeated enemies, NOT the creature's own feathers). NO real skin, NO real eyes (mechanical eyes with cyan glow only).

**Beak specifics**:
Long spear-shape with subtle terminal hook. NOT short. NOT wide flat duckbill. NOT fully curved hooked vulture beak. Approximately 30-40cm long. Sharp piercing point.

**Wing specifics**:
Vestigial size only (2/3 functional flight-wing scale). NOT capable of flight. Folded against body in 3 panels, partially extended in 3/4 view for threat display. NOT removed entirely (must be present even if vestigial).

**Tail specifics**:
Long mechanical horizontal sprint-balance tail (80cm-1m). NOT a short stub tail. NOT a peacock-fan upright tail. Segmented bronze plates. Held nearly horizontal in stance.

**Aesthetic anchoring**:
NOT precision modern robotics, NOT clean factory-new metal (war-torn weathered patina only), NOT shiny chrome (aged bronze with verdigris only), NOT steampunk Victorian (this is ancient lost-tech NOT industrial-revolution), NOT cyberpunk neon, NOT obvious CGI 3D render, NOT cartoon, NOT anime, NOT cute mascot bird aesthetic, NOT fantasy phoenix elegance.

**Cockpit hatch presence**:
The dorsal cockpit hatch between wing roots MUST be visible in TOP-DOWN and 3/4 panels. NOT a fully sealed creature.

**Layout and lighting**:
NOT 1:1 square aspect (must be horizontal 16:9), NOT 9:16 vertical, NOT colorful background (solid neutral light-warm-grey only), NOT soft even neutral lighting (must be single-side rim backlight only), NOT front light, NOT fill light, NOT colorful saturated palette (muted desert bronze tones only), NOT any text other than the four panel labels and the title.

【实操注意】

1. 跑出来重点检查（按风险优先级）：
   - **lean predatory sprinter silhouette（MAX风险）**——容易跑成胖鸵鸟/鸡/鹰。必须是水平精瘦+长腿+长尾+像Velociraptor但是鸟版
   - **spear beak with terminal hook**——容易跑成普通鸟喙或纯弯钩鹰嘴。必须是长尖矛+末端微钩
   - **vestigial wings但保留**——容易跑成完全退化（鸵鸟翅膀）或完全flight wings。必须是2/3 size+折叠+3/4 view展开威慑
   - **long horizontal sprint-balance tail**——容易跑成短鸡尾或孔雀立尾。必须是长水平+segmented+用于平衡
   - **forward-facing predatory eyes**——容易跑成侧视鸟眼。必须前视像猛禽
   - **纯机械（无生物羽毛）**——羽毛形装甲是bronze panel不是real feathers（trophies feather除外）
   - **背部cockpit hatch**——和赛加同DNA的合体接口
   - **同沙猎部落青铜DNA**+青色眼发光
   - **16:9横版+4格均等**

2. 跑歪兜底加强：
   - 跑成胖鸵鸟 → "lean predatory sprint-bird, NOT bulky NOT plump, hybrid of Phorusrhacidae terror bird and roadrunner sprinter, lean horizontal posture, long sprint-legs"
   - 喙跑成普通鸟嘴或纯弯钩 → "long spear-shaped beak 30-40cm with subtle hook only at the very tip, primarily a piercing weapon NOT a tearing weapon"
   - 翅膀跑全没了或全flight wings → "vestigial mechanical wings approximately 2/3 functional size, NOT removed entirely, NOT fully functional flight wings, can extend in threat display"
   - 尾巴跑短/立起 → "long horizontal mechanical tail 80cm-1m segmented bronze plates, held nearly horizontal in stance for sprint balance, NOT short stub, NOT upright peacock fan"
   - 跑成生物羽毛 → "all 'feathers' are bronze metal panel armor in feather-shape arrangements, NOT real biological feathers"
   - 跑成鸡或鹰 → "this is a flightless ground predator hybrid, terror bird × roadrunner × dromaeosaur, lean horizontal sprinter silhouette"

3. **关于"兽形态"vs"合体外骨骼形态"**：
   - 这张prompt是兽独立形态——cockpit hatch CLOSED+在dormant/standalone configuration
   - 合体外骨骼形态（沙匪钻进去之后）后续单独做+基于这张兽形态做参考

4. **2-3次Iteration预期**：
   - 这只兽因为是杂交silhouette+机械化+带cockpit接口——AI模型可能需要2-3次iteration才稳定
   - 优先级修正：先确保lean sprinter silhouette + 喙形对+ 长尾对+ 翅膀位置对，其他细节后续微调


### 第8条｜左上角和右上角的图片应当可见黑红色外套的衣领

修正图片。左上角和右上角的图片应当可见黑红色外套的衣领。黑色丝袜应当有些许破损。裙子被微风掀起，原本被遮挡的那条腿可见并没有穿丝袜（也就是一条腿穿丝袜，一条腿没穿。）


### 第9条｜是刘回，声音是 ， 是副手，声音是

@图1 是刘回，声音是@音频1 ，@图2 是副手，声音是@音频2 。两个人在距离镜头很远的地方说话。全场以白噪音作为bgm。副手：“不自量力的东西，真搞不懂这种人为什么会有邀请函。”刘回：“看他这扮相，怕是胡家的余孽，家大业大，富得流油。”

## 二、人设四视图、载具武器、故事板、色卡与海报


### 第13条｜万物生 - 关羽机甲多角度细节图

**万物生 - 关羽机甲多角度细节图**
**工具：GPT-Image-2 image2image**
**Reference：关羽机甲主图（25米巨型机甲完整版）**
**用途：项目核心视觉资产+穿越段手部素材+长期项目素材库**
**输出：1张16:9合成图，含6个视角分镜**
【Change】
Transform the reference image (the full-body Guan Yu mecha hero shot) into a multi-angle character reference sheet showing 6 distinct views of the same mecha character. The output is a single 16:9 widescreen canvas divided into 6 panels arranged in a 3x2 grid layout. Each panel shows a different view of the mecha against a clean neutral warm-toned studio background.

The 6 panels are:

PANEL 1 (top-left, full-body front view): The complete Guan Yu mecha shown in full body straight-on front view, standing upright with neutral pose, both arms hanging naturally at sides, the curved blade weapon held in the right hand resting tip-down on the ground beside the right foot. Head tilted slightly downward in characteristic temple-guardian gaze. Show the entire mecha from head to feet, occupying the full vertical space of this panel.

PANEL 2 (top-middle, full-body back view): The complete Guan Yu mecha shown in full body straight-on back view, standing upright with neutral pose, both arms hanging at sides. The two large articulated heat exhaust panels extending from the back like a flowing cape are clearly visible and prominent. Show the back-side ornamentation density - dragons, clouds, ancient script engravings on the back armor plates. Show the entire mecha from head to feet.

PANEL 3 (top-right, head close-up front view): A tight close-up of the mecha's head and upper shoulders ONLY, showing the face plate in detail. Clearly show: the smooth featureless mechanical face plate with the single horizontal optical sensor slit emitting dim red glow; the tall pointed conical sensor mast on top reading as helmet spike; the two horizontal cooling fins on the head sides reading as helmet ear flaps; the vertical bundle of long thin metal cables hanging from the lower mask plate reading as a stylized mechanical beard; the engraved patterns radiating from the central spike. The head is at center frame, filling most of this panel's vertical space.

PANEL 4 (bottom-left, right hand close-up extended forward): An extreme close-up of the mecha's RIGHT mechanical hand, the hand is extended forward toward the camera in a NATURAL OPEN STATE - all five mechanical fingers fully extended and spread naturally, palm slightly visible facing the camera, NOT a fist, NOT a grip, NOT closed, the hand is in a relaxed open position as if reaching forward or offering. Show clearly: the long articulated mechanical fingers, the palm articulation, the visible hydraulic pistons and cable bundles at the wrist and finger joints, the tarnished bronze and aged gold accent details at the joint sockets, the antique crimson red and dark jade green matte paint with weathering and chipping. The hand fills the panel and is the clear focal point. The wrist and a small portion of forearm are visible at the edge of the panel for scale context. NO weapon held in this hand in this panel.

PANEL 5 (bottom-middle, weapon close-up): A close-up of the curved blade polearm weapon (mechanical Guan Dao / Green Dragon Crescent Blade), shown floating against the neutral background or held by a partial mechanical hand at the bottom edge of the panel. Show the weapon clearly: the long pole shaft with engraved Chinese dragon patterns; the curved crescent blade with industrial mechanical detail; ancient script etched into the blade surface; tarnished bronze and aged gold accents along the pole. The weapon is angled diagonally across this panel for dynamic composition.

PANEL 6 (bottom-right, foot and lower leg close-up): A close-up showing one of the mecha's lower legs and foot, from approximately mid-thigh down to the ground. Show clearly: the sectioned mechanical skirt of overlapping armor lamellae plates partially visible at top of panel; the sculpted thigh armor with engraved cloud patterns; the mechanical knee joint with visible hydraulics; the calf armor with mechanical detailing; the large mechanical foot. Show the deeply lowered planted stable stance the leg is in. Emphasize the engraved ornamentation density on the leg armor.

LAYOUT AND COMPOSITION RULES:
- The 6 panels are arranged in a clean 3x2 grid (3 columns, 2 rows)
- Each panel is approximately equal in size, separated by thin clean white/cream borders
- Above each panel, a small label in clean monospace black sans-serif text identifies the view: "FRONT", "BACK", "HEAD DETAIL", "RIGHT HAND OPEN", "WEAPON", "LEG DETAIL"
- Title text at the top center of the entire canvas: "GUAN YU MECHA - CHARACTER REFERENCE SHEET" in clean sans-serif black text
- Background of all panels is a uniform neutral warm-toned studio backdrop, slightly desaturated, subtle warm-tan vignette, NO environmental scenery, NO sacred ground elements (NO banners, NO steles, NO altar - this is a clean character reference, not a scene)
- Lighting on all 6 panels is consistent: soft directional warm key light from upper-left at approximately 30 degrees, gentle fill from right, rim lighting picking up edges of mecha forms

【Preserve】
THE GUAN YU MECHA IDENTITY MUST REMAIN 100% IDENTICAL to the reference image across all 6 panels:

- Exact same overall silhouette: extremely tall and slender humanoid (translates to whatever framing in each panel)
- Exact same proportions: small head, narrow shoulders, slim torso, dramatically narrow waist, long arms, oversized mechanical hands, very long thin legs
- Exact same face plate design: smooth featureless mechanical mask with single horizontal optical sensor slit emitting dim red glow, NO human features
- Exact same head ornamentation: tall pointed conical sensor mast on top, two horizontal cooling fins on sides, vertical bundle of long thin metal cables hanging from lower mask plate as stylized mechanical beard
- Exact same body construction: bio-mechanical aesthetic, segmented chest plates resembling exposed ribs and sternum, sculpted shoulder pieces with sharp angular edges, mechanical arms with exposed cable bundles, articulated waist with visible mechanical spine column, sculpted thigh and calf armor with clear mechanical jointing
- Exact same chest engraving: massive central dragon engraving on chest plate
- Exact same skirt: sectioned mechanical skirt of overlapping armor lamellae plates around narrow waist
- Exact same back panels: two large articulated heat exhaust panels extending from back like flowing cape
- Exact same ornamentation density: dense intricate ornamentation across all body surfaces, dragons clouds flames and ancient script engraved on every armor plate, like a Tibetan ritual artifact or Ming dynasty imperial object
- Exact same weapon design: long-handled curved blade polearm with engraved dragon patterns and ancient script
- Exact same paint and color palette: thick weathered military matte paint, dominant deep antique crimson red on chest pauldrons and helmet, secondary deep faded jade green on lower body and skirt sections, accent details in tarnished bronze and aged gold at engravings rivets and joint sockets, all paint matte non-reflective with no shine, decades of weathering causes paint chipping revealing dull bare grey metal underneath at edges
- Exact same color grade: warm sun-bleached tonality, vivid antique red and rich jade green clearly readable, tonal contrast compressed and gentle, heavy 35mm film grain texture
- Exact same Wong Kar-wai aesthetic: warm rich and saturated colors NOT muted grey, like a frame from In the Mood for Love or Ashes of Time
- Exact same overall impression: ancient war god temple guardian deity at absolute rest, weight and stillness and dread, sacred and inevitable

EVERY visual DNA element from the reference must be preserved across all 6 panels.

【Constraints】
NOT a battle scene, NOT environmental setting (no sacred ground, no banners, no steles, no altar - clean studio reference sheet ONLY).
NOT illustrated or cartoonish style - maintain the photorealistic film-grain aesthetic from reference.
NOT 3D rendering look, NOT anime style, NOT video game character sheet style.
NOT a different mecha design - the mecha identity must be 100% recognizable as the same character from reference.
NOT changing the proportions, colors, ornamentation, or design language between panels.
NOT muted grey palette - the warm saturated antique red and jade green must remain vivid as in reference.
NOT panels showing different mechas - all 6 panels show the SAME single character from different angles.
NOT empty or sparse panels - each panel must be clearly composed with the mecha element prominently visible.
NOT motion blur, NOT action poses except where specified (Panel 4 hand extended is NOT action, it is a clean reference pose).
NOT a fist or closed hand in Panel 4 - the right hand MUST be in fully open natural extended state.
NOT environmental scenery in any panel background - clean uniform neutral warm studio backdrop only.

Aspect ratio: 16:9 landscape cinematic widescreen.


### 第14条｜ultra wide super-extreme long shot + c

王家卫色调 Wong Kar-wai cinematic look + Christopher Doyle cinematography + 库布里克《Paths of Glory》战后空镜美学。荒漠美学《英雄》《卧虎藏龙》。35mm anamorphic widescreen + heavy 35mm Kodak Vision3 film grain + shallow DoF + low saturation dusk。
@图1 作为整段画面色板锚定+所有色彩严格按色卡10色HEX执行+不允许偏色。
 
【镜头数量】
整段画面通过2个镜头硬切hard cut构成+第二个镜头末尾约2秒做遮罩衔接为下一段留无缝接口。
 
【镜头1：大漠夕阳+残兵纵队行进（约5秒）】
 
ultra wide super-extreme long shot + camera very far back maximum vast scale + 摄影机固定不动 + 摄影机水平视角略低于地平线 + 大漠和天空各占画面一半。
 
画面构成：大漠沙脊从画面左下延伸到右上方。夕阳从画面右后方斜射逆光。
 
残兵纵队tiny silhouettes far in the distance + 沿沙脊从画面右后远端向画面左前方缓慢移动 + 队列散乱 + 渺小如蚂蚁stretching across dune ridge。约10个残兵剪影+夹杂2-3匹损坏的青铜机械马剪影。所有剪影only tiny black silhouettes + 无具体细节 + 漆黑无光#1A1610。
 
【镜头2：队列里一个士兵的特写——行进中+末尾遮罩衔接（约7秒）】
 
硬切到镜头1队伍里其中一个士兵+medium shot side tracking + 摄影机在士兵右侧+水平视角等高+slow horizontal tracking向画面左侧+速度略慢于士兵+士兵在画面前景偏右。
 
取景：腰部以上+画面下边缘在士兵腰部+头顶距画面上沿约1/8。
 
人物动作：士兵行走中+上半身随步伐微起伏+肩膀一上一下+左臂自然摆动+视线游离远方不聚焦+表情木然+嘴微张+疲惫呼吸。
 
服装：破损明光铠（隋唐主流甲胄）+胸甲扭曲变形+表面锈铜青绿#7A6845+干涸血污#5A2820。没戴铁兜鍪+头发凌乱黏汗。
 
义肢细节：右臂青铜机械义肢（徐克《狄仁杰》古代机关+战锤40k Tech Priest被毁感）+主色锈铜青绿#7A6845+金属翘起卷边+内部冒出白烟#B8B0A0。义肢侧面贴烧焦道家符箓+焦黄#6E5230基色+焦黑卷曲#2A1F18边缘+字符不可辨（《Doctor Strange》古代符号烧焦感）。
 
背景空间承接：士兵是镜头1纵队里的一员+不是孤立特写。
 
画面左侧中景：另一个残兵的部分轮廓（肩膀+头）+在士兵前方+同方向行进+部分模糊。
 
画面后方深处一匹损坏的青铜机械马——关键视觉奇观——体型类似真马+主色锈铜青绿#7A6845（和士兵义肢同色系+暗示同族失落科技）+一条前腿弯曲不正常瘸着+步态僵硬+头部下垂+耳朵塌着+部分外壳脱落露出内部古代齿轮和机关+表面焦痕和血污#5A2820。视觉参考宫崎骏《风之谷》巨神兵+《もののけ姫》Tatara工坊机械感。机械马在士兵和另一个残兵之间偏后+shallow DoF部分模糊+能看到马首+马腹+破损腿+齿轮。
 
光线：王家卫式低饱和暮色侧光chiaroscuro side lighting+夕阳从画面右侧斜射+士兵脸右侧有暮色光+左侧陷阴影。
 
**镜头2末尾约2秒——遮罩衔接段**：背景的青铜机械马加速从画面后方走向前景+身体侧面（马腹+马腿）从画面右侧切入前景+遮挡镜头视野+填满画面。**关键：机械马背对夕阳+逆光backlit pure black silhouette+所有细节不可见+无金属反光+无齿轮可见+主色漆黑无光#1A1610+画面接近全黑near total darkness+只在画面边缘残留一丝暮色赭金#C49B5C漏光。**整段在纯黑剪影填满画面状态下结束。
 
【声音设计】
镜头1：大漠风声low frequency drone+远方脚步沙沙sand crunching+金属铠甲吱嘎creaking armor+机械马步伐金属碰撞metallic clinking distant+一声Tibetan dungchen long horn drone solo从远方响起。
 
镜头2前段：风声+士兵疲惫呼吸+脚步沙地摩擦+机械马金属关节摩擦+齿轮卡顿+Tibetan dungchen drone延续。
 
镜头2末尾遮罩段：随着机械马靠近+其金属关节摩擦声+齿轮卡顿声+步伐金属碰撞声逐渐放大+占据声场主导+其他环境声逐渐被遮挡压制+ Tibetan dungchen drone继续延续低频。
 
【关键约束】
- 镜头2承接镜头1空间——士兵是纵队里的一员+在行进中+背景必有其他残兵和机械马
- 镜头2末尾必须做遮罩衔接——青铜机械马走到前景遮挡镜头+**机械马身体必须是逆光backlit pure black silhouette+所有细节不可见+画面接近全黑near total darkness**+给下一段留无缝接口
- 残兵剪影只是黑色剪影轮廓+不可见具体面孔+不可见血腥细节
- 残兵特写取景腰部以上+不描述下半身（画面外属性铁律）
- 大漠夕阳逆光为主光+所有光线都是暮色侧光或逆光+NOT正面平光+NOT顶光
- 镜头1摄影机固定+NOT handheld+NOT dolly+NOT pan+NOT zoom
- 镜头2摄影机slow horizontal tracking向画面左侧+NOT handheld+NOT dolly in/out+NOT zoom
- 青铜机械马必须看起来损坏+NOT functional+NOT pristine+NOT shiny
- NOT bloody, NOT graphic gore, NOT corpses visible
- NOT modern technology visible (except broken bronze prosthetic and broken bronze mechanical horse)
- NOT happy, NOT bright daylight, NOT noon, NOT cheerful
 
【通用约束】
真人电影实拍质感 cinematic realistic film。NOT cartoon, NOT illustrated, NOT 3D render, NOT CGI, NOT anime。NOT bright kelly green, NOT cheerful saturated colors。仅生成动作音效+环境音效+only Tibetan dungchen drone音乐+无人声no voice-over。no subtitles, no watermark。


### 第15条｜整段画面是1个连续镜头single continuous tracking

王家卫色调 Wong Kar-wai cinematic look + Christopher Doyle cinematography + 《琅琊榜》《大明宫词》皇宫夜戏调性。35mm anamorphic widescreen + heavy 35mm Kodak Vision3 film grain + shallow DoF + low saturation night palette。
@图1 作为整段画面色板锚定+所有色彩严格按色卡10色HEX执行+不允许偏色。
 
【镜头数量】
整段画面是1个连续镜头single continuous tracking shot+全程slow horizontal tracking向画面左侧+无切镜+无pan+无zoom+无dolly in/out。
 
【镜头描述】
摄影机贴近地面+camera positioned about 0.5-1 meter above the floor+slight high angle略微俯拍angle 15-25 degrees+slow horizontal tracking continuously向画面左侧+匀速slow steady pace。
 
**起始约1秒**：纯黑色宫廷立柱完全遮挡镜头+pillar in extreme foreground filling the entire frame+near total darkness+只有柱子表面微弱的木质纹理（漆黑#1A1610+紫微宫朱红#6E2820边缘极弱漏光）。
 
**约1-2.5秒**：前景的宫廷立柱逐渐从画面右侧出框slides out of frame to the right+逐渐露出寝宫内景。寝宫内景：东都洛阳紫微宫某寝殿内部+夜间+昏暗烛光candle warm amber #C9763A从画面左侧远处flickering+寝宫主调深殿绛紫#2A1828+两侧依稀可见紫微宫朱红#6E2820漆色立柱+丝帷淡赭#6E5A40帷幕悬挂在画面深处+地面是反光的深色石板reflective dark stone floor。
 
地面前景：一件隋唐赭黄#A56E2A的空黄袍散落在地面+robe is empty no one inside+龙袍上的暗金#846035龙纹刺绣依稀可见+袍子扭曲褶皱+像被随手脱下扔在地上的状态。
 
**约2.5-5.5秒**：摄影机继续slow tracking向画面左侧+揭示一段巨型蛇怪躯干侧腹紧贴地面lying flat on stone floor+长长一条横向贯穿画面+从画面右侧延伸到画面左侧+**躯干直径如成年男性身高diameter as tall as a man standing**+**比身边紫微宫朱红立柱粗两倍twice as thick as the palace pillar**+躯干巨大占画面纵向至少二分之一+底部贴地+绝不悬空NOT floating。鳞片暗绿#1F2A1F+边缘冷青#2A3838+缓慢起伏暗示活物+地面有接触阴影。**关键**：只看下腹部belly section only+NOT visible head/tail/claws/whiskers/horns+贴地。
 
**约5.5-7秒**：另一根宫廷立柱从画面左侧逐渐切入画面slowly slides into frame from the left edge+逐渐占据更大画面比例+最终完全遮挡镜头视野+画面再次接近全黑near total darkness+pillar表面紫微宫朱红#6E2820+漆黑#1A1610阴影主导。整段在立柱遮挡near total darkness状态结束。
 
【声音设计】
全程：Tibetan dungchen drone延续（slow majestic ancient solo drone）。
起始遮罩段：木质共振wood resonance+远处烛光flickering+远处夜风。
揭示寝宫段：烛光flickering+远处宫殿空旷回响hollow palace echo。
揭示龙身段：deep guttural beast growl野兽愤怒低吟+low frequency rumble+鳞片摩擦的轻微金属声subtle scale metallic friction。
末尾：dragon growl逐渐被柱子遮挡声压制muffled+声音变闷。
 
【通用约束】
真人电影实拍质感 cinematic realistic film。NOT cartoon, NOT illustrated, NOT 3D render, NOT CGI, NOT anime。NOT bright saturated colors+NOT cheerful。仅生成动作音效+环境音效+only Tibetan dungchen drone音乐+无人声no voice-over。no subtitles, no watermark。


### 第16条｜全程使用白噪音作为bgm

全程使用白噪音作为bgm。写实电影。禁止3d动画。【总调性】
王家卫色调 Wong Kar-wai cinematic look + Christopher Doyle cinematography + 故宫航拍纪录片真实质感。35mm anamorphic widescreen + heavy 35mm Kodak Vision3 film grain + dawn warm palette黎明暖色调。
 
【素材引用】
@图1 作为整段画面色板锚定+所有色彩严格按色卡12色HEX执行+不允许偏色。
 
【镜头数量】
整段画面是1个连续镜头single continuous shot+全程vertical dolly up持续向上+追踪海东青+加速超越+最终俯瞰城邦+白云遮罩结尾+无切镜无cut+无pan+无变焦zoom。
 
【镜头描述】
 
摄影机贴近紫微宫屋顶琉璃瓦表面+near total darkness渐显+屋顶琉璃瓦近景遮罩filling the frame+屋顶深褐绿琉璃#3F4A38+暗紫黑#2A1F2A阴影主导+只有瓦片纹理隐约可见。摄影机开始vertical dolly up向上拉升+加速。
 
摄影机继续向上拉升+揭示瓦片边缘+紫微宫屋顶轮廓+黎明天空在画面上方（黎明暖橘#D4884A+粉霞#E8A878晨曦glow）。一只海东青Haidongqing falcon（Sui-Tang imperial royal hunting falcon）从屋顶上方飞过+嘴里衔着一张古代邀请函。
摄影机上升并转向，同时向下俯瞰，跟随海东青camera angle gradually tilts down并模仿航拍视角持续跟随下方飞行的海东青向前快速移动。背景天空从画面侧面（黎明暖橘+粉霞渐变）转到画面深处。
摄影机已在海东青上方+视角完全俯瞰downward bird's-eye view+海东青在画面下方（变小+但仍清晰可见+继续向前飞）。**视觉奇观**：紫微宫城邦在画面更深处展开——隋唐皇宫建筑群Sui-Tang palace architecture complex+紫微宫朱红#6E2820宫墙红柱+城邦深棕#6E5840民居屋顶+屋顶深褐绿琉璃#3F4A38主殿琉璃瓦屋顶+整个城邦在黎明金色光线中铺开+视觉参考故宫航拍纪录片
**约12-14秒**：摄影机持续向前推进+一团白云从画面深处迅速冲向镜头+a bank of pale clouds rapidly approaches the camera from depth+白云越来越大+最终填满画面+near total whiteness+远天淡云白#E8E8DC填满整个画面。整段在白云填满画面接近全白的状态下结束。
 
【声音设计】
全程：BGM v5万国来朝版（多元素堆叠层）持续延续+Hans Zimmer风hybrid epic ceremonial。

【关键约束】

### 第17条｜Subject】

A single cinematic still frame. 16:9 widescreen aspect ratio.
 
【Subject】
Bird's-eye top-down aerial view of an extraordinary ancient Chinese mechanical train rushing through a vast desert landscape. The train is composed of 15-20 connected carriages stretching in a single long winding line from the bottom of the frame all the way back to the distant vanishing point on the horizon, creating dramatic perspective convergence — foreground carriages near the bottom of the frame appear large and detailed, while distant carriages diminish progressively to tiny shapes vanishing into the haze.
 
**Critical detail — the train has NO rails and NO wheels**. Instead, each carriage has many ancient bronze mechanical legs underneath (centipede-like legs), approximately 12-16 legs per carriage. The mechanical legs are made of ancient bronze (#7A6845 main color) with dark gold (#846035) joint accents — they alternate asynchronously (not in unison), each leg lifting and falling at different moments creating the illusion of a giant mechanical centipede sprinting forward. The legs kick up clouds of golden sand dust that trail behind the train, hanging in the air.
 
Train carriage tops: deep brown wood (#5A4028) structures with ancient Eastern decorative roofing — slight curved roof tiles, wooden beam construction, occasional bronze fittings catching warm light.
 
The vast desert landscape surrounding the train: undulating sand dunes in earth orange (#8E5A2A) primary color, dune ridges highlighted in ochre gold (#C49B5C) catching late afternoon sunlight, distant horizon line in cool dusk blue (#3A4258), vast empty space dominating the surrounding negative area, occasional rocky outcrops scattered across the desert. The train cuts a striking diagonal/curved line through the empty landscape.
 
【Composition】
**Top-down bird's-eye view from high altitude**, camera positioned far above the train looking straight down. Strong perspective convergence with the train extending from foreground (largest, most detailed carriages and their mechanical legs visible at the bottom of the frame) to vanishing point on horizon (tiny carriages disappearing into atmospheric haze). The train forms a dramatic diagonal or gentle S-curve through the desert. Empty desert dominates the surrounding negative space — approximately 70% desert / 30% train.
 
【Lighting】
Late afternoon / golden hour directional lighting from a low sun (sun positioned outside frame, casting long sharp shadows of the mechanical legs and carriages on the desert sand). Low saturation cinematic palette. Warm desert glow with cool dusk shadows. Atmospheric haze toward the horizon for depth.
 
【Style】
Cinematic still frame in the style of Wong Kar-wai + Christopher Doyle cinematography. 35mm anamorphic widescreen with heavy 35mm Kodak Vision3 film grain texture. Low saturation dusk warm color palette. Epic widescreen aerial cinematography.
 
Visual references: Mad Max Fury Road desert convoy aerial shots, Snowpiercer aerial train shots, Princess Mononoke Tatara workshop mechanical creatures, Boston Dynamics quadruped robots aerial photography, Hayao Miyazaki Nausicaä Giant God Soldier ancient mechanical aesthetic, Dune (2021) sand worm desert vastness aesthetic.
 
【Quality】
Hyperrealistic photographic quality, 8k cinematic still frame, deep depth of field with the train sharply rendered from foreground all the way to vanishing point, real film camera aerial capture aesthetic, dramatic golden hour lighting, professional aerial cinematography composition.
 
【Constraints】
NOT modern train, NOT bullet train, NOT steam engine, NOT railway tracks, NOT wheels, NOT futuristic, NOT cyberpunk, NOT cartoon, NOT illustrated, NOT 3D render obvious, NOT painterly, NOT abstract.

【镜头描述】
 
**画面起手**：完全遮挡镜头的深蓝色纯色块（深蓝#1F2840底+art deco金色几何纹路#C9A050+古代封印字符）完全遮挡视野+near total darkness。摄影机贴地仰拍extreme low angle from ground looking upward。一双戴着青铜机械装甲手套的手（手套主色锈铜青绿#7A6845+暗金#846035关节）把邀请函从镜头前缓慢拿开。
 
**接着**：揭示一个一体改造的隋朝镖人——本来蹲着+现在缓慢站起身。镖人造型：30-40岁男性+脸上一道刀疤+左眼是青铜机械瞳孔（古代望远装置+#7A6845主色+#846035金属边缘）+右臂从肩部以下完全机械化（金属装甲+齿轮关节+#7A6845主色+#846035铜质装饰）+腰间挂着一枚古代机械徽章+佩有一柄改造过的弯刀（刀柄是机械结构）+穿有灰尘的隋朝深色护卫古装+衣黑#2A2A2A外袍+内衣灰#4A5258+腰带+古代发饰束发。视觉参考：战锤40k Tech Priest（功能正常版）+徐克《狄仁杰》古代机关+《长安十二时辰》护卫。
 
**然后**：镖人后退几步steps backward casually+饶有兴致地把玩邀请函examining the card with curious interest+邀请函在他的金属手套手指间翻动+面部带着审视+轻微微笑。
 他后退的过程中，镜头固定不动，揭示火车内部，东方版本霍格沃茨列车风格Eastern Hogwarts-style train carriage+车厢内部木质结构+车厢深棕#5A4028+古代雕花立柱+顶部木梁+方格窗+窗外大漠土橘#8E5A2A持续流动（火车在飞驰）
车厢内还有6-8个其他赛博镖人。各人造型不同+不同程度的机械改造（有的全身改造+有的只是一只手臂+有的是面部装甲）+不同武器（弯刀/弓/长枪/飞镖）。**站位**：一个靠墙站着+抱臂+冷漠表情；一个坐在长凳上+擦拭手中机械义肢；一个倚在窗边+望着窗外大漠；一个蹲在地上+检查武器；一个站在车厢中央+背对镜头+面向某处。**关键构图**：极限仰拍extreme low angle+镖人显得高大压迫+站位美学asymmetric striking composition+全员恶人即视感menacing ensemble。视觉参考：《热血高校Crows Zero》群像+《杀死比尔Vol.1》88青龙会。
 
**镜头开始向上升**：穿过车厢顶部+从车厢内转到火车外部上方+视角变成俯瞰downward bird's-eye view。
 
**此时镜头固定揭示火车全貌**：火车从画面底部沿透视消失点延伸到画面深处+train extending from foreground bottom to distant vanishing point+绵延不绝stretching endlessly+靠近镜头底部车厢更大foreground carriages larger due to perspective。**关键**：每个车厢底部有大量金属机械腿交替抬起落下而非同步抬起落下+古代青铜+像千足虫centipede legs在沙地上奔跑+扬起沙尘+古代机关风。视觉参考：宫崎骏《幽灵公主》Tatara工坊机械+《风之谷》巨神兵+Boston Dynamics四足机器人。火车在大漠中飞驰+大漠土橘#8E5A2A+沙脊赭金#C49B5C+远天暮蓝#3A4258地平线（视觉参考《疯狂的麦克斯狂暴之路》沙漠车队）。
 
最后几秒镜头急速向上升rapidly accelerating upward+视角持续向下俯拍continues looking downward+火车在画面中越来越小+大漠展开+进入平流层+平流层天蓝#6088B8。来自镜头后方的白云从画面边缘进入clouds approaching from behind covering the lens+near total whiteness（云白#E8E8DC填满画面）。

### 第19条｜Layout】

@图1

---

A character design reference sheet. **Vertical 9:16 aspect ratio. Maximum resolution output.** Pure solid neutral light-warm-grey studio backdrop. Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45 degrees behind the subject, no fill light, no front light, no other light sources—dramatic cinematic studio lookbook lighting.

【Layout】

**2x2 grid with unequal panel heights**:
- Top row: two smaller panels (each approximately 1/3 of total canvas height) — head/face close-ups
- Bottom row: two larger panels (each approximately 2/3 of total canvas height) — full body shots
- Thin neutral light-grey dividing lines between all four panels
- All four panels share the same solid backdrop and same lighting setup

【4 Panels】

**Top-left panel — FRONT FACE CLOSE-UP**:
Tight close-up of the chieftain's face, framed from approximately collarbone-line up to top of head. Subject facing camera directly. Side-rim backlight catches the right edge of the face/hair (camera's left side of frame), leaving the left side of the face in deeper shadow with subtle ambient bounce. The LEFT EYE empty socket scar is partially in shadow but still clearly visible (rim light catches the scar tissue ridge). Face fully visible, no headwrap covering nose or mouth, headcloth pulled down loose around shoulders. Calm direct cold gaze with the remaining right eye toward camera.

**Top-right panel — SIDE FACE CLOSE-UP (PROFILE)**:
Tight close-up of the chieftain's face in pure side profile, framed from collarbone-line up to top of head. Subject facing camera-right (so the right side of his face faces camera-right and the LEFT side of his face faces camera—revealing the left eye scar/empty socket in full profile, dramatically lit by the rim backlight catching the back of the head and silhouetting the profile against the backdrop). Face fully visible.

**Bottom-left panel — FULL BODY FROM NECK DOWN, FRONT VIEW (NO FACE)**:
Full body shot from collarbone-line down to feet, facing camera directly. **The face/head is intentionally cropped out of frame at the top of this panel—only the neck, shoulders, torso, arms, hips, legs, and feet are visible.** Side-rim backlight catches the right shoulder and right side of body. **Both hands are empty—the right mechanical hand rests at his side or hooked into his belt buckle area, the left hand rests at his side. NO weapon held in either hand.** Weapons are visible strapped to body (war-blade across back, throwing knives at belt) but he is not gripping or holding any weapon.

**Bottom-right panel — FULL BODY BACK VIEW (INCLUDING BACK OF HEAD)**:
Full body shot from head to feet, facing directly away from camera (back of subject toward camera). **Includes back of head with short carefree black hair visible, headwrap loose around neck/shoulders.** War-blade strapped diagonally across the back fully visible. Trophy decorations on back of belt visible. Side-rim backlight catches right shoulder silhouette.

【Subject — same character across all 4 panels】

A male desert raider chieftain—weathered war-torn ancient lost-tech tribal hunter aesthetic, leader of the Sand Hunter tribe.

**Character Core**:
Approximately **35-45 years old** seasoned veteran warrior. Wiry lean muscular build (NOT bulky, NOT obese—desert hardened combat physique).

**ETHNICITY — CRITICAL (matches the reference photo provided)**:
**East Asian / Han Chinese frontier, with possible Silk Road mixed-blood influence but predominantly East Asian features**. Historically corresponds to the Han-deserter-turned-tribal-warlord archetype found in Sui-Tang frontier "杂胡" mixed-tribe peoples. NOT pure Caucasian European, NOT pure Central Asian Turkic, NOT pure Mongolian—**East Asian features matching the provided reference photo**.

**Face — DETAILED FEATURES (must match the reference photo)**:
- **Eyes**: **monolid or very low-fold (single-eyelid) almond-shaped eyes**, narrow elongated horizontal shape, slight upward tilt at outer corners, dark brown/black irises, calm direct cold gaze (this is a critical East Asian feature—NOT round Western double-fold eyes). The remaining right eye intact.
- **LEFT EYE missing — STRUCTURAL FEATURE THAT MUST BE PRESERVED EVEN WITH FACE REFERENCE**: a deep diagonal scar runs from above the left brow down across the empty eye socket and onto the upper cheek; NO eye patch, raw scarred tissue exposed, dark hollow socket. This is mandatory in every panel where the face is visible.
- **Eyebrows**: thick black eyebrows, **subtly asymmetric** (left brow slightly higher and straighter, right brow slightly more arched), natural unkempt growth, slight downward angle at outer ends.
- **Nose**: **straight medium-bridged nose**, neither high pointed nor flat, slightly rounded tip, normal-width nostrils (NOT thin pointed Western Caucasian, NOT flat-bridged exaggerated East Asian—medium real proportions matching reference).
- **Lips**: **thin upper lip, slightly fuller lower lip**, neutral set (not smiling, not frowning), straight horizontal mouth line.
- **Chin**: slightly pointed but not dramatically tapered, modest chin projection.
- **Face shape**: oval-to-slightly-square, balanced proportions. **Subtle natural facial asymmetry** (left side slightly higher than right, right eye slightly larger than left)—matches reference photo.
- **Skin**: **relatively fair East Asian skin tone** (NOT deeply tanned/bronze, NOT pale Caucasian—medium-fair Asian skin) but with weathered desert exposure showing in fine lines around eyes and forehead, occasional small healed mark on bridge of nose. The skin reads as "an East Asian man who has spent years in the desert sun but retains his original East Asian fair complexion underneath."
- **CLEAN-SHAVEN — NO BEARD, NO STUBBLE, NO MUSTACHE**: face is freshly clean-shaven, smooth skin on cheeks, jaw, and chin. Mandatory across all panels where face is visible.

**Hair**: **short black hair**, kept short on the sides, top hair short and casually messy (slightly tousled forward/up at the front in a carefree natural way—NOT styled, NOT modern hair gel, just naturally short-and-messy from desert living). Black, no graying.

**Headwear**: tribal headwrap of weathered tan-and-rust desert cloth, partially wrapped around the back of the head and back of the neck but FACE FULLY UNCOVERED in face close-up panels (cloth pulled down loose around shoulders, NOT covering nose or mouth, NOT hiding the hair on top).

**Upper body**: sleeveless weathered dark leather vest, shoulders and forearms exposed showing battle scars and faded tribal tattoo markings on shoulders and chest.

**Lower body**: loose weathered dark leather trousers, multiple belt straps and small pouches around waist.

**Right forearm — local mechanical modification**: right forearm and hand replaced with a CRUDE PRIMITIVE BRONZE MECHANICAL PROSTHETIC—NOT precision cybernetic, NOT modern. War-torn brass-and-leather replacement with visible bolts, leather strap bindings to upper arm stump, exposed gear mechanisms at wrist, slight verdigris patina (Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845, Aged Gold #846035 accent—same patina as Sand Hunter tribe's combat exoskeletons). Desert blacksmith's salvage repair aesthetic.

**Trophy decorations**: animal bones (small skulls, claws, vertebrae fragments) and metal scraps tied to belt and shoulder straps. Fragments of defeated enemies' gear hanging from leather cords—chieftain wears combat history as ornament. Moderate quantity (more than common raiders, less than would be flashy).

**Footwear**: rugged desert leather boots wrapped with cloth bindings around the calves.

【Weapons - on body but NOT in hands】
- **Main weapon**: massive heavy two-handed war blade—curved sand-pitted iron cleaver-machete hybrid, brutal not elegant, bone-and-leather wrapped handle. **Strapped diagonally across the back** (NOT held in hand).
- **Secondary**: 3-4 short throwing knives at waist belt, coiled grappling rope/bolas at hip
- **Ranged**: composite recurve bow strapped diagonally across back, small arrow quiver attached
- **CRITICAL: Both hands are empty in all panels.** No weapon gripped, no weapon held, no hand on a weapon hilt. Right mechanical hand rests naturally at his side or hooked casually into the belt buckle area. Left hand rests naturally at his side.

【Color Palette】
Desert sand tan, deep burnt umber leather, aged bronze patina (Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845 + Aged Gold #846035), dried-blood-red tribal accent on cloth bindings. ALL materials weathered war-torn dust-coated, NOT clean NOT polished.

【Pose - same across all 4 panels】
Default standing pose with subtle power stance—weight slightly back, feet shoulder-width apart, **both hands empty and resting at sides or hooked into belt area** (right mechanical hand and left biological hand both visibly free of weapons). Subtle authority/menace presence purely through bearing—NOT aggressive action pose, NOT relaxed civilian, NOT showy. The body language reads "veteran chieftain at quiet confidence, weapons sheathed because none are needed right now."

【Critical consistency】
SAME individual character in all 4 panels—identical face structure (East Asian features matching reference), identical LEFT eye scar/empty socket, identical clean-shaven jaw and chin (NO beard, NO stubble), identical clothing items, identical mechanical right forearm, identical trophy decorations, identical weapons in identical mounts/positions (all on body, none in hand). Same studio backdrop, same single-sided side-rim backlighting setup across all 4 panels.

【Lighting - critical】
**Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45-degrees behind subject. NO fill light. NO front light. NO ambient. NO other light sources.** Strong dramatic edge-light catches the right side of subject silhouette in every panel, leaving the left side of subject in deep falloff shadow with subtle ambient bounce. Cinematic high-end fashion-lookbook studio photography (Peter Lindbergh / Annie Leibovitz portrait style with single rim source).

【Style】
Hyperrealistic photographic character design rendering—shot like a high-end practical costume photographed in studio for a film production design board. NOT illustration, NOT obvious 3D CGI render.

【实操注意】

1. 跑出来重点检查（按风险优先级）：

   - **clean-shaven NO beard NO stubble**——容易跑成蓄须老炮（之前v3的设定残留）。必须clean刮干净
   - **左眼缺失+疤痕**——结构性特征，face reference不能覆盖这个+必须在face panels mandatory可见
   - **35-45岁**——不是20岁年轻+不是60岁老人。weathered veteran but still in prime
   - **两手都空**——容易跑成手握武器（之前v3的pose设定）。两手都要明显空+武器都在身上不在手上
   - **右前臂机械义肢**——粗糙war-torn bronze不是赛博精良
   - **9:16+4格不等大**+左下panel不能露脸
   - **同沙猎部落DNA**（青铜patina+部族纹+战利品）

2. 跑歪兜底加强：
   - 跑出欧美双眼皮 → "monolid single-eyelid East Asian eyes, narrow elongated almond shape, NO Western double-fold, matching the reference photo"
   - 跑出蓄须 → "ABSOLUTELY CLEAN-SHAVEN, no beard, no stubble, no mustache, smooth shaven skin on cheeks jaw and chin, mandatory in every face panel"
   - 跑出modern K-pop脸 → "weathered 35-45 year old desert veteran East Asian face, sun-aged fine lines around eyes, NOT modern K-pop, NOT smooth young actor face, real frontier veteran with combat history"
   - 跑出手握武器 → "BOTH HANDS COMPLETELY EMPTY, right mechanical hand resting at side or hooked into belt buckle area, left hand resting at side, NO weapon held, NO grip on any weapon hilt, weapons on body but not in hand"
   - 跑出65岁老人脸 → "35-45 years old veteran in his prime, NOT elderly, NOT gray-haired, weathered but still capable"
   - 左眼疤痕没了 → "LEFT EYE COMPLETELY MISSING with deep diagonal scar across empty socket, NO eye patch, raw scar tissue exposed, mandatory in every face panel even when face reference is being matched"

3. **关于使用方式建议**：
   - 如果用GPT-Image-2 image2image：建议把参考图作为face reference input + 用此prompt作为text guidance
   - 如果用纯text-to-image：此prompt独立能跑出matching reference的face（但可能需要2-3次iteration达到完美匹配）

【参考锁定】
@图2 作为刘回(沙猎部落首领)的视觉锚定+独眼刀疤+斯巴达式爆炸短发+#6B2419部落血红与#B8A082米色围巾+赤膊纹身+机械义肢右臂+破旧粗革无袖+背挂大型弯刀严格按此图渲染。
