【镜头与机位】
Camera: 50mm prime lens + rectilinear lens projection + no barrel distortion + no fisheye + no wide-angle warping + geometric lines remain straight + classic portrait/character close-up cinematography focal length + Christopher Doyle 50mm prime aesthetic + Wong Kar-wai 50mm character portraiture style.

Camera position: positioned approximately at Liu Hui's facial level (medium height, riding-mount eye line) + camera angled to capture Liu Hui's right 3/4 face profile + camera at medium distance from Liu Hui's face approximately 1.5 meters away + slight slight upward angle by 5 degrees giving Liu Hui slight dominance in frame.

Frame: 16:9 horizontal aspect ratio cinematic single frame still.

【构图】
Medium close-up framing. Liu Hui's head and shoulders dominate the right two-thirds of the frame. The framing extends from just above his head down to mid-chest level, including his shoulders, neck, upper torso showing his bare tattooed chest and tribal scarves. The Mechanical Saiga's bronze-armored neck and partial head are visible in the bottom-left foreground area where Liu Hui is mounted, the Saiga's rear-pointing twin spire horns of #846035 ancient gold partially visible at lower-left edge of frame, the Saiga's bronze armor body slightly out of frame to the left.

Liu Hui's face is captured in 3/4 right profile - his right cheek and most of the right side of his face directly facing camera, the complete healthy right eye fully visible and in sharp focus, only a partial sliver of his left side visible (where the eye-patch and scar are partially obscured behind his profile) - the right side of his face dominates the visual reading, with the eye-patched left side suggested but not centered.

【面部表情 关键】
Liu Hui's facial expression: a calm cold villain smile of restrained predatory intelligence. The corners of his mouth lifted in a subtle slight smirk - mouth corners turn upward by a small amount approximately 5-10 degrees, lips closed or slightly parted but no teeth showing, no aggression no rage no madness. His complete right eye is sharp focused intense calculating gaze fixed forward in the direction of his prey (slightly screen-left toward where the protagonist's ship is fleeing in the background). His brow is relaxed not furrowed. The expression reads as: "the prey is already mine, I have all the time in the world." Cold confidence + restrained predatory satisfaction + the calm of a hunter who knows the kill is inevitable. NOT a Joker grin + NOT a feral snarl + NOT teeth bared + NOT eyebrows lifted + NOT manic + NOT shouting. The expression is the kind of calm villain smile seen in characters like Anton Chigurh in No Country for Old Men or Immortan Joe's calm moments in Mad Max Fury Road - quiet menace not loud menace.

His spiky black hair flies wildly backward in the slipstream wind from the high-speed charge, frozen in motion at the moment of capture, individual hair strands streaking horizontally backward from his head. His #6B2419 deep crimson and #B8A082 cream tribal scarves trail violently behind him backward in the wind, frozen in mid-flutter. The scarves and hair flying backward emphasize the high speed.

【空间关系与背景动态模糊】
Background: visible behind Liu Hui's head and shoulders, the desert landscape and pursuit elements are rendered with motion blur from the high-speed forward motion, the background appearing to streak horizontally from screen-right to screen-left.

The motion blur is moderate not extreme - background elements remain identifiable but visibly streaked. Through the motion-blurred background, several elements are visible:

(1) In the upper-middle background slightly screen-left of Liu Hui's head profile, the @图6 protagonist's mechanical desert ship is clearly visible at moderate distance, larger than it appeared in the establishing shot earlier in the sequence - now occupying about 1/6 of the frame area in the background, the ship's bronze hull and 9 mechanical legs visible through the motion blur, indicating Liu Hui has closed significant distance toward his prey.

(2) In the middle-distance background streaking past in motion blur on screen-left and screen-right, the silhouettes of two 
@图1 sand-skiffs are now level with or being passed by Liu Hui's mount - the sand-skiffs appearing slightly behind or alongside Liu Hui's plane of motion, no longer ahead of him in the chase, indicating Liu Hui's Saiga has accelerated to overtake the sand-skiffs in the time elapsed.

(3) The vast #8E5A2A desert earth orange ground and distant horizon line streak past in horizontal motion blur, the #C8581F burnt orange dusk sky filling the upper portion of the frame.

The spatial story told by this frame: Liu Hui has accelerated past the sand-skiffs and is now closing rapidly on the protagonist's ship - the chase has progressed from "Liu Hui far behind" to "Liu Hui imminently overtaking."

【光线】
Golden hour late afternoon dusk lighting. The sun is positioned slightly behind Liu Hui (camera screen-left and slightly behind), creating dramatic backlight rim-lighting along his hair edges and right shoulder edge of #F5C870 golden glow. His face is partially side-lit by reflected dusk light, his right eye and cheekbone catching warm #F5C870 golden highlights, his left side of face fading into deeper #1A1610 shadow tones. Atmospheric haze of #D9A55C amber dust visible in motion-blurred background. Subtle anamorphic lens flare from the dusk sun visible at frame edge.

【色彩】
Strict adherence to 
@图3 color palette: #1A1610 deep shadow black for hair and shadows, #7A6845 mecha bronze olive for Saiga armor and Liu Hui's prosthetic arm visible in lower-left frame edge, #846035 ancient gold for Saiga horns visible at frame edge, #4FC3D9 cyan electric arc subtly visible if Saiga's eye is partially in frame, #F5C870 golden sun rim-light, #C8581F burnt orange dusk sky in motion-blurred background, #6B2419 deep crimson Sand Hunter tribal red for Liu Hui's primary scarf, #B8A082 cream cloth for Liu Hui's secondary scarf, #4A2F1F eye patch dark brown leather for the patch on his left side, #5A2A1F dark blood-red scar visible on partially-seen left cheek, #2D2A2A dark gunmetal gray for tattoos and prosthetic arm, #8E5A2A desert earth orange ground in motion-blurred background, #C49B5C ochre golden in dust haze.

【风格质感】
Photorealistic film still captured with real 35mm motion picture camera + Kodak Vision3 500T color negative film stock + analog film grain texture visible + photochemical halation around bright highlights + slight subtle anamorphic lens flare from the dusk sun + organic film noise + tactile weathered real skin texture on Liu Hui's face showing pores wrinkles fine details + lived-in battle-worn appearance + the kind of skin and texture detail that only comes from real photography on film.

【关键约束】
镜头铁律: 50mm prime lens + rectilinear lens projection + geometric lines straight + Liu Hui's facial proportions natural and undistorted + classic portrait focal length + NO wide-angle distortion + NO barrel distortion + NO fisheye + Liu Hui's face must be rendered with anatomically natural proportions not exaggerated.

构图铁律: Liu Hui occupies right 2/3 of frame in 3/4 right profile + Saiga visible in lower-left foreground edge only as a small bronze visual anchor + background contains motion-blurred protagonist ship clearly identifiable in upper-background and motion-blurred sand-skiffs alongside or behind Liu Hui's plane indicating overtaking.

表情铁律: 嘴角微扬冷静狩猎笑容+lips closed or barely parted+no teeth+mouth corners lifted slightly approximately 5-10 degrees+eyes sharp calculating not bulging+brow relaxed not furrowed+expression reads as cold confidence and restrained predatory satisfaction+绝不疯狂+绝不咆哮+绝不张牙舞爪+绝不Joker grin+the calm of Anton Chigurh or Immortan Joe quiet menace not loud menace。

空间叙事铁律: background must show protagonist ship clearly visible in upper background indicating Liu Hui has closed distance + sand-skiffs visible alongside or behind Liu Hui's plane indicating overtaking has occurred + background motion-blurred but elements remain identifiable not abstracted into pure light streaks.

风格铁律: 35mm film stock photography texture+analog film grain+photochemical look+NOT 3D rendered+NOT CGI+NOT video game graphics+NOT Unreal Engine render+NOT Blender render+NOT clean digital surface+NOT plastic look+all surfaces must show real physical wear and weathering+film print quality not digital cinema clean.

NOT 24mm wide-angle + NOT barrel distortion + NOT forced perspective + NOT extreme low-angle + NOT facial distortion + NOT teeth showing + NOT shouting + NOT manic + NOT joker grin + NOT feral snarl + NOT clear blue sky + NOT modern vehicles + NOT cyberpunk neon + NOT clean digital surface + NOT cartoon + NOT illustrated + NOT painterly + NOT concept art + NOT digital painting + NOT typical AI generated illustration aesthetic + 无字幕水印边框装饰花纹。


### 第21条｜万物生第1集 副手登场段 抽象故事板 1x5竖排版 v2

**万物生第1集 副手登场段 抽象故事板 1x5竖排版 v2**
【用途】副手登场段的15秒分镜图+5个panel故事板+1x5竖排布局+每panel保持16:9横构图+确保每格构图与最终视频画面比例一致

A 5-panel film storyboard laid out as a 5-row 1-column vertical grid. Overall canvas aspect ratio is 16:45 vertical (extremely tall portrait orientation). Each individual panel within the grid is a 16:9 horizontal landscape rectangle. The 5 panels are stacked vertically from top to bottom. Each panel is a rectangular pencil-and-marker rough sketch with a white margin border and a small information strip underneath each panel.

Hand-drawn animation school storyboard aesthetic with pencil line-work, grey marker shading, occasional red-pencil arrow annotations indicating camera movement or character motion arc, off-white paper texture background. Deliberately rough and abstract sketch quality - NOT a finished illustration - all human figures rendered as minimal stick-figure silhouettes or simple body shapes with no facial features no clothing details no accessories visible, all objects represented as basic geometric shapes (boxes for crates, triangles for sails, simple curved lines for ship hull). The purpose is to communicate composition and spatial relationships only, not visual details.

[PANEL 1 顶部 - WIDE ESTABLISHING AERIAL]
Label underneath: "PANEL 1 · WIDE AERIAL · stratosphere · static · 2s · SFX: bird scream"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: extreme wide aerial shot showing high-altitude clouds in upper portion of frame and vast desert landscape in lower portion. The desert horizon line sits at the bottom third of the frame. A tiny silhouette of a winged mechanical bird-figure appears as a small dark shape descending from upper-center of frame, drawn as a simple rough pencil silhouette of spread-wing bird shape no larger than a fingernail in the panel. A red-pencil arrow points downward from the bird-figure indicating direction of dive. The desert below shows a few faint horizontal lines suggesting dunes and at the very bottom-right of the desert area a tiny barely-visible rectangular shape with a few small dot-shapes around it represents the protagonist ship and pursuing sand-skiffs as distant tiny silhouettes. Cloud layer is rendered as soft grey marker smudges in upper portion of frame.

[PANEL 2 第二格 - OTS DIVE TRACKING]
Label underneath: "PANEL 2 · OTS DIVE / 50mm / 3s / SFX: wind tear, war cry"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: over-the-shoulder shot from behind a diving figure. In the immediate foreground occupying left and right edges of the frame, the rough silhouette outlines of a figure's shoulders and partial wing edges visible as dark silhouette frames at the screen edges (the over-the-shoulder element). The figure is shown from behind with wings partially spread visible at frame edges, drawn as simple pencil silhouettes. The middle and lower portion of the frame shows the desert landscape rapidly approaching the camera from a steep diving angle - dunes drawn as simple curved horizontal lines with strong perspective lines pointing toward a vanishing point in the lower portion of frame. In the deep middle-distance below, a small rectangular shape with leg-lines underneath represents the protagonist's ship as a small target the figure is diving toward. A red-pencil arrow shows the dive trajectory descending diagonally from upper-left to lower-right.

[PANEL 3 第三格 - REVERSE SHOT FROM BELOW]
Label underneath: "PANEL 3 · REVERSE LOW ANGLE / 35mm / 4s / wings deploy"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: low-angle reverse shot looking up at the diving figure. The frame shows the figure descending from upper portion toward the camera, drawn as a stick-figure with arms and legs in dive pose, with wings partially deployed extending outward from the back. The wings are drawn as simple curved-line shapes radiating outward from the figure's back. Above the figure in the upper portion of frame, the cloud layer and sky background drawn as soft marker shading. The figure occupies the central upper portion of frame, growing larger in the panel composition compared to panel 2. A red-pencil arrow indicates wings spreading outward from the figure's back.

[PANEL 4 第四格 - REVERSE TO PROTAGONIST DIVING INTO CRATE]
Label underneath: "PANEL 4 · MEDIUM SHOT PROTAGONIST CRATE-DIVE / 50mm / 3s / SFX: chest banging, distant scream"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: medium shot of the protagonist on the ship deck. The protagonist is drawn as a simple stick-figure with NO upper body visible at all - the protagonist's entire upper half (head, torso, arms) has plunged headfirst INTO a large rectangular crate-shape in the center of the frame. Only the protagonist's lower half is visible sticking out from the open crate top: the legs and butt are sticking up into the air at a comical angle, legs kicking awkwardly, feet flailing - like a cartoon character who has dived too deep into something. The crate occupies the center-foreground of the frame, drawn as a simple rectangular box-shape with the lid open and the protagonist's lower body comically protruding from the opening. Several small geometric shapes (squares, circles, triangles) are scattered around the crate representing items the protagonist has already pulled out and tossed aside. The deck is drawn as horizontal lines representing wooden planks. In the deep background visible at the upper portion of frame, faint suggestions of ship railings and mast verticals as simple straight pencil lines. Small motion-line clusters around the protagonist's flailing feet and the scattered objects suggest hurried slapstick comedic rummaging.

[PANEL 5 底部 - LANDING POSE STERN]
Label underneath: "PANEL 5 · MEDIUM WIDE LANDING / 35mm / 3s / SFX: impact, wing fold"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: medium-wide shot on the ship's stern area. The ship's stern building structure is drawn as simple rectangular and triangular geometric shapes - a vertical mast in the right portion of frame with a triangular sail at top, and the stern building structure as a stepped rectangular shape in the middle-distance. The figure (副手) has just landed on the deck in the center foreground of the frame, drawn as a stick-figure in a one-knee-down landing pose with one hand touching ground and the other arm bent. Wings are shown half-folded behind the figure as simple curved lines arcing back from the shoulders. A small dust-cloud burst is drawn around the figure's landing point as soft marker smudges. The figure's head is angled forward looking off-screen toward the screen-left direction (toward the bow of the ship where the protagonist is). A few small motion lines show wings still folding.

【整体美术方向】
Classic animation school storyboard aesthetic. Pencil line-work with visible sketch lines, occasional construction lines and rough corrections. Grey marker shading for tonal areas (sky, shadows, smoke). Red-pencil arrow annotations on panels 1, 2, 3, and 5 to indicate camera movement and character motion arcs. Off-white paper texture background. NOT a finished illustration. NOT photorealistic. NOT detailed character art. NOT colored. The entire storyboard is intentionally monochromatic (pencil grey + red arrow accents) to emphasize that this is a planning document for composition only, not a visual reference for final rendering.

Title at top center reads "WANWU SHENG EP1 SEG5 - LIEUTENANT BIRD-DIVE ENTRANCE STORYBOARD" in small clean black sans-serif text.

【核心约束】
布局铁律: 整体画布是16:45 vertical aspect ratio竖向超长比例+5 panels stacked vertically from top to bottom in a 5-row 1-column grid+绝对不要横向排列+绝对不要2x3网格+绝对不要任何其他布局。

每格比例铁律: EACH individual panel inside the grid MUST be a 16:9 horizontal landscape rectangle+每一个panel都是宽多于高的横向矩形+绝对不要竖向panel+绝对不要正方形panel+5个panel高度相等+5个panel宽度相等+5个panel都严格保持16:9 horizontal landscape ratio.

简笔画铁律: ALL human figures must be rendered as MINIMAL STICK-FIGURE SILHOUETTES or basic body shapes only+NO facial features+NO clothing details+NO accessories+NO equipment details+NO weapon details+NO hair details+NO color on figures+absolutely NO character design rendering+the figures are placeholder shapes indicating position and pose only+not character illustrations.

几何形状铁律: ALL objects must be rendered as BASIC GEOMETRIC SHAPES only+ship as simple rectangles+sails as triangles+crates as squares+masts as straight lines+wings as simple curved lines+no detail rendering of any object+all objects are placeholder shapes indicating spatial position only.

NOT horizontal layout+NOT 1-row 5-column grid+NOT 2x3 grid+NOT square panels+NOT vertical 9:16 panels inside the grid+NOT photorealistic+NOT 3D rendered+NOT CGI+NOT video game graphics+NOT detailed concept art+NOT finished character illustration+NOT colored characters+NOT realistic lighting+NOT cinematic photography+NOT detailed costume design+NOT detailed weapon design+NOT shaded character details+NOT clean digital line art+NOT manga-style detailed art+NOT comic book detailed art+NOT brand-style mascot illustration+无字幕水印+无装饰元素除铅笔箭头与shot label外。

### 第28条｜万物生第1集 段6主角反击+副手变身 抽象故事板 1x4竖排版

**万物生第1集 段6主角反击+副手变身 抽象故事板 1x4竖排版**
【用途】段6主角反击+副手变身15秒分镜图+4个panel故事板+1x4竖排布局+每panel保持16:9横构图+确保每格构图与最终视频画面比例一致

A 4-panel film storyboard laid out as a 4-row 1-column vertical grid. Overall canvas aspect ratio is 16:36 vertical (extremely tall portrait orientation). Each individual panel within the grid is a 16:9 horizontal landscape rectangle. The 4 panels are stacked vertically from top to bottom. Each panel is a rectangular pencil-and-marker rough sketch with a white margin border and a small information strip underneath each panel.

Hand-drawn animation school storyboard aesthetic with pencil line-work, grey marker shading, occasional red-pencil arrow annotations indicating camera movement or character motion arc, off-white paper texture background. Deliberately rough and abstract sketch quality - NOT a finished illustration - all human figures rendered as minimal stick-figure silhouettes or simple body shapes with no facial features no clothing details no accessories visible, all objects represented as basic geometric shapes (cannons as cylinders, cannonballs as solid circles, ships as simple rectangles, sand explosions as expanding cloud shapes, mechanical bird as simple bird-silhouette outline, armor pieces as small geometric shards floating in air). The purpose is to communicate composition and spatial relationships only, not visual details.

[PANEL 1 顶部 - SHIP HULL EXTERIOR LOW UP-ANGLE PROTAGONIST FIRES CANNON]
Label underneath: "PANEL 1 · SHIP HULL EXT LOW UP-ANGLE / 50mm / 4s / SFX: cannon BOOM at 4s"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: shot from outside the ship hull looking up at slight low angle toward the deck railing edge. The ship's hull railing occupies the bottom one-third of the frame as a horizontal line with vertical post supports. Behind the railing in the middle-foreground stick-figure protagonist figure is rising up holding a cylindrical cannon barrel hoisted onto the right shoulder. The cannon is drawn as a long simple cylinder shape angled forward and outward toward screen-left. The protagonist figure shows tense effort posture - body slightly leaning back from cannon weight, arms strained holding the barrel. A small puff cloud of smoke and red-pencil flash mark drawn at the cannon muzzle indicating firing moment. Background sky drawn as soft grey marker shading occupying the upper half of frame. A red-pencil arrow shows the slight camera push-in toward the ship from outside.

[PANEL 2 第二格 - BULLET TIME 180 ORBIT TO LIU HUI SAND IMPACT]
Label underneath: "PANEL 2 · BULLET TIME ORBIT / 50mm / 5s / SFX: low whoosh, BOOM impact"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: extreme bullet time shot. In the foreground center a black cannonball drawn as a simple solid circle is frozen mid-flight with a curved smoke trail behind it like a comma-shape. Around the cannonball drawn motion-streak lines indicating slow-motion air. The frame shows two spatial moments captured in one composition: in the center-foreground the frozen cannonball with smoke trail, and in the deeper distance toward the right side of frame, a ground-level sand impact zone drawn as an expanding circular cloud-burst sand explosion with radial lines. Near the impact zone a stick-figure on top of a four-legged mechanical creature shape (representing Liu Hui on Saiga) is shown in reaction pose - the creature's front legs raised in emergency stop, the rider stick-figure leaning back. Soft grey marker shading for slow-motion atmospheric haze. A red-pencil curved arrow shows the camera 180-degree orbit path from cannonball to impact zone.

[PANEL 3 第三格 - LIEUTENANT MEDIUM CLOSE-UP YELLING]
Label underneath: "PANEL 3 · LIEUTENANT MCU YELLING / 85mm / 2s / SFX: shout 'LAO DA!'"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: medium close-up of the lieutenant figure positioned in the center of the frame. The figure is drawn as a stick-figure with broad-shoulder fusion-form silhouette including a bird-head helmet shape on top of head and partial wing edges visible behind shoulders. The figure is shown from chest up only - half-body framing. The figure's body is angled slightly to one side with the bird-head turned facing toward off-screen direction in profile or 3/4 view. The figure's posture conveys angry yelling - mouth open shape drawn as a small dark oval. Background is simple sky drawn as soft marker shading occupying most of the frame, with a small partial silhouette of ship's stern structure suggested at one edge of frame as faint lines. A small red-pencil arrow indicates slight camera push-in toward the figure.

[PANEL 4 底部 - WALK-AND-TRANSFORM HERO LANDING POSE]
Label underneath: "PANEL 4 · WALK-TRANSFORM TO HERO POSE / 50mm to 35mm / 4s / SFX: armor disengage, bird-mount lock"
Panel aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape.
Composition: this panel shows the final ending pose of a walk-and-transform sequence. In the center-foreground of the frame a stick-figure of the lieutenant in human-body form (without fusion exoskeleton) stands centered, drawn in low-angle hero pose - the camera is positioned below the figure's waist height looking up, so only the figure's upper-body and head are within frame, with the lower portion of frame showing the ground/deck plane. The figure occupies the central foreground at half-body framing with an imposing silhouette posture - one hand at hip or relaxed at side, head tilted slightly downward to look at the camera with implied authority. Behind the figure approximately 2 meters back, a separate four-legged bird-creature silhouette is drawn standing on the deck, positioned slightly to one side of the figure not directly behind. The bird-creature is drawn as a simple long-necked four-legged mechanical bird outline with a long tail and partial wing folded shapes. Around and between the two figures, several small geometric shard shapes scattered in air as if armor pieces are still settling in their final positions during the last fraction of the transform. Background shows soft marker shading representing the deck and sky, with faint suggestions of ship stern structure as simple straight lines. A red-pencil arrow at the bottom of the frame indicates the camera's low up-angle position relative to the standing figure.

【整体美术方向】
Classic animation school storyboard aesthetic. Pencil line-work with visible sketch lines, occasional construction lines and rough corrections. Grey marker shading for tonal areas (sky, shadows, smoke, sand explosions). Red-pencil arrow annotations on all 4 panels to indicate camera movement and key motion arcs. Off-white paper texture background. NOT a finished illustration. NOT photorealistic. NOT detailed character art. NOT colored. The entire storyboard is intentionally monochromatic (pencil grey + red arrow accents) to emphasize that this is a planning document for composition only, not a visual reference for final rendering.

Title at top center reads "WANWU SHENG EP1 SEG6 - PROTAGONIST CANNON RETALIATION + LIEUTENANT TRANSFORM STORYBOARD" in small clean black sans-serif text.

【核心约束】
布局铁律: 整体画布是16:36 vertical aspect ratio竖向超长比例+4 panels stacked vertically from top to bottom in a 4-row 1-column grid+绝对不要横向排列+绝对不要2x2网格+绝对不要任何其他布局。

每格比例铁律: EACH individual panel inside the grid MUST be a 16:9 horizontal landscape rectangle+每一个panel都是宽多于高的横向矩形+绝对不要竖向panel+绝对不要正方形panel+4个panel高度相等+4个panel宽度相等+4个panel都严格保持16:9 horizontal landscape ratio.

风格铁律: 纯手绘铅笔素描风格+pencil-and-marker rough sketch+animation school storyboard aesthetic+visible sketch lines+rough construction corrections+灰色marker shading+red-pencil camera arrow annotations+off-white paper background texture+intentionally rough and abstract+NOT finished illustration+NOT photorealistic+NOT colored+NOT detailed character rendering+NOT clean line art+NOT digital perfectly clean lines+all lines must look hand-drawn with natural variations.

简笔画铁律: ALL human figures must be rendered as MINIMAL STICK-FIGURE SILHOUETTES or basic body shapes only+NO facial features+NO clothing details+NO accessories+NO equipment details+NO weapon details+NO hair details+NO color on figures+absolutely NO character design rendering+the figures are placeholder shapes indicating position and pose only+not character illustrations.

几何形状铁律: ALL objects must be rendered as BASIC GEOMETRIC SHAPES only+cannon as a simple cylinder+cannonball as a solid circle+ship hull railing as horizontal lines with verticals+sand explosion as expanding circular cloud with radial lines+mechanical bird-creature as simple long-necked four-legged outline+armor pieces as small floating geometric shards+no detail rendering of any object+all objects are placeholder shapes indicating spatial position only.

NOT horizontal layout+NOT 1-row 4-column grid+NOT 2x2 grid+NOT square panels+NOT vertical 9:16 panels inside the grid+NOT photorealistic+NOT 3D rendered+NOT CGI+NOT video game graphics+NOT detailed concept art+NOT finished character illustration+NOT colored characters+NOT realistic lighting+NOT cinematic photography+NOT detailed costume design+NOT detailed weapon design+NOT shaded character details+NOT clean digital line art+NOT manga-style detailed art+NOT comic book detailed art+NOT brand-style mascot illustration+无字幕水印+无装饰元素除铅笔箭头与shot label外。

Mad Max: Fury Road kinetic chase aesthetics + Wong Kar-wai painterly desaturation + Christopher Doyle sand-haze cinematography + Kodak Vision3 35mm film grain + golden hour low-angle backlight + 16:9 anamorphic + handheld micro-tremor on tracking rig.

【阶段1：开场琥珀沙尘散去】
首帧画面被#D9A55C琥珀色沙尘雾完全填满+无任何可见地形+只有暖色雾气+承接前段白云遮罩状态。雾气从画面边缘向中心退散+中央先露出#8E5A2A大漠土橘色沙地+然后#C8581F落日橙天空显现+地平线上#3A4258远天暮蓝带+画面左侧低位出现#F5C870落日金太阳+太阳逆光照射形成god rays穿过剩余沙尘+#C49B5C沙脊赭金色沙丘起伏覆盖中景。地面有零星骆驼骨架+夯土残壁。

第8秒【阶段2：飞船爆发出场】
画面正中最后一层沙雾被一个庞然大物撕开+里的巨型机械飞船以全速狂奔状态砸入画面正中央+占画面宽度约50%。船体#7A6845铜青绿主色+#846035暗金装饰板+船腹下方9条细长昆虫式机械腿全部展开+每条腿独立踏地+腿与腿之间步频错开不同步+每次落地踏起#C49B5C赭金色沙浪向后炸开。船身侧面布幡狂烈飘动+铜钟摇晃+船体接缝处#4FC3D9青色电弧线条脉动闪烁+船头缓慢摇晃。

【阶段3：平行侧拍狂飙追击】
机位转入严格的平行侧面跟拍+相机以飞船完全相同的速度横向移动+飞船永远锁死画面正中央+wide shot距离全程不变+船占画面50%。地面与远方沙丘从画面右侧向画面左侧高速反向流动+背景速度极快+给出强烈的横向飞驰感。9条机械腿持续独立galloping+每条腿步频不同+腿落地节奏错落不齐+沙地砂砾飞溅。

画面右侧远处+三艘@图5 同款沙艇追进画面+沙艇排成松散三角阵型+每艘沙艇上站立2-3名沙猎喽啰：包含@图2 的女喽啰+
@图3 的年轻男喽啰+
@图4 的老喽啰+喽啰身上#6B2419沙猎血红布条飘动+皮革+毛皮+裸露伤疤+粗糙骨片机械义肢。沙艇#7A6845铜色机械底盘+破旧布幡疯狂翻卷。

最靠近飞船的领头沙艇上+老喽啰双手握住一根铜色钩索+钩索头是带倒钩的金属爪+他举钩索过头顶+钩索悬在头顶但不释放。

画面后方远地平线上+#5A4677靛紫色沙暴墙占据画面后方约1/4高度+越往下越深变#3D2C5C深紫+沙暴墙缓慢扩大占据更多地平线。

夕阳侧光从画面左侧低位斜射+船体右侧被#F5C870金色光镶边+喽啰背光只剩#1A1610漆黑剪影+扬尘空气中可见#F5C870光柱条纹。

【声音设计】
0-2秒：低频沙暴白噪声呼啸+从远处渐近+muffled低频持续drone。
2秒整：船砸入画面瞬间+一声沉重的低频金属BRAAAM crescendo脉冲+9条机械腿同时落地的厚重金属脚步crunchy crashing声开始建立。
3-15秒持续：9条机械腿以错落不同步的rhythm持续脚步声+每脚crunchy金属落沙crash+船身铜钟金属clink碰撞回响+布幡rip-flap撕扯声+三艘沙艇低频沙地划过shhhhh的滑行音+喽啰部落式嘶吼+老喽啰挥钩索时金属链条jangling。
背景层：风声呼啸+zhuanang沙暴远处低频rumbling逐渐增强+给出后方沙暴墙的存在感。
仅生成动作音效与环境音效+不要bgm+不要抒情背景音乐+sound design only ambient and action sounds NO background music。

【关键约束】
机位铁律：阶段1阶段2固定机位+阶段3切入严格的平行侧面横向跟拍+相机与船保持完全相同速度+船永远锁死画面正中央+全程wide shot距离不变+无变焦+无dolly+无环绕。
9条腿铁律：飞船下方共9条机械腿+腿与腿之间步频独立错落+不可同步+不可两两成对+每条腿独立踏地+galloping而非walking。
沙艇位置铁律：3艘沙艇全程位于画面右侧后方+不进入画面正中+不超过飞船。
钩索铁律：老喽啰钩索举过头顶后悬停+不释放+不抛出+不接触飞船。
画面纵深铁律：前景飞船+中景沙艇与喽啰+远景沙暴墙+三层纵深。

NOT slow motion+NOT speed ramping+实时正常速度+NOT静态镜头+全程高速横向位移+NOT cyberpunk neon+NOT modern vehicles+NOT modern weapons+NOT clear blue sky+NOT green vegetation+NOT polished sci-fi materials+NOT futuristic soldiers+NOT illustrated+NOT cartoon+无字幕+无水印。

【场景物理设置】
本段是追逐戏中的副手登场+变身戏码+追逐戏背景持续:主角飞船@图6 在大漠上以全速狂奔状态向画面深处方向逃跑+9条机械腿持续狂奔+船底沙尘plume扬起+船腿每秒3-4步独立galloping节奏+绝对不能渲染成船腿缓慢移动+绝对不能渲染成船静止。
本段画面展示船尾甲板+主体是副手+不展示外景沙艇与刘回+但船在持续高速逃跑(通过音轨与微妙画面颠簸维持)。

【前段 副手合体形态medium close-up喊"老大!"】
机位:相机位于@图6 主角船船尾甲板某位置+medium close-up中近景+焦段85mm slight telephoto+rectilinear lens projection+no barrel distortion+相机有微妙的slow push-in缓慢推进运动+不完全固定。
画面:
@图4 副手合体形态+刚刚落地在船尾甲板+参考关键帧@图2 但副手已经从落地姿态站起+副手胸部以上half-body framing入画+副手身体侧对镜头+鸟头机械头盔向画面外某一侧倾斜+鸟头长喙朝向画面外某方向+#4FC3D9青色电弧机械眼瞳孔放大+脸部表情焦急愤怒+眉头紧皱+面部肌肉紧绷+下颌张开呼喊状+副手张嘴爆发一声短促有力的怒吼"老大!"——这一声吼是焦急愤怒的中年女性嗓音+喉咙挤压感+像Mad Max部落女将看到首领遇袭的暴怒呼喊。

副手身上服装与装备:副手合体形态的#7A6845铜青绿装甲+#846035暗金装饰+#6B2419沙猎血红与#4A2030暗紫红织物在风中翻飞+#6B4A2E赭红羽毛+#2E1F15深褐羽尖。
末段:副手喊完"老大!"后愤怒地向镜头方向转头+这个转头方向延续到下一镜头作为match cut。
声音:副手喊一声"老大!"嗓音焦急愤怒中年女性+背景大漠风声呼啸+背景9条机械腿crash循环略远暗示船在持续逃跑。

【后段 副手match cut转头+走位变身+鸟龙重组+末段low angle hero shot】
机位:开篇匹配剪辑match cut承接前段副手转头方向+相机位置略远+medium-wide shot中宽景+焦段50mm prime lens+rectilinear lens projection+相机随副手走向镜头的过程缓慢dolly后退保持副手在画面前景中央+全程相机有微妙的handheld呼吸感。
末段:相机略下沉到副手腰部以下高度+变成low up-angle仰拍+焦段缩短到35mm slight wide-angle强化威胁感+不完全固定。

开篇阶段(约前2秒):
@图4 副手合体形态从画面深处某位置向相机方向走来+步幅稳定+步速中等+不快不慢+像钢铁侠walk and transform那种英雄步态+副手脸部从前段的焦急愤怒过渡到坚定怒视。变身开始:副手身上的#7A6845铜青绿外骨骼装甲开始一片片机关式弹开+先从肩部和胸部装甲片解锁飞离+#7A6845铜青绿装甲片与#846035暗金装饰片在副手身后空中悬浮。

中段阶段(约中3秒):副手继续向相机方向走来+步幅持续+变身完全展开:副手腰部+腿部+鸟头机械头盔依次解锁飞离+所有解体的装甲片在副手身后空中按几何路径飞行汇集重组+机械重组过程中咔哒咔哒机关声不断+#4FC3D9青色电弧机械眼仍在飞行的装甲片与机械头盔上发光。
副手本体显露(同步进行):副手身体随装甲解体逐渐露出@图3 本体形态:女性中年+脸部刀疤+#1A1610漆黑长发辫子+#6B2419沙猎血红部落红丝带头饰发饰+#4A2820副手部落深棕皮革赤膊背心+#B8A082米色织物围巾+双手#2A1F18棕黑过渡色部落纹身+左大腿与左小腿是#7A6845铜青绿青铜机改义肢机械腿+#C49B5C沙脊赭金夕阳光下泛皮肤金光+不持任何武器+表情逐渐从合体时的焦急愤怒转为本体定型的隐隐怒视。
鸟龙重组(在副手身后空中):解体的装甲片继续在副手身后空中飞行重组+按@图5 鸟龙独立形态结构精准重组+四肢机械腿+长尾巴+长喙鸟头+羽毛装甲展开+#4FC3D9前额单只青色发光机械眼+整个机械鸟龙在副手身后约2米位置在空中逐渐成形。

末段阶段(约末1秒):副手@图3 走到画面前景中央停下+相机位置已下沉到副手腰部以下+仰拍副手+画面里副手腰部以上的half-body半身入画+副手居高临下俯视画面前景方向某处(看镜头方向)+脸部表情是隐隐的愤怒+眉头紧皱+下颌紧+嘴角微抿+#1A1610漆黑长发辫子与#6B2419沙猎血红丝带头饰在风中略微飘动。
鸟龙落地完成:副手身后约2米位置@图5 机械鸟龙四爪足同时落地完成+鸟龙的鸟头超出副手肩膀高度可见+#4FC3D9鸟头青色发光机械眼锁定方向与副手目光大致一致+副手与鸟龙形成双主体并存的英雄定姿构图+鸟龙落地一瞬间机械锁定声咔哒。
构图:副手居于画面正中央前景+鸟龙在副手身后画面中景某一侧+背景是#C8581F落日橙天空+整体构图low angle hero shot+副手居高临下威胁性+经典反派英雄登场画面+视觉对标钢铁侠Mark 50装甲解体+复仇者大厦顶楼边走边变身。

声音:副手走路脚步声循环+装甲解体过程的机械机关弹开声+装甲片在空中飞行的咔哒声层+鸟龙重组过程的金属重组声+鸟龙四爪足落地咔哒+末段定姿瞬间短暂寂静beat+背景持续大漠风声+背景9条机械腿crash远景+末段背景隐约一声机械鸟龙低沉机械嘶吼宣示存在。

【声音设计 全段统一】
全段8秒2镜头依次:
前段:副手喊一声"老大!"焦急愤怒中年女性嗓音+背景大漠风声+背景9条机械腿crash远景。
后段:副手走路脚步+装甲机关弹开声依次+装甲片飞行咔哒层+鸟龙重组金属声+鸟龙四爪足落地咔哒+末段短暂寂静beat+背景大漠风声+背景9条机械腿crash+末段鸟龙低沉机械嘶吼。
全程:仅生成动作音效与对白音效与环境音效+不要bgm+不要抒情背景音乐+sound design only ambient and action sounds NO background music+副手喊话调性是中年女性焦急愤怒。

【关键约束】
色卡锚定铁律:所有色彩严格按@图1 色卡13色执行+不可偏离色板。

镜头硬切铁律:本段8秒由Seedance在内部处理2个镜头之间的硬切hard cut+前段medium close-up副手喊话+后段medium-wide走位变身+绝对禁止镜头过渡或运动连接+但前段末尾副手转头与后段开篇副手已开始走可以做match cut方向延续。

变身机制铁律:后段的变身是副手合体形态的外骨骼装甲一片片机关式弹开飞离副手身体+在副手身后空中重组成独立鸟龙+副手身体逐渐显露本体形态+绝对禁止变身没发生+绝对禁止装甲只是简单脱落到地面+绝对禁止鸟龙凭空出现+变身的过程必须可见装甲片在空中飞行重组的视觉过程+变身节奏:前2秒装甲开始解体+中3秒装甲完全解体并空中重组+末1秒鸟龙四爪足落地完成定姿。

副手两形态铁律:副手有两个形态+合体形态严格按@图4 渲染(前段+后段开篇)+本体形态严格按@图3 渲染(后段变身后期与末段定姿)+绝对禁止两个形态混合+绝对禁止副手在末段还穿着外骨骼+本体形态左大腿与左小腿必须是青铜机改义肢机械腿+其余身体是肉身+本体形态不持任何武器。

low angle hero shot铁律:后段末段相机位置必须低于副手腰部高度+仰拍副手+画面里副手只有腰部以上的half-body入画+副手居高临下俯视镜头+表情隐隐愤怒+这是Marvel英雄定姿hero shot的标志性low angle构图+绝对禁止平视+绝对禁止俯拍。

船腿速度铁律:整段8秒里主角船的9条机械腿持续保持全速独立galloping状态+每秒3-4步高速节奏+船底踏起大量沙尘plume+暗示主角船仍在持续高速逃跑追逐戏中+但本段画面焦点是副手不是船+所以船的画面元素仅通过音轨与微妙甲板颠簸维持+不需要在画面里直接展示船腿。

主体身份铁律:副手合体形态严格按@图4 渲染+副手本体形态严格按@图3 渲染+独立鸟龙严格按@图5 渲染四条机械腿+船尾甲板严格按@图6 渲染。

风格铁律:35mm film stock photography texture+analog film grain+photochemical look+NOT 3D rendered+NOT CGI+NOT video game graphics+NOT Unreal Engine render+NOT Blender render+NOT clean digital surface+NOT plastic look+NOT storyboard sketch style+NOT pencil drawing aesthetic+NOT hand-drawn animation+all surfaces must show real physical wear and weathering+film print quality not digital cinema clean。

NOT slow motion+NOT speed ramping+NOT静态镜头+NOT完全固定相机+NOT变身没发生+NOT副手保持合体形态到末段+NOT两个形态混合+NOT鸟龙凭空出现+NOT船腿缓慢移动+NOT船静止+NOT 2个镜头之间使用过渡(必须硬切)+NOT末段平视或俯拍(必须low angle仰拍)+NOT副手本体持武器+NOT cyberpunk neon+NOT clear blue sky+NOT modern vehicles+NOT modern weapons+NOT cartoon Q版+NOT illustrated+NOT chibi+NOT exaggerated cute deformation+无字幕+无水印。

Photorealistic film still aesthetic + real physical scene captured through a camera lens + not a 3D render.

【机位与构图】
机位:相机位于飞船甲板船头方向+略低于江流站立高度+从船头方向反向拍向船尾方向。江流面朝相机正向站立。船舵在江流身前+处于画面前景下方约画面下三分之一区域+大铜青绿色船舵的上半部分入画+江流双手扳动船舵+船舵的轮辐铜符号在前景特写。江流上半身从船舵后方升起+占画面中景中央+屏脸面具完整入画。背景是飞船船尾方向的甲板建筑结构+布幡飘动+铜钟悬挂+船尾雕花门可见。

机位倾斜:Dutch angle荷兰角倾斜镜头+相机绕镜头光轴向某一侧倾斜约12-15度+整个画面框架呈倾斜状态+水平线倾斜+垂直线倾斜+加重追逐戏的不安感。

光线:#F5C870金色夕阳从画面右侧低角度侧光打在喽啰脸上+喽啰右脸被金边镶光+左脸#1A1610阴影+空气中尘土飞扬+#F5C870光柱条纹+atmospheric haze。

【开篇阶段】
@图2 里的老喽啰站立在@图3 同款沙艇的木质甲板上+双脚分开稳稳扎住沙艇甲板+双手握住一根铜色钩索的绳尾+绳尾在双手中+钩索头是带倒钩的金属爪+整根钩索绳长约2米+喽啰用手腕的力量持续做水平圆周旋转动作+让带倒钩的铜色钩索头在喽啰头顶上方做水平圆周旋转+像美国西部牛仔扔套索lasso前那样持续旋转积蓄力量+钩索头每秒转一圈半的速度持续转动+破风声whoosh whoosh whoosh不断+喽啰双眼专注盯紧画面右侧外的目标方向+面部表情是冷酷专注的捕猎专注神态+#6B2419沙猎血红与#B8A082米色织物围巾在风中rip-flap飘动+背挂的复合弓和箭袋随身体微微晃动+喽啰的#2D2A2A粗糙骨片机械义肢在夕阳下泛冷光。

背景:沙艇下方甲板+沙艇向画面右侧高速行进+背景#8E5A2A大漠土橘色地面从画面右侧向画面左侧高速反向流动+远处地平线#3A4258暮蓝带+#C8581F落日橙夕阳天空+背景隐约可见远处主角飞船的轮廓在画面右侧前方狂奔+其他沙艇队友在画面左右两侧的远景中也在追击。

【中段阶段】
画外右后方位置+从遥远的距离传来一个低沉但充满威胁感的男声怒吼:"滚开!"——这个吼声的位置不在画面里+而是来自画面右后方的远处+从遥远位置以muffled低音渐近到画面前景中等清晰+震得空气都微微颤动+喽啰头顶的钩索仍在惯性继续旋转。

【末段阶段】
喽啰猛地用脖子带动头部朝画外右后方吼声方向急速转头+整个头部从面朝右侧外的捕猎专注状态+猛地转向画面右后方追溯吼声来源+黑色长辫子甩动+面部表情从冷酷捕猎瞬间变成困惑加警觉:眉头皱起+独眼瞪大+嘴角紧绷+一脸"哪儿来的怪声"的表情。

但喽啰的双手没有立刻停止旋转钩索的动作+手腕力道因为分神略松+钩索头在头顶继续做水平圆周旋转但旋转节奏失稳+钩索摆动幅度变大+绳子有点抖+破风声从均匀的whoosh whoosh whoosh变成紊乱的wobble whoosh+给观众一种"这个老喽啰被打断了节奏"的紧张感。

【声音设计】
开篇:沙艇引擎呼啸的低频drone+9条机械腿远景持续crunchy落沙脚步循环背景+钩索头顶旋转破风声whoosh whoosh whoosh匀速节奏+喽啰自身的喘气声短促有力+背景风声呼啸+其他沙艇队友远景的部落式嘶吼remote。

中段:画外右后方位置传来一声低沉沙哑男声怒吼"滚开!"+这个吼声从遥远距离muffled低音渐近到中等清晰+怒吼带着压迫感和威胁感+像Mad Max里的部落首领台词调性+震得空气微颤+remainder of soundscape持续。

末段:喽啰猛地转头瞬间+脖子转动布料摩擦声+辫子甩动声+钩索旋转节奏从均匀whoosh变成紊乱wobble whoosh+绳子抖动声+喽啰自身轻微"啊?"般的疑惑短哼。

全程:仅生成动作音效与对白音效与环境音效+不要bgm+不要抒情背景音乐+sound design only ambient and action sounds NO background music。

【关键约束】
机位铁律:全程medium close-up固定机位+喽啰上半身入画+无变焦+无dolly+无环绕+只有handheld微抖。

钩索铁律:钩索全程做水平头顶圆周旋转like a Western cowboy lasso+开篇中段匀速旋转+末段因喽啰分神惯性继续旋转但节奏失稳幅度变大+钩索全程不释放+不接触喽啰身体+不缠绕+不掉落。

转头铁律:喽啰开篇全程面朝画面右侧外的捕猎方向+只在末段听到吼声后猛地朝画面右后方转头+不是缓慢转+不是抬头+不是低头+是颈部主导的快速侧向转头。

吼声位置铁律:画外吼声位置在画面右后方远处+不在画面里+不显示吼声来源+只通过音效暗示+喽啰转头方向暗示音源方位。

风格铁律:35mm film stock photography texture+analog film grain+photochemical look+NOT 3D rendered+NOT CGI+NOT video game graphics+NOT Unreal Engine render+NOT Blender render+NOT clean digital surface+NOT plastic look+all surfaces must show real physical wear and weathering。

NOT slow motion+NOT speed ramping+实时正常速度+NOT静态镜头+NOT cyberpunk neon+NOT clear blue sky+NOT cartoon+NOT illustrated+NOT chibi+NOT exaggerated cute deformation+无字幕+无水印+NOT显示画外吼声来源人物+NOT喽啰立刻停止钩索旋转。


### 第33条｜追逐戏中段的整体格局:主角飞船在大漠上以全速狂奔状态向画面深处方向逃跑+多艘

Shot on 35mm Kodak Vision3 500T film stock + analog film grain visible + practical light only + Christopher Doyle cinematography + Wong Kar-wai painterly desaturation + photochemical film texture + slight halation on highlights + organic film noise + heavy atmospheric haze + dust particles in light beams + tactile real-world materials + lived-in worn surfaces + film print look not digital cinema look + photorealistic film still aesthetic + real physical scene captured through a camera lens + not a 3D render.

Marvel Cinematic Universe Falcon dive entrance aesthetics + Mad Max: Fury Road kinetic chase + Top Gun Maverick aerial dive cinematography + JOJO replica deadpan villain reveal + golden hour lighting + 16:9 anamorphic.
@图1 作为主角江流的视觉锚定+屏脸面具/青色眼睛屏幕/暗酒红外袍背心/米色泡泡袖衬衣/深棕皮革斜挎包/颈部机械义体严格按此图渲染+江流头发#2A1F18棕黑过渡色不是纯黑。
@图2 作为飞船甲板的视觉锚定+船体所有结构/9条机械腿/木质质感/铜青绿色调/船尾建筑/桅杆布幡严格按此图渲染。
@图3 作为副手(沙猎部落副首领+女性人鸟合体外骨骼)的视觉锚定+全身青铜机改装甲/鸟头机械头盔/前额单只青色发光机械眼/背部展开机械翅膀/部落血红与紫红织物/赤褐羽毛/爪足严格按此图渲染。
@图6 作为追击沙艇的视觉锚定+木质沙艇结构/铜色机械底盘/破旧布幡+沙艇上喽啰按段1段3已建立的沙猎部落造型严格按此图。
@图4 作为整段画面色卡锚定+所有色彩严格按色卡13色执行+不可偏离色板。
@图5 作为整段构图与镜头空间关系的故事板锚定+5个镜头依次按故事板的5个panel构图执行+但故事板仅是构图参考绝对不参考其铅笔素描美学+最终视频是写实摄影质感不是storyboard sketch。

整段15秒一个连续视频+内含Seedance自行处理的5个镜头硬切+按故事板的5个panel顺序执行。

【场景物理设置】
追逐戏中段的整体格局:主角飞船在大漠上以全速狂奔状态向画面深处方向逃跑+多艘沙猎沙艇在主角船后方紧追不舍+副手从平流层向追逐场景下方俯冲降下+所有车辆与生物的运动方向严格一致都朝向画面深处。
速度铁律:主角飞船的9条机械腿正在全速独立踏地+每条腿独立galloping节奏不同步+每秒3-4步的高速节奏+船身随9腿步频微颠+船底踏起大量沙尘plume+绝对不能渲染成船腿缓慢移动+绝对不能渲染成船静止+船的速度感必须明显与"高速逃跑追逐戏"匹配+船底沙尘扬起的高度必须达到船腹中部以上才显示出狂奔速度。
追逐顺序:主角船在最前方逃跑+多艘沙艇在主角船后方追击+副手从天空俯冲是新加入的威胁。

【镜头1 Wide Aerial 平流层establishing+鸟啸先声夺人】
机位:extreme wide aerial shot超广角航拍+24mm wide-angle+轻微桶形畸变可接受+相机位于平流层云层视角。
画面:画面上半部分是#C8B8A8平流层云灰云层+被#F5C870金光从侧面照亮+云层之间露出#6A7B8E平流层高空蓝。画面下半部分是远景大漠地表+#8E5A2A大漠土橘+几条沙丘曲线+地平线在画面下三分之一+地平线上方是#C8581F落日橙天空。
追逐场景在远景大漠的某处:
@图2 主角飞船以全速狂奔状态+9条机械腿独立踏地+船底大量沙尘plume扬起+船在画面里只是一个小尺寸但9腿狂奔的姿态可见+船朝向画面深处逃跑。在主角船后方约几个船身距离+3艘@图6 沙艇在追击+沙艇底部铜机械结构滑过沙地+沙艇尾部扬起沙尘+沙艇全部朝向画面深处与船同向。整个追逐队列从俯瞰视角看是船在前+沙艇在后+全部高速向画面深处方向移动+留下平行的沙尘轨迹。
副手:在画面上半部分某个位置出现一个极小的剪影+
@图3 副手翼展张开但因为距离极远只是一个小silhouette+体积约画面的1/40大小+副手开始向画面深处的下方加速降落。
音轨:一声极尖锐高亢的机械鸟啸先声夺人+remixed from peregrine falcon scream + metallic resonance+穿云破风声+背景大漠环境音+背景9条机械腿快速crash脚步循环。

【镜头2 OTS Dive Tracking 越肩俯冲】
机位:over-the-shoulder shot越肩视角+相机位于副手身后约1米+50mm prime lens+rectilinear lens projection+no barrel distortion。
画面:前景两侧画面边缘是@图3 副手的肩膀与机械翅膀edge silhouette+前景肩膀虚焦+中景与远景是大漠地表从俯冲视角快速逼近相机+大漠表面有强烈的perspective lines朝向画面下方vanishing point。
在画面深景的中下方:
@图2 主角飞船作为俯冲目标+在画面里占约1/8面积+船头朝画面深处方向+9条机械腿全速独立踏地+每条腿独立galloping节奏不同步+船底大量沙尘plume扬起到船腹高度+船在自身全速逃跑状态。3艘@图6 沙艇紧跟在主角船后方+沙艇排列在船后形成松散追击阵型+沙艇全部朝向画面深处与船同向+沙艇尾部扬起的沙尘形成多条平行轨迹。
副手:画面里仅显示前景肩膀+翅膀edge+背部+不显示脸+副手身体俯冲姿势+翅膀部分展开。
音轨:破风声从尖锐到撕裂wind tear+副手喉咙发出战吼war cry+背景9条机械腿持续快速crash脚步循环+背景3艘沙艇引擎呼啸的低频drone叠加。

【镜头3 Reverse Low Angle 反打仰拍翅膀展开】
机位:reverse shot反打+相机位于副手前下方+35mm slight wide-angle+轻微桶形畸变作为表现手法+仰拍向上+俯仰角约-20度。
画面:
@图3 副手从画面上方朝相机方向急速俯冲+副手身体在画面中央偏上+面朝相机+鸟头机械头盔可见+#4FC3D9青色电弧机械眼锁定相机+副手身上的#7A6845铜青绿装甲与#846035暗金装饰可见+#6B2419沙猎血红与#4A2030暗紫红织物在风中violently翻飞+背部机械翅膀从俯冲收缩状态向两侧#6B4A2E赭红羽毛+#2E1F15深褐羽尖快速展开deploy。
背景大漠地表占画面下方约1/4:背景里可见远景@图2 主角飞船小尺寸+9条机械腿狂奔+船底扬起沙尘+3艘@图6 沙艇紧跟船后追击+全部小尺寸但持续运动状态可见。
副手:从镜头2的俯冲姿态过渡到翅膀展开滑翔姿态+翅膀从背部向两侧伸展+羽毛从折叠状态展开为完整翼面+整个动作是收缩→展开的可见过程。
音轨:破风声延续+翅膀展开的金属机关弹开声wing deploy + metallic clack+羽毛在空气中的rustle声+副手长啸延续+背景持续9条机械腿crash与沙艇引擎呼啸。

【镜头4 反打主角扎进储物箱】
机位:medium shot+相机位于船甲板侧面+50mm prime lens+rectilinear lens projection+水平视线human eye level。
画面:
@图2 主角船甲板上+一个大型青铜储物箱在画面中央偏下+箱盖完全打开+
@图1 江流的整个上半身(头/胸/双臂)完全没入箱内消失+只露出他的下半身:腰部以下/屁股/双腿在画面里+腿在空中乱蹬乱踢flailing+#4A1F1F深酒红外袍后摆+#2D2A28深灰金属颈部机械义体的下半部分可见+江流的姿势是头朝下倒栽进箱子+整个上半身扎得太深拔不出来。
箱子周围地面散落着各种古代物品:小青铜杯/卷轴/小皮袋/小铜片/小绳团等geometric scattered items+全部是被江流翻腾扔出来的+#7A6845机改铜青绿与#846035暗金物品。
背景:甲板上方部分船尾建筑结构与桅杆可见+布幡飘动+整个甲板因船腿全速狂奔而微微颠簸+暗示船在持续高速逃跑状态。
喜剧效果:江流的腿在箱口外乱踢的动作明显非常滑稽+像cartoon character翻得太深的画面+和俯冲段副手的潇洒形成强烈对比。
音轨:储物箱内江流碰撞箱壁的木质砰砰声chest banging+物品散落金属与木头哐当声+江流闷在箱内的喘气与"嗯啊"等闷哼声(声音被箱体闷住模糊)+背景副手俯冲的尖啸声从远处渐近暗示副手即将到达+背景9条机械腿持续crash与沙艇引擎呼啸。

【镜头5 副手潇洒落地船尾】
机位:medium-wide shot中宽景+相机位于船尾甲板侧面+35mm slight wide-angle+rectilinear lens projection+水平视线接近船尾甲板高度。
画面:
@图2 主角船的船尾区域作为构图基础+船尾建筑结构在画面右侧middle-distance+一根桅杆与三角船帆在画面右侧上方vertical+船尾建筑drawn as stepped rectangular structure+甲板木质地板可见+整个甲板因船腿全速狂奔而持续微微颠簸+暗示船在持续高速逃跑状态。
@图3 副手刚刚落地在画面中央前景的甲板上+猎鹰式半跪姿势:双爪足先着地接触甲板+一手撑地+一膝跪地+另一手手肘弯曲向后+背部机械翅膀完全展开后立刻向背后半折叠收回half-fold+羽毛收拢的过程可见+副手周围地面扬起一圈dust burst尘土爆炸+#D9A55C琥珀沙雾环绕在副手腰部高度+落地瞬间冲击力可见。副手的鸟头机械头盔抬起+#4FC3D9青色电弧机械眼朝向画面左方向(船头方向+主角所在方位)锁定目标。

【声音设计 全段统一】
全段5镜头的音效整体设计依次:
镜头1:平流层风声+一声尖锐高亢机械鸟啸"AAAA-KEEEE"穿云破风声+背景大漠环境音+背景9条机械腿crash快速循环+背景沙艇引擎drone。
镜头2:破风wind tear声+副手战吼war cry+背景9条机械腿持续快速crash+背景3艘沙艇引擎呼啸drone。
镜头3:翅膀展开金属机关弹开声+羽毛rustle声+副手长啸延续+背景9条机械腿与沙艇引擎持续。
镜头4:储物箱内闷碰撞声chest banging+物品散落哐当声+江流闷哼+背景副手尖啸渐近+背景9条机械腿与沙艇引擎持续。
镜头5:翅膀减速空气声+双足落地crash+翅膀收拢咔哒+短暂静默+背景大漠风声+背景9条机械腿持续+背景江流箱内扑腾。
全程:仅生成动作音效与对白音效与环境音效+不要bgm+不要抒情背景音乐+sound design only ambient and action sounds NO background music+副手鸟啸是猎隼peregrine falcon scream + metallic resonance混合+副手war cry是低沉成熟女性威胁感+江流闷哼是被箱体闷住模糊的微弱声音。

【关键约束】
故事板锚定铁律:本段5个镜头的构图与空间关系严格依据@图5 故事板的5个panel执行+但故事板仅作为构图与空间关系参考+绝对不参考故事板的铅笔素描美学+最终视频是35mm film stock胶片写实摄影质感+绝对不是storyboard sketch+绝对不是pencil drawing+绝对不是hand-drawn animation。

镜头硬切铁律:本段15秒由Seedance在内部处理5个镜头之间的硬切hard cut+5个镜头各自独立机位与构图+按故事板顺序依次切换+绝对禁止镜头过渡或镜头运动连接。

船腿速度铁律:主角飞船的9条机械腿在所有镜头中(镜头1/2/3/4/5)持续保持全速独立galloping状态+每条腿独立踏地+每秒3-4步的高速节奏+不同步+船底踏起大量沙尘plume扬起到船腹中部以上+船底沙尘必须明显可见+绝对禁止船腿缓慢移动+绝对禁止船腿同步行走+绝对禁止船静止+绝对禁止船的逃跑速度感弱化+主角船在所有镜头里都必须读出"全速逃跑追逐"的速度感。

沙艇追逐铁律:在镜头1/2/3里3艘沙艇必须明显出现并紧跟在主角船后方+沙艇朝向画面深处与船同向+沙艇尾部扬起沙尘形成平行轨迹+沙艇与船保持几个船身距离的追逐间距+绝对禁止沙艇消失+绝对禁止沙艇与船对冲+绝对禁止沙艇超过主角船。

运动方向铁律:副手从平流层向地面方向俯冲+从远到近从高到低+绝对禁止副手对冲飞行+绝对禁止副手转向上升+绝对禁止副手往镜头外飞走。主角船与3艘沙艇在所有镜头中持续向画面深处全速狂奔+船头朝画面深处+船尾朝相机或画面前景+绝对禁止船与沙艇面对副手或彼此面对。

主体身份铁律:副手严格按@图3 渲染+江流严格按@图1 渲染+江流头发#2A1F18棕黑过渡色不是纯黑+主角船严格按@图2 渲染9条机械腿+沙艇严格按@图6 渲染。

风格铁律:35mm film stock photography texture+analog film grain+photochemical look+NOT 3D rendered+NOT CGI+NOT video game graphics+NOT Unreal Engine render+NOT Blender render+NOT clean digital surface+NOT plastic look+NOT storyboard sketch style+NOT pencil drawing aesthetic+NOT hand-drawn animation+all surfaces must show real physical wear and weathering+film print quality not digital cinema clean。

NOT slow motion+NOT speed ramping+实时正常速度+NOT静态镜头+NOT副手转向上升+NOT副手对冲飞行+NOT副手往镜头外飞走+NOT主角船面对副手+NOT沙艇消失+NOT沙艇超过主角船+NOT船腿缓慢移动+NOT船腿同步行走+NOT船静止+NOT 5个镜头之间使用过渡(必须硬切hard cut)+NOT镜头4里江流上半身可见(必须只露下半身)+NOT cyberpunk neon+NOT clear blue sky+NOT modern vehicles+NOT cartoon Q版+NOT illustrated+NOT chibi+NOT exaggerated cute deformation+无字幕+无水印。


### 第34条｜万物生第1集 沙猎喽啰C · 弓箭手 默认形态四视图人设图 v1

**万物生第1集 沙猎喽啰C · 弓箭手 默认形态四视图人设图 v1**
【格式继承】
完全同头领/女副手/喽啰A/B格式：9:16竖版+4格不等大+正脸特写/侧脸特写/脖子以下/背面+single-side rim backlight。

【角色定位】
沙猎部落普通喽啰之一——**弓箭手 Archer**。功能：远程支援+追踪+战术情报。在战术单位里负责"远程压制"环节，配合A钩索手锁定+B沙艇驾驶手围困。3个喽啰中最年长最老练。

【3喽啰体型/年龄差异化总结】
- A · 钩索手：30岁/stocky chunky working laborer
- B · 沙艇驾驶手：25岁/slender athletic pilot
- **C · 弓箭手：35岁/lean tall veteran archer**（本角色）

构成"年轻精瘦 / 中年壮实 / 老练高瘦"三种体型谱+年龄谱。

---

A character design reference sheet. **Vertical 9:16 aspect ratio. Maximum resolution output.** Pure solid neutral light-warm-grey studio backdrop. Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45 degrees behind the subject, no fill light, no front light, no other light sources—dramatic cinematic studio lookbook lighting.

【Layout】

**2x2 grid with unequal panel heights**:
- Top row: two smaller panels (each approximately 1/3 of total canvas height) — head/face close-ups
- Bottom row: two larger panels (each approximately 2/3 of total canvas height) — full body shots
- Thin neutral light-grey dividing lines between all four panels
- All four panels share the same solid backdrop and same lighting setup

【4 Panels】

**Top-left panel — FRONT FACE CLOSE-UP**:
Tight close-up of the male archer's face, framed from approximately collarbone-line up to top of head. Subject facing camera directly. Side-rim backlight catches the right edge of the face/hair (camera's left side of frame), leaving the left side of the face in deeper shadow with subtle ambient bounce. Both eyes intact, calm focused veteran archer's gaze toward camera. **Tribal headwrap is currently pulled down loose around the neck** revealing his face fully (in field operations the headwrap covers his nose/mouth for sand protection, but in this character sheet stance the wrap is down to show the face).

**Top-right panel — SIDE FACE CLOSE-UP (PROFILE)**:
Tight close-up of his face in pure side profile, framed from collarbone-line up to top of head. Subject facing camera-right. Side-rim backlight catches the back of the head and silhouettes the profile against the backdrop. **Long black hair gathered into a single braid down the back/side** visible falling along the spine. **Long groomed black beard** (longer and more cultivated than raider A's stubble) catches the rim light. Face fully visible.

**Bottom-left panel — FULL BODY FROM NECK DOWN, FRONT VIEW (NO FACE)**:
Full body shot from collarbone-line down to feet, facing camera directly. **The face/head is intentionally cropped out of frame at the top of this panel—only the neck, shoulders, torso, arms, hips, legs, and feet are visible.** Zero face involvement so this panel survives any future face replacement edit. Side-rim backlight catches the right shoulder and right side of body, defining lean tall silhouette. **Left forearm vambrace with mechanical sighting device prominently visible**, dark metallic claw-cap fingertips on right hand visible.

**Bottom-right panel — FULL BODY BACK VIEW (INCLUDING BACK OF HEAD)**:
Full body shot from head to feet, facing directly away from camera (back of subject toward camera). **Includes back of head with the long black braid visible falling down to mid-back length**, headwrap loose around neck/shoulders. **Long composite recurve bow strapped diagonally across the back fully visible** (signature equipment), large arrow quiver attached at hip with feathered arrow ends visible. Sparse trophy decorations on back of belt visible. Side-rim backlight catches right shoulder silhouette.

【Subject — same character across all 4 panels】

A male desert raider, common-rank tribe member specializing in long-range archery and tracking for hunting parties. Most experienced of the tactical unit.

**Character Core**:
Approximately 33-37 years old, weathered seasoned working veteran. **Lean tall slim build—long limbs, narrow shoulders relative to height, the body type of an experienced archer who values reach and precision over raw strength. NOT bulky muscular, NOT chunky, NOT slender frail, NOT teenage gangly. Built like a desert hunter who has tracked and killed at distance for fifteen years.**

**ETHNICITY — CRITICAL**: Central Asian / Turkic / Sogdian Silk Road ethnic features—the historically accurate "杂胡" mixed-tribe peoples of the Sui-Tang frontier desert. Wide prominent cheekbones, deep-set almond-shaped eyes with monolid or low-fold (NOT round Western double-fold), black or very dark brown eyes (calm steady focused archer's gaze), black hair, weathered tan-bronze skin from years of desert sun exposure with deep sun lines around the eyes, strong jawline, straight or slightly aquiline nose (the Sogdian merchant Silk Road profile), thick natural eyebrows.

**Hair**: long black hair gathered tightly back into a single thick braid running down the back/side, falling to mid-back length. Some hair pulled back smooth across the scalp before tying. Possibly a small leather cord or fragment of bone tied at the end of the braid.

**Beard**: long groomed black beard reaching to mid-chest, sometimes braided in a single segment at the chin. Cultivated and intentional grooming (not the chieftain's wild stubble or raider A's short stubble)—this is a man with the patience to groom himself, the same patience that makes him an archer.

**Battle marks**: a thin diagonal scar across the left cheekbone, deep crow's feet around both eyes from years of squinting at distant targets. Weathered skin with sun damage texture.

**Headwear (CRITICAL)**: tribal headwrap of weathered tan-and-rust desert cloth, currently pulled down loose around the neck/shoulders (face fully uncovered for character sheet purposes). The wrap is the type that can be pulled up to cover the lower face/nose for sandstorm/wind protection during operations.

**Upper body**: weathered dark leather long-sleeved tunic with reinforced **left forearm vambrace** (the bow-arm side, protecting from bowstring slap and serving as armor). 

**LEFT forearm vambrace - mechanical sighting mechanism (CRITICAL signature equipment)**: the vambrace incorporates a small bronze mechanical sighting device—a folding bronze frame with small lens and adjustable sight markers, designed to mount on the bow arm during draw. War-torn brass-and-leather construction with visible bolts, leather strapping, slight verdigris patina (Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845, Aged Gold #846035 accent). Same patina as the rest of the tribe's equipment. NOT a prosthetic limb replacement—the underlying arm is intact, this is wearable equipment armor with mechanism.

**RIGHT hand - metallic finger-cap claws**: each fingertip on the right hand (the draw hand) is capped with a small dark bronze metal claw-cap—originally functional protection for the bowstring-drawing fingers, doubles as scratching/clawing close-combat tool when bow is unavailable. Subtle dark verdigris patina.

**Lower body**: form-fitting weathered dark leather trousers tucked into knee-high desert boots wrapped with cloth bindings and reinforced inner-leg panels (rider/horseman wear). Multiple small belt pouches (extra arrow tips, bowstring spares, repair tools).

**Trophy decorations**: SPARSE BUT REFINED — a few carefully chosen small bone trophies, a polished arrow tip from a defeated enemy archer, one small carved bead. Less quantity than the lieutenant's refined woven beads but more cultivated/chosen than raider A's pure utilitarian sparseness. He picks his trophies carefully—the marks of an archer's discriminating taste.

**Footwear**: knee-high desert leather boots with cloth bindings, reinforced soles for steady archer's stance.

【Weapons】
- **Primary**: long composite recurve bow (full hunting size, horn and sinew construction, dark wood and bone color, longer than the lieutenant's quick-draw short bow) strapped diagonally across the back. The signature weapon of his role.
- **Quiver**: large arrow quiver attached at right hip or across back, with 15-20 arrows, feathered ends visible
- **Sidearm**: short combat knife at left waist belt for close-quarters

【Color Palette】
Desert sand tan, deep burnt umber leather, aged bronze patina (Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845 + Aged Gold #846035) on the forearm sighting mechanism and finger claws, dried-blood-red tribal accent on cloth bindings. ALL materials weathered war-torn dust-coated, NOT clean NOT polished.

【Pose - same across all 4 panels】
Default standing pose with feet shoulder-width apart, weight evenly distributed (the steady archer's stance). Left arm slightly raised or at side showing the vambrace mechanism, right hand at side or near the knife showing the finger claw-caps. Subtle calm patient veteran energy—NOT aggressive action pose, NOT relaxed civilian, NOT showy. The body language of an experienced hunter at rest.

【Critical consistency】
SAME individual character in all 4 panels—identical face structure (Central Asian/Turkic/Sogdian features), identical long braided hair, identical long groomed beard, identical clothing items, identical left vambrace with sighting mechanism, identical right finger claw-caps, identical sparse refined trophy decorations. Same studio backdrop, same single-sided side-rim backlighting setup across all 4 panels.

【Lighting - critical】
**Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45-degrees behind subject. NO fill light. NO front light. NO ambient. NO other light sources.** Strong dramatic edge-light catches the right side of subject silhouette in every panel, leaving the left side of subject in deep falloff shadow with subtle ambient bounce.

【Style】
Hyperrealistic photographic character design rendering—shot like a high-end practical costume photographed in a film production studio for a cinematic lookbook reference board. NOT illustration, NOT obvious 3D CGI render.

**Reference aesthetics for Central Asian Silk Road ethnic accuracy**:
- **《长安十二时辰》(The Longest Day in Chang'an, 2019) 西域狼卫** — Sui-Tang Silk Road frontier desert peoples ethnographic anchor
- **Mongol: The Rise of Genghis Khan (2007)** — Central Asian battle-hardened warrior physique and weathered features
- **Monster Hunter Wilds desert hunter aesthetic** — for the tribal armor/weathered-leather DNA

Light Kodak Vision3 35mm film grain texture overlay.

【Quality】
Maximum resolution output. 8k ultra clean rendering. Sharp clean edges. Every leather strap, every arrow feather, every gear/lens on the vambrace sighting mechanism, every finger claw-cap clearly defined.

【Panel Labels】
Small clean monospace sans-serif black text below each panel:
- Top-left: "FRONT FACE"
- Top-right: "SIDE FACE"
- Bottom-left: "BODY FRONT (NO FACE)"
- Bottom-right: "BACK"

Title at top center of canvas (small clean black sans-serif): "SAND HUNTER COMMON RAIDER C · ARCHER — DEFAULT FORM CHARACTER SHEET"

【Constraints】
**Ethnic accuracy (CRITICAL)**: NOT pure Caucasian European face, NOT blonde or red or light brown hair, NOT blue or green or grey or hazel eyes, NOT pale fair Northern European skin, NOT thin pointed delicate Western nose, NOT round Western double-fold eyes, NOT pure Han Chinese face, NOT modern K-pop or Chinese drama actor aesthetic. MUST be Central Asian Turkic/Sogdian Silk Road mixed ethnicity.

**Body, age & framing**: NOT bulky muscular, NOT chunky, NOT slender frail, NOT teenage gangly, NOT young appearance (must be 33-37 weathered veteran with deep sun lines and patient archer demeanor). NOT clean-shaven or stubble (long groomed beard mandatory).

**Equipment differentiation**: NOT a prosthetic limb replacement (no mechanical limbs, only wearable vambrace and finger caps), NOT same equipment as raider A (no lasso winch, no back-mounted hook launcher, no large mechanical chest device), NOT same equipment as raider B (no goggles, no throwing-dart bandolier, no high ponytail), NOT chieftain-level brutalist trophy mass, NOT lieutenant-level woven decoration.

**Aesthetic**: NOT precision cybernetic, NOT modern, NOT clean (war-torn salvage bronze only), NOT medieval European, NOT Ming-Qing imperial, NOT samurai, NOT cyberpunk neon, NOT steampunk Victorian, NOT obvious CGI 3D render, NOT cartoon, NOT anime, NOT fantasy elf-archer aesthetic, NOT colorful saturated palette.

**Layout & lighting**: NOT 1:1 square aspect (must be vertical 9:16), NOT equal-sized 4 panels (top row smaller, bottom row larger), NOT face visible in bottom-left panel (face strictly cropped out at top of panel), NOT face covered by headwrap in face close-up panels (wrap pulled down to neck, face fully visible), NOT colorful background (solid neutral light-warm-grey only), NOT soft even neutral lighting (must be single-side rim backlight only), NOT front light, NOT fill light, NOT any text other than the four panel labels and the title.

【实操注意】
1. 跑出来重点检查：
   - **族群准确**——Central Asian/Turkic/Sogdian。欧美白人脸或纯汉人脸都失败
   - **9:16+4格不等大**+最高分辨率
   - **左下panel不能露脸**
   - **lean tall修长体型**——不是bulky也不是slender frail
   - **长辫+长须**——和A的短发短stubble + B的女性高马尾形成清楚区分
   - **左前臂vambrace机械瞄准器**——signature equipment+在front view body panel可见
   - **右手指尖金属甲套**——distinct细节+在front view body panel可见
   - **背挂长复合弓**——signature equipment+在back view可见
   - **不是fantasy elf archer**（容易跑歪）+不是日式弓道家
   - **战利品sparse but refined**——和A的"pure utilitarian sparse"+B的"slightly more"形成微差

【3个喽啰差异化总览（一致性check）】

| 维度 | A钩索手 | B沙艇驾驶手 | C弓箭手 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 性别 | 男 | 女 | 男 |
| 年龄 | 28-32 | 23-26 | 33-37 |
| 体型 | stocky chunky | lean athletic | lean tall |
| 头发 | 短cropped | 高马尾长 | 长辫至mid-back |
| 胡子 | short stubble | 无 | 长groomed beard |
| 服装 | sleeveless vest | cropped vest | long-sleeved tunic |
| Signature equipment 1 | 右前臂lasso winch（叠加） | 风沙护目镜（额头上） | 左前臂vambrace瞄准器 |
| Signature equipment 2 | 背挂hook launcher | 通讯哨子+throwing darts bandolier | 右手指尖metal claw-caps |
| 主武器 | lasso/grappling hook | throwing darts + javelin | long composite bow |
| 副武器 | 单短刀 | 短矛+短刀 | 短刀 |
| 战利品挂饰量 | 2-3 sparse utilitarian | 3-4 sparse + small bead | sparse but refined chosen |
| 角色气质 | working laborer | alert pilot | calm patient veteran |


### 第35条｜万物生第1集 沙猎喽啰A · 钩索手 默认形态四视图人设图 v1

**万物生第1集 沙猎喽啰A · 钩索手 默认形态四视图人设图 v1**
【格式继承】
完全同头领/女副手格式：
- 9:16竖版+最高分辨率
- 4宫格不等大（上小下大）
- 左上正脸特写+右上侧脸特写+左下脖子以下全身（无脸）+右下背面全身含后脑勺
- 单侧侧逆光+无其他光源+定妆照感
- Central Asian/Turkic/Sogdian Silk Road族群锚定（继承女副手的反欧美脸纠正）

【角色定位】
沙猎部落普通喽啰之一——**钩索手 Lasso**。功能：发射钩索锁定目标（江流飞船的bug-like腿是猎物结构弱点）。在战术单位里负责"锁定"环节，配合B沙艇驾驶手围困+C弓箭手远程支援。

装备等级低于头领/女副手——**没有义肢替换+只有局部叠加机改**（wearable mechanism而非bodily replacement）。战利品挂饰少（新一代+战绩未积累）。

A character design reference sheet. **Vertical 9:16 aspect ratio. Maximum resolution output.** Pure solid neutral light-warm-grey studio backdrop. Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45 degrees behind the subject, no fill light, no front light, no other light sources—dramatic cinematic studio lookbook lighting.

【Layout】

**2x2 grid with unequal panel heights**:
- Top row: two smaller panels (each approximately 1/3 of total canvas height) — head/face close-ups
- Bottom row: two larger panels (each approximately 2/3 of total canvas height) — full body shots
- Thin neutral light-grey dividing lines between all four panels
- All four panels share the same solid backdrop and same lighting setup

【4 Panels】

**Top-left panel — FRONT FACE CLOSE-UP**:
Tight close-up of the male raider's face, framed from approximately collarbone-line up to top of head. Subject facing camera directly. Side-rim backlight catches the right edge of the face/hair (camera's left side of frame), leaving the left side of the face in deeper shadow with subtle ambient bounce. Both eyes intact, sharp focused gaze toward camera. Face fully visible—no headwrap covering nose or mouth.

**Top-right panel — SIDE FACE CLOSE-UP (PROFILE)**:
Tight close-up of the raider's face in pure side profile, framed from collarbone-line up to top of head. Subject facing camera-right. Side-rim backlight catches the back of the head and silhouettes the profile against the backdrop. Short cropped hair and stubble beard catch the rim light. Face fully visible.

**Bottom-left panel — FULL BODY FROM NECK DOWN, FRONT VIEW (NO FACE)**:
Full body shot from collarbone-line down to feet, facing camera directly. **The face/head is intentionally cropped out of frame at the top of this panel—only the neck, shoulders, torso, arms, hips, legs, and feet are visible.** Zero face involvement so this panel survives any future face replacement edit. Side-rim backlight catches the right shoulder and right side of body, defining stocky muscular silhouette. **Right forearm clockwork lasso-winch mechanism prominently visible**, large mechanical grappling-hook launcher strapped across upper back/shoulder visible at top edge of frame.

**Bottom-right panel — FULL BODY BACK VIEW (INCLUDING BACK OF HEAD)**:
Full body shot from head to feet, facing directly away from camera (back of subject toward camera). **Includes back of head, short cropped hair visible, headwrap loose around neck.** **Mechanical lasso-winch hook launcher across upper back fully visible** (the signature equipment of his role), thick coiled rope cable running from launcher down to right forearm winch. Trophy decorations on back of belt visible (sparse—2-3 small trophies only). Side-rim backlight catches right shoulder silhouette.

【Subject — same character across all 4 panels】

A male desert raider, common-rank tribe member specializing in lasso/grappling-hook deployment for hunting parties.

**Character Core**:
Approximately 28-32 years old, hardened working-rank warrior. **Stocky chunky muscular build—broad shoulders, thick arms with developed forearms (the kind of grip-and-pull strength needed to operate a heavy mechanical hook launcher). Built like a working laborer-fighter, NOT bulky bodybuilder, NOT obese, NOT slender.**

**ETHNICITY — CRITICAL**: Central Asian / Turkic / Sogdian Silk Road ethnic features—the historically accurate "杂胡" mixed-tribe peoples of the Sui-Tang frontier desert (突厥+粟特+西域本土+汉流亡者混合). Wide prominent cheekbones, deep-set almond-shaped eyes with monolid or low-fold (NOT round Western double-fold), black or very dark brown eyes, black hair, weathered tan-bronze skin from desert sun, strong jawline, straight or slightly broad nose (NOT thin pointed Western Caucasian), thick natural eyebrows.

**Hair**: short cropped black hair, practical working-warrior cut. Short stubble beard, weathered face with sun lines and small windburn marks.

**Battle marks**: a few minor scars on cheeks, knuckles, forearms—the marks of a working raider, NOT the dramatic veteran scars of the chieftain or lieutenant.

**Headwear**: tribal headwrap of weathered tan-and-rust desert cloth, currently pulled down loose around the neck (face fully uncovered). Wrap can extend down as a hood/scarf around shoulders.

**Upper body**: weathered dark leather sleeveless vest, shoulders and forearms exposed showing tribal tattoo markings on shoulders. **CRITICAL EQUIPMENT — Right forearm lasso-winch mechanism**: a wearable bronze clockwork device strapped to the entire right forearm from elbow to wrist (NOT a prosthetic replacement—the underlying arm is intact, this is wearable armor with mechanism). Visible bronze gears, leather strap bindings to the upper arm and wrist, exposed clockwork mechanisms, slight verdigris patina (Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845, Aged Gold #846035 accent). A thick rope cable feeds from a winch drum on the forearm device up over the shoulder to the back-mounted launcher. Left forearm wrapped only with thick leather wrist guards (no mechanism on left side).

**Back-mounted lasso launcher**: a large primitive mechanical grappling-hook launcher strapped horizontally across the upper back/shoulder blades—roughly the size of a small crossbow, made of bronze and dark wood with a heavy iron grappling hook visible at one end, thick rope cable feeding from the launcher to the forearm winch. War-torn salvage construction with bolts and leather strapping.

**Lower body**: loose weathered dark wide-leg leather trousers tucked into knee-high desert boots wrapped with cloth bindings. Multiple belt straps and small pouches around waist (rope spool, hook accessories, small tools).

**Trophy decorations**: SPARSE — only 2-3 small bone fragments and one small enemy belt buckle hanging from leather cords on the belt. He's a young raider with limited combat record (markedly less than the chieftain's brutalist trophy mass or the lieutenant's refined woven beads).

**Footwear**: rugged desert leather boots wrapped with cloth bindings around the calves.

【Secondary Weapon】
- Single short combat knife at left waist belt (sidearm only)—the lasso/winch system is his primary tool, melee is secondary

【Color Palette】
Desert sand tan, deep burnt umber leather, aged bronze patina (Mecha Crimson Bronze #7A6845 + Aged Gold #846035) on the lasso mechanism, dried-blood-red tribal accent on cloth bindings. ALL materials weathered war-torn dust-coated, NOT clean NOT polished.

【Pose - same across all 4 panels】
Default standing pose with feet shoulder-width apart, weight slightly forward (the stance of someone always ready to brace and pull on a tensioned rope). Right hand near the forearm winch mechanism, left hand resting at side. Subtle laborer-warrior solid presence—NOT aggressive action pose, NOT relaxed civilian, NOT showy.

【Critical consistency】
SAME individual character in all 4 panels—identical face structure (Central Asian/Turkic/Sogdian features), identical hair (short cropped + short stubble), identical clothing items, identical right forearm lasso-winch mechanism, identical back-mounted hook launcher, identical sparse trophy decorations. Same studio backdrop, same single-sided side-rim backlighting setup across all 4 panels.

【Lighting - critical】
**Single-sided side-rim backlighting from camera-right at 45-degrees behind subject. NO fill light. NO front light. NO ambient. NO other light sources.** Strong dramatic edge-light catches the right side of subject silhouette in every panel, leaving the left side of subject in deep falloff shadow with subtle ambient bounce. Cinematic high-end fashion-lookbook studio photography (Peter Lindbergh / Annie Leibovitz portrait style with single rim source).

【Style】
Hyperrealistic photographic character design rendering—shot like a high-end practical costume photographed in a film production studio for a cinematic lookbook reference board. NOT illustration, NOT obvious 3D CGI render. 

**Reference aesthetics for Central Asian Silk Road ethnic accuracy**:
- **《长安十二时辰》(The Longest Day in Chang'an, 2019) 西域狼卫** — Sui-Tang Silk Road frontier desert peoples ethnographic anchor
- **Mongol: The Rise of Genghis Khan (2007)** — Central Asian battle-hardened warrior physique and weathered features
- **Monster Hunter Wilds desert hunter aesthetic** — for the tribal armor/weathered-leather/bone-trophy DNA

Light Kodak Vision3 35mm film grain texture overlay.

【Quality】
Maximum resolution output. 8k ultra clean rendering. Sharp clean edges. Every leather strap, every gear tooth, every bolt on the lasso mechanism clearly defined. Real physical costume cinematic photography aesthetic.

【Panel Labels】
Small clean monospace sans-serif black text below each panel:
- Top-left: "FRONT FACE"
- Top-right: "SIDE FACE"
- Bottom-left: "BODY FRONT (NO FACE)"
- Bottom-right: "BACK"

Title at top center of canvas (small clean black sans-serif): "SAND HUNTER COMMON RAIDER A · LASSO — DEFAULT FORM CHARACTER SHEET"

【Constraints】
**Ethnic accuracy (CRITICAL)**: NOT pure Caucasian European face, NOT blonde or red or light brown hair, NOT blue or green or grey or hazel eyes, NOT pale fair Northern European skin, NOT thin pointed delicate Western nose, NOT round Western double-fold eyes, NOT pure Han Chinese face, NOT modern K-pop or Chinese drama actor aesthetic. MUST be Central Asian Turkic/Sogdian Silk Road mixed ethnicity.

**Body & costume**: NOT bodybuilder bulky muscle (working laborer stocky build only), NOT obese, NOT slender, NOT teen-aged appearance (must be 28-32 working raider). Forearm winch is wearable armor mechanism NOT a prosthetic limb replacement.

**Equipment differentiation from leadership**: NOT a prosthetic limb replacement (he has both arms intact, the winch is wearable equipment), NOT heavy quantity of trophy decorations (only 2-3 sparse), NOT heavy multiple weapons (lasso system is primary, single knife is secondary), NOT chieftain-level brutalist trophy mass, NOT lieutenant-level refined woven beads.

**Aesthetic**: NOT precision cybernetic, NOT modern, NOT clean (war-torn salvage bronze only), NOT medieval European, NOT Ming-Qing imperial, NOT samurai, NOT cyberpunk neon, NOT obvious CGI 3D render, NOT cartoon, NOT anime, NOT colorful saturated palette.

**Layout & lighting**: NOT 1:1 square aspect (must be vertical 9:16), NOT equal-sized 4 panels (top row smaller, bottom row larger), NOT face visible in bottom-left panel (face strictly cropped out at top of panel), NOT face covered by headwrap in face close-up panels, NOT colorful background (solid neutral light-warm-grey only), NOT soft even neutral lighting (must be single-side rim backlight only), NOT front light, NOT fill light, NOT any text other than the four panel labels and the title.

   - **9:16+4格不等大**+最高分辨率
   - **左下panel不能露脸**——face strictly cropped out at top
   - **右前臂是叠加机械装置不是义肢替换**（key差异——区分头领的右前臂义肢）
   - **背挂钩索发射器**——signature equipment必须可见
   - **战利品sparse**（2-3个）——区分头领的brutalist mass和副手的refined beads
   - **stocky working laborer build**——不是bodybuilder不是obese不是slender
   - **Title + 4 labels精度**："SAND HUNTER COMMON RAIDER A · LASSO" + FRONT FACE/SIDE FACE/BODY FRONT (NO FACE)/BACK
