【场景物理设置】
本段是追逐戏中的江流被偷袭+萌螃蟹怂跑的喜剧反差节拍+主角飞船@图3 在大漠上以全速狂奔状态向画面深处方向逃跑+9条机械腿持续狂奔+本段画面焦点是船尾甲板上的人物与船舵不展示船腿+船的速度感仅通过音轨与微妙甲板颠簸维持+绝对不能渲染成船腿缓慢移动+绝对不能渲染成船静止。

【前段 江流被偷袭 medium close-up约前4秒】
机位:相机位于船尾甲板某位置+medium close-up中近景+焦段50mm prime lens+rectilinear lens projection+相机基本固定但有微妙handheld呼吸感。
画面构图:江流@图1 上半身入画+从胸部以上到头顶完整可见+江流身体侧前方对镜头+刚开完炮的状态+扛在肩上的青铜古代炮筒还在画面前景占画面左半部分+炮口正面对画面外左侧+炮身#7A6845铜青绿青铜材质+#846035暗金装饰+江流右手扶炮身下方+左手扶炮筒+江流屏脸面具#4FC3D9青色双瞳屏幕亮着刚开完炮余光仍闪烁+背景甲板桅杆与#C8581F落日橙天空+背景轻微motion blur暗示船在持续逃跑。

开篇阶段(约0至1.5秒):江流缓慢把炮筒从肩上往下放+炮筒下沉到江流胸前位置+江流身体放松一瞬+屏脸面具#4FC3D9青色双瞳屏幕由开炮的高亮状态降到普通待机亮度+江流面具微微转向画面外某方向像观察战果+这是被偷袭前的放松瞬间。

中段阶段(约1.5至2.8秒):副手@图2 从画面右侧边缘快速入画+副手本体形态+左大腿与左小腿青铜机改义肢机械腿+双手各持一把弯刀+右手举着弯刀的刀柄高过头顶+刀身朝画面外指向画面右上+副手脸部表情冷静狠辣不是怒喊+像偷袭一般的隐蔽性凶狠+副手挥手用刀柄末端的青铜金属球状部分朝江流后脑勺侧面砸下。

末段阶段(约2.8至4秒):刀柄末端青铜金属球砸中江流后脑勺侧面+砸中瞬间一声"咚"的滑稽闷击音效+像动画片平底锅砸头那种高频金属clang加哑光闷击的混合音色+江流屏脸面具#4FC3D9青色双瞳屏幕在被砸的一瞬间一闪+然后双瞳同时熄灭变成漆黑屏幕+江流身体瞬间瘫软+扛着的炮筒从江流手中滑落+江流整个人开始向画面下方倒下+江流头部呈现"被砸晕"的滑稽顿挫姿态+副手仍在画面右侧持双刀低头审视瘫倒的江流+副手嘴角微微一抿表情仍冷静狠辣不是嬉笑。

【后段 萌螃蟹剥离船舵+变球+滚出画面 close-up约后5秒】
机位:相机硬切到船尾甲板上的青铜船舵位置+medium close-up中近景+焦段50mm prime lens+rectilinear lens projection+相机基本固定但有微妙handheld呼吸感+船舵占画面中央。
画面构图:画面中央是被萌螃蟹机械装置包裹的青铜船舵+按段4-2状态的"半生命体机械船舵"形态+#7A6845铜青绿青铜机械装置完全包覆船舵主体+船舵中央有#4FC3D9青色发光关节缝隙+背景船尾甲板的木质纹理+背景天空可见小部分#C8581F落日橙。

开篇阶段(约前2秒):包裹船舵的青铜机械装置开始机关式解体+装置上的一片片机械片#7A6845铜青绿外壳从船舵表面机关式弹开飞离+#846035暗金符号刻面装饰一片片解体+#4FC3D9青色关节缝隙在装置解体过程中持续发光+解体的装甲片在船舵前方空中悬浮+开始按几何路径汇集重组+船舵主体逐渐显露原本的普通青铜船舵静态状态+@图7 普通船舵+不再旋转+静止状态。

中段阶段(约中1.5秒):空中悬浮的装甲片继续重组+按@图6 萌螃蟹本体形态精准重组+八条腿+#5BB8E0蓝色圆眼睛+萌螃蟹本体形态在船舵前方空中成形+然后掉落到船舵旁边的甲板上+萌螃蟹四脚着地+蓝色圆眼睛快速左右扫了一眼像是确认环境+表情怂萌不是怒气。

末段阶段(约末1.5秒):萌螃蟹八条腿快速向身体内侧收拢+整体蜷成一个青铜球体+#7A6845铜青绿外壳完整闭合+蜷成球体瞬间萌螃蟹画外音同步开始小声嘀咕@音频1 "看不到我看不到我看不到我看不到我..."+天津话尖嗓子+小声碎碎念+怂萌可爱不是怒骂+然后青铜球体开始滚动+滚动方向由模型自行决定+球体快速滚出画面边缘+滚出画面后画外音持续念到本段末尾。

【声音设计】
前段:江流脚步轻微移动+副手快速入画脚步突然短促+副手挥刀柄破空声+砸中瞬间"咚"高频金属clang加哑光闷击cartoon物理音效(像动画片平底锅砸头那种滑稽感+绝对不是真实战斗的金属武器格斗声)+江流瘫软倒下衣物摩擦+炮筒滑落青铜碰撞。
后段:包裹船舵机械装置解体咔哒机关弹开层+装甲片飞行咔哒+萌螃蟹空中重组金属声+萌螃蟹掉落甲板金属碰撞+萌螃蟹八腿甲板小幅移动咔哒+蜷成球体的金属闭合声+球体滚动青铜滚地声渐远+萌螃蟹画外音"看不到我看不到我看不到我看不到我..."天津话尖嗓子小声碎碎念怂萌可爱不是怒骂。
全程:背景大漠风声+背景9条机械腿crash远景+仅生成动作音效与对白音效与环境音效+不要bgm+不要抒情背景音乐+sound design only ambient and action sounds NO background music。

【关键约束】
镜头硬切铁律:本段9秒由Seedance在内部处理2个镜头之间的硬切hard cut+绝对禁止镜头过渡+绝对禁止淡入淡出。

砸头音效铁律:副手刀柄砸江流后脑勺的"咚"音效必须是cartoon物理音效+像动画片平底锅砸头的滑稽感+绝对禁止真实战斗的金属武器格斗声+绝对禁止血腥音效+绝对禁止真实暴力动作的物理音效+这是喜剧反差节拍。

副手刀柄不刀刃铁律:副手用右手弯刀的刀柄末端青铜金属球状部分砸江流后脑勺+不是用刀刃砸+绝对禁止刀刃见血+绝对禁止真实暴力描绘+这是滑稽偷袭不是真实战斗。

萌螃蟹三形态依次铁律:本段萌螃蟹依次:包裹船舵的机械装置形态→机关式解体→空中重组成本体形态(八腿+蓝眼)→蜷成青铜球体→滚出画面+绝对禁止跳过任何中间形态+绝对禁止机械装置直接消失+绝对禁止萌螃蟹凭空出现+变身过程必须可见装甲片在空中飞行重组的视觉过程+剥离后船舵恢复成普通青铜船舵静态+绝对禁止船舵旋转+绝对禁止船舵保持半生命体形态。

萌螃蟹画外音铁律:画外音必须从蜷成球体瞬间开始+天津话尖嗓子+小声碎碎念+怂萌可爱+绝对禁止怒骂+绝对禁止大声尖叫+绝对禁止普通话。

NOT slow motion+NOT speed ramping+NOT静态镜头+NOT 3D rendered+NOT CGI+NOT video game graphics+NOT Unreal Engine render+NOT Blender render+NOT clean digital surface+NOT cartoon animation visual style+NOT chibi+NOT cyberpunk neon+NOT clear blue sky+NOT modern weapons+无字幕+无水印。


### 第50条｜本段呈现一场沙漠追逐戏的中段+所有运动物体的运动方向必须严格一致+全部朝向画

【场景物理设置 chase formation】
本段呈现一场沙漠追逐戏的中段+所有运动物体的运动方向必须严格一致+全部朝向画面深处的同一方向狂奔逃跑或追击。

运动方向铁律:画面里的所有大型移动物体(主角飞船+2艘沙艇+机械赛加)运动方向完全一致+全部朝向画面深处的同一矢量方向+不存在任何对冲方向+不存在任何物体面对相机或面对其他物体。

追逐顺序:主角飞船@图2 在追逐队列的最前方逃跑+2艘沙艇@图3 跟在主角飞船后方追击+机械赛加@图5 起手时跟在2艘沙艇的后方追击。

身体朝向:所有车辆与生物的身体头部/船头/兽头/驾驶员朝向均指向画面深处的逃跑/追击方向+主角飞船的船头朝向画面深处+船尾朝向画面前景+2艘沙艇的船头同样朝向画面深处+沙艇上喽啰的脸朝向画面深处+机械赛加的头部朝向画面深处+刘回骑姿前倾朝向画面深处。

速度铁律:所有车辆均处于全速狂奔状态+主角飞船的9条机械腿正在独立高速踏地踩沙+每条腿独立galloping节奏不同步+船身随9腿步频微颠+船底踏起大量沙尘+2艘沙艇引擎全力运转+沙艇底部铜机械结构滑过沙地+沙艇尾部扬起大量沙尘+机械赛加四条机械腿全力狂奔+蹄声急促。

相对速度:主角飞船虽是被追的猎物但本身也在全速逃跑+只是飞船的速度略慢于机械赛加的全速狂奔+2艘沙艇速度比飞船略快但比机械赛加略慢+机械赛加是三者中速度最快的+正在缩短与飞船的距离+正在追平并超越2艘沙艇。

【起手阶段】
画面起点:24mm wide-angle prime lens广角+intentional barrel distortion广角桶形畸变作为表现手法+dramatic forced perspective震撼透视+camera positioned extremely low to the ground约30cm above desert sand+lens tilted slightly upward by 5-10 degrees from horizontal+camera positioned directly behind and slightly off-center from the running mechanical Saiga+the camera is essentially being dragged along the desert floor as the Saiga charges away from camera at high speed.

起手帧构图:画面前景下方约三分之一区域被@图5 机械赛加占据+赛加身体侧3/4view朝向画面深处方向+四条机械腿全部展开+前景的赛加后蹄因24mm广角forced perspective拉大+一条后蹄凝固在大跃步状态+踏起大量#C49B5C赭金沙浪向相机方向飞溅+赛加身体的#7A6845铜青绿装甲板沿背脊在夕阳下泛金光+赛加双角螺旋塔尖部分入画+赛加身体的#846035暗金装饰板细节可见。
@图4 刘回骑在赛加背部装甲鞍位上+从相机视角看是从赛加后方略偏后视角+刘回身体微微前倾朝向画面深处+刘回的黑色短发wild backward streaking向后飞舞+#6B2419深红与#B8A082米色部落围巾violently trailing backward在风中疯狂翻飞+刘回的赤膊纹身上半身可见+#2D2A2A机械义肢右臂可见+背挂弯刀的刀柄露出。

画面中景与远景:深景里@图2 主角飞船以小尺寸出现+船身朝向画面深处+船尾朝向相机方向+船的9条机械腿独立踏地的动态可见+船底扬起沙尘+船在远景中占画面深景约八分之一面积。2艘@图3 沙艇位于赛加和主角船之间的中景距离+沙艇船头朝向画面深处+沙艇追逐姿态可见。

【中段过渡阶段】
相机从起手的赛加后方低机位贴地+逐步运动调整位置至刘回的侧前方约150度方位+相机高度也从30cm贴地高度逐步抬升到刘回脸部高度的水平视线+焦段从24mm wide-angle广角逐渐过渡到50mm prime lens标准焦段+广角桶形畸变逐渐减弱直至消失+画面从forced perspective夸张透视过渡为rectilinear projection直线投影+相机环绕路径由模型根据空间几何自行选择最物理合理的过渡轨迹+不指定具体环绕方向。

过渡过程中:机械赛加@图5 持续狂奔向画面深处+四条机械腿独立踏地+每秒3-4步的高速节奏+赛加身体加速度可见+赛加逐渐缩短与前方2艘沙艇的距离+赛加超越2艘沙艇的过程必须可见:赛加先是从2艘沙艇后方位置追上+然后被并行追平+然后超越到沙艇前方位置。

刘回@图4 在赛加背上保持向前狂奔的攻击性骑姿+身体微微前倾+头发持续飞舞+围巾持续向后翻飞+刘回喉咙发出低沉雄性怒吼"滚开!"的余韵延续。

主角飞船@图2 持续以全速狂奔向画面深处+9条机械腿持续独立踏地+船底扬起沙尘+但飞船仍在画面深景里逐渐变大+从远景小尺寸过渡到中景中等尺寸+这种"既在远离又显得变大"的视觉来自于赛加追击的速度比飞船快+赛加在缩短与飞船的距离。

2艘沙艇@图3 持续以全速向画面深处追逐+在11秒过渡过程中先是与赛加平行+然后被赛加超越+但沙艇本身始终保持向画面深处的运动方向+不出现转向或减速。

【收手阶段】
画面收手状态:50mm prime lens长焦+rectilinear lens projection直线投影+geometric lines straight直线笔直+no barrel distortion无桶形畸变+medium close-up中近景+相机位于刘回侧前方约150度方位+相机高度水平在刘回脸部高度。

收手帧构图:刘回@图4 头肩占画面主导+刘回的3/4侧脸朝向相机+完整的眼睛可见+清晰锐利的捕猎凝视+被眼罩遮盖的另一侧只露出部分轮廓不是焦点+面部表情:嘴角微微上扬approximately 5-10度幅度+lips closed or slightly parted but no teeth showing+brow relaxed not furrowed+eyes sharp calculating+反派冷静狩猎笑容a calm cold villain smile of restrained predatory satisfaction+the calm of Anton Chigurh in No Country for Old Men or Immortan Joe quiet menace not loud menace+表达"猎物已经在我眼里我有的是时间"的cold confidence。

刘回的黑色短发持续wild backward streaking向后飞舞+部落围巾#6B2419深红与#B8A082米色组合在风中持续向后翻飞streak。

机械赛加@图5 头部和颈部在画面下边缘可见+赛加双角螺旋塔尖部分入画+赛加单眼青色发光#4FC3D9可能在边缘可见+赛加狂奔的运动姿态可见+赛加身体仍朝向画面深处。

背景空间叙事:画面背景大漠地面与远方天空motion blur动态模糊+背景元素流向画面后方+motion blur幅度moderate中等不极端+背景元素仍然identifiable可辨认+背景中可清晰辨认出主角飞船@图2 此时位于刘回头部背景方位的中景距离+船的尺寸明显大于起手时的远景小尺寸+占画面背景约六分之一面积+9条机械腿持续狂奔可见+船头仍朝向画面深处+船仍在自身高速逃跑状态。同时背景中2艘@图3 沙艇此时已被赛加超越+沙艇位于刘回身侧或身后的motion blur位置+沙艇剪影可见但已经在赛加的平面或后方位置+沙艇仍朝向画面深处。

光线:夕阳从画面后方低角度逆光+刘回头发与肩edges被#F5C870金色边光镶光rim-light+脸部部分阴影fall into deeper #1A1610 shadow tones+背景motion blur中可见#F5C870金色god rays穿过#D9A55C琥珀沙雾。

【声音设计】
起手:沙漠高速风声+赛加四条机械蹄crunchy落沙crash脚步循环+赛加机械关节金属嘎吱声+赛加机械喷气吼声+刘回喉咙刚刚怒吼完"滚开!"的低沉残响余韵+主角飞船的9条机械腿远景crash脚步声明确可闻+2艘沙艇的引擎呼啸声明确可闻+多源运动音效共存表明所有车辆均在狂奔。

中段过渡:持续赛加机械蹄声+蹄声节奏从中速逐渐加速到急速+赛加机械喷气声逐渐加强+多普勒效应:背景2艘沙艇引擎声从画面前方位置逐渐多普勒下降经过相机视角再过渡到画面后方位置+主角飞船9条机械腿的脚步crash声从远景渐近变为中景明显+表明赛加正在缩短与飞船的距离。

收手:赛加蹄声达到最急促节奏+赛加金属嘎吱机械关节声明显+刘回喉咙发出一声短促低沉冷哼snort短促残忍的捕食者满足声+主角船的脚步crash声大量明显且节奏与画面同步+背景沙暴风声持续呼啸。

全程:仅生成动作音效与对白音效与环境音效+不要bgm+不要抒情背景音乐+sound design only ambient and action sounds NO background music+刘回的喉咙吼声调性是低沉沙哑成熟男性威胁感+像Mad Max部落首领台词调性。

【关键约束】
运动方向铁律:所有大型移动物体(主角飞船+2艘沙艇+机械赛加)运动方向完全一致+全部朝向画面深处的同一矢量方向+不存在任何对冲+所有车辆船头/兽头/骑姿身体朝向均指向画面深处+绝对禁止主角飞船面对赛加+绝对禁止沙艇面对赛加+绝对禁止任何车辆转向或对冲。

速度共存铁律:11秒过程中所有车辆始终保持全速狂奔状态+主角飞船的9条机械腿全程独立踏地踩沙+主角飞船绝不静止+绝不缓行+绝不仅仅"被赛加追"而自己不动+主角飞船扬起的沙尘必须明显可见+主角飞船的速度只是略慢于赛加但仍在快速移动。

加速超越铁律:11秒过程中机械赛加从起手时位于2艘沙艇后方位置+加速到收手时位于2艘沙艇前方位置+赛加超越沙艇这一空间叙事过程必须明显呈现+但所有车辆始终向画面深处运动不存在任何转向。

收手姿态铁律:视频收手最后一帧画面必须呈现刘回3/4侧脸medium close-up+50mm长焦无畸变+嘴角微扬冷静狩猎笑容+头发飞舞+背景motion blur+主角船在背景中景且船头仍朝画面深处+沙艇被超越在刘回侧后方且船头仍朝画面深处。

焦段过渡铁律:焦段从起手24mm wide-angle barrel distortion广角桶形畸变+逐步过渡到收手50mm rectilinear projection标准直线投影+过渡平滑不突变+不在11秒中段出现明显的焦段跳变。

主体身份铁律:刘回严格按@图4 渲染男性中年独眼刀疤斯巴达爆炸短发部落围巾赤膊纹身机械义肢右臂背挂弯刀+赛加严格按@图5 渲染四条机械腿不可改+双角螺旋塔尖+单眼青色发光+全身青铜装甲+主角飞船严格按@图2 渲染9条机械腿+船头红腹铜青绿+布幡铜钟。

风格铁律:35mm film stock photography texture+analog film grain+photochemical look+NOT 3D rendered+NOT CGI+NOT video game graphics+NOT Unreal Engine render+NOT Blender render+NOT clean digital surface+NOT plastic look+all surfaces must show real physical wear and weathering+film print quality not digital cinema clean.

### 第51条｜用白噪音和动作音效作为整段声学底层+不要配抒情背景音乐+sound desi

Shot on 35mm Kodak Vision3 500T film stock + analog film grain visible + practical light only + Christopher Doyle cinematography + Wong Kar-wai painterly desaturation + photochemical film texture + slight halation on highlights + organic film noise + heavy atmospheric haze + tactile real-world materials + film print look not digital cinema look + photorealistic film still aesthetic + real physical scene captured through a camera lens + not a 3D render.

镜头末段+钩索瞬间被绷得更紧+钩索本身在画面里向画面深处方向被拽动+刘回的手部被钩索方向猛地拽动+手在画面里的位置略向画面深处方向移动+下半张脸跟随手部一起被向前带动+头部略向画面深处方向倾倒+背景的motion blur速度突然加快从匀速变成急速向画面深处方向加速流过暗示整个身体被向那个方向猛地拽动伴随massive armor mechanism groan under load低频金属呻吟声急剧加强。

**HARD CUT.**

镜头2:大俯拍high-angle overhead shot+焦段24mm wide angle+rectilinear lens projection+相机从刘回正上方略偏一点的高位俯拍+相机有subtle handheld呼吸感。

### 第53条｜1×5 grid layout vertical stack with fi

A professional hand drawn sketch storyboard sheet, pencil and ink illustration style, rough artistic linework, cross-hatching for shadows, loose expressive strokes, monochrome black and white on aged cream/off-white paper texture background.

1×5 grid layout vertical stack with five equal storyboard panels arranged top to bottom, each panel landscape 16:9 ratio with thick hand-drawn black ink frame border. Total sheet aspect ratio 16:45 vertical.

Top header text on the sheet (handwritten style, centered): "WANWU SHENG EP1 SEG8 - SAND HUNTERS HOOK PROTAGONIST SHIP STORYBOARD"

Each panel must include:
- Pencil sketch illustration with grey marker shading
- Stick figures and simple geometric shapes for all subjects (NOT detailed anatomy, NOT realistic faces, NOT detailed mecha)
- Red colored pencil arrows annotating motion direction and camera movement
- Handwritten panel info strip below each panel in plain typography:
  "PANEL N · 【景别与机位】 / 【镜头参数】 / 【音效与动作说明】"

PANEL 1 sketch — HIGH ANGLE ESTABLISHING AERIAL:
- Bird's eye view roughly 60 to 70 degrees down angle looking at the desert battlefield
- Center of frame: large protagonist ship as a long rectangular silhouette with nine stick-leg shapes underneath striding forward, sand plume trailing behind in cross-hatched cloud
- Three small sand skimmer triangular shapes positioned around the ship: one trailing left rear, one trailing right rear, one approaching from rear center, all aiming converging trajectories toward the ship's legs
- Each skimmer has a stick figure rider visible on top
- Red pencil arrows: one large arrow showing the ship moving deep into frame toward upper area, three smaller arrows showing each skimmer converging toward the ship
- Background: empty desert horizon line with light cross-hatching for sand texture
- Handwritten label below: "PANEL 1 · HIGH ANGLE AERIAL ESTABLISHING / 35mm wide / SFX: ship legs galloping crash, three skimmer engine roars closing in"

PANEL 2 sketch — MEDIUM WIDE THREE SAND HUNTERS THROW HOOKS SIMULTANEOUSLY:
- Side angle medium-wide framing showing three sand skimmers in a row across the frame at slight diagonal arrangement, each skimmer in profile
- Each skimmer has one stick figure sand hunter on top in mid-throwing pose: arm extended forward, body leaning back for momentum, hook rope coiled then extending forward in arc
- Three rope arcs drawn as curved lines extending from each hunter forward and upward into the frame, ropes converging toward upper frame area where the ship leg silhouettes are partially visible at top of frame
- Three small grappling hook shapes visible mid-air at the tips of the rope arcs
- Red pencil arrows: three arrows along each rope showing throw direction toward upper frame
- Background: simple desert horizon line, sand plume cross-hatching beneath each skimmer
- Handwritten label below: "PANEL 2 · MEDIUM WIDE 3 HUNTERS THROW HOOKS / 35mm / SFX: three rope whooshes layered, hooks clank-click locking onto metal"

PANEL 3 sketch — LOW ANGLE GROUND-LEVEL ROPES PULL TAUT:
- Extreme low angle camera positioned at sand level looking up at three massive ship leg shapes in foreground occupying most of frame, legs as thick cylindrical stick shapes towering up out of top of frame
- Three taut ropes drawn as straight rigid diagonal lines stretched tight from upper portion of legs (where hooks have locked on) angling down and out toward the lower edges and corners of frame, disappearing off frame edge toward the off-screen sand skimmers
- Vibration marks (small cross-hatch zigzag) drawn along each rope to indicate extreme tension and trembling
- Sand particles drawn as small dots flying around the leg bases
- Red pencil arrows: three arrows along each rope showing pulling tension force pointing outward and down off-frame, one upward arrow on each leg showing the legs still moving forward despite resistance
- Background: small portion of sky visible at top corners, mostly filled with the towering leg shapes
- Handwritten label below: "PANEL 3 · LOW ANGLE GROUND ROPES TAUT / 24mm wide / SFX: rope creak under tension, metallic strain groans, sand crunch"

PANEL 4 sketch — WIDE TRACKING SIDE PROFILE TUG OF WAR:
- Side profile wide shot showing the entire battlefield in a horizontal strip
- Center frame: the protagonist ship in side view as a long rectangular silhouette with nine leg shapes underneath striding forward into deep frame direction (toward right side of frame)
- Behind and to the rear of the ship: three sand skimmers visible trailing behind, each connected to the ship by a long rope rendered as taut diagonal line from skimmer to ship leg
- The skimmers are tilted forward-downward indicating they are being dragged forward against their own engine reverse-thrust attempts to brake
- Sand plumes erupting from beneath both the ship legs and the dragging skimmers, the skimmer plumes drawn as larger thicker cross-hatched dust clouds indicating heavier dragging friction
- Stick figure hunters visible on each skimmer leaning back hard, arms extended gripping their rope ends, bodies in maximum effort tug pose
- Red pencil arrows: large arrow showing the ship continuing forward direction at top, three smaller opposing arrows on each skimmer showing the futile reverse pull, all the smaller arrows being overridden by the dominant ship-direction arrow
- Background: desert horizon line stretching across frame
- Handwritten label below: "PANEL 4 · WIDE SIDE TRACKING TUG OF WAR / 50mm / SFX: ropes humming under load, skimmer engines whining in reverse, ship leg crash continuous"

PANEL 5 sketch — MEDIUM CLOSE-UP SAND HUNTER TURNS HEAD AT VOICE:
- Medium close-up framing showing one stick figure sand hunter from chest up, on top of his sand skimmer
- The hunter's pose: still gripping the taut rope with both hands leaning back, but his head is in mid-rotation turning sharply over his shoulder toward off-frame direction (model decides which side)
- Facial expression on the simple stick figure head: surprise marks, eyes drawn as wide circles, mouth as small open oval
- Speed lines around the hunter's head indicating sudden head turn motion
- Behind hunter: blurred suggestion of the taut rope still extending off-frame toward upper area where the ship leg connects, blurred sand plume background
- Red pencil arrow: one arrow showing the hunter's head rotation direction toward off-frame
- A small label/note in the panel margin: "off-screen voice: 'WO LAI!' (here I come)"
- Handwritten label below: "PANEL 5 · MCU HUNTER REACTION TO OFFSCREEN VOICE / 85mm / SFX: distant offscreen shout 'WO LAI!', rope tension continues, hunter sharp head turn whoosh"

Art direction: classic animation-school storyboard aesthetic — pencil line-work for primary subjects, grey marker shading for shadows and atmosphere (sand, dust, smoke), red colored pencil for arrows and motion annotations only. Loose expressive strokes throughout, intentionally rough, NOT polished, NOT clean digital line art, NOT colored illustration, NOT painted artwork.

Constraints: All five panels must remain inside one single sheet image. Maintain the 1x5 vertical grid layout with each panel landscape 16:9. Keep stick figure abstraction consistent across all panels — NO detailed faces, NO realistic anatomy, NO detailed mecha designs, NO color other than red pencil for arrows and black/grey for sketch. Handwritten panel info strips must be readable plain typography. NO logos, NO watermarks, NO extra decorative elements.


### 第54条｜本段是追逐戏中的多线压制开场+三艘沙艇上各一个喽啰抛钩索套住主角飞船三条不同

Shot on 35mm Kodak Vision3 500T film stock + analog film grain visible + practical light only + Christopher Doyle cinematography + Wong Kar-wai painterly desaturation + photochemical film texture + slight halation on highlights + organic film noise + heavy atmospheric haze + tactile real-world materials + film print look not digital cinema look + photorealistic film still aesthetic + real physical scene captured through a camera lens + not a 3D render.

Mad Max Fury Road kinetic chase + tug of war physical tension + JOJO deadpan beat for the final reaction + 16:9 anamorphic + golden hour lighting.

整段15秒一个连续视频+内含Seedance自行处理的5个镜头硬切+按故事板5格分镜节奏执行。

【场景物理设置】
本段是追逐戏中的多线压制开场+三艘沙艇上各一个喽啰抛钩索套住主角飞船三条不同机械腿+试图把船拽停+但大船仍持续狂奔拖着三艘沙艇向画面深处方向继续前进+末段画外音传来狼王刘回一声"我来！"+其中一个喽啰惊讶顺声转头。所有大型移动物体（主角飞船+三艘沙艇）运动方向完全一致+全部朝向画面深处的同一矢量方向+绝对禁止任何对冲方向。

【镜头1 高角度俯瞰建立全局空间——按故事板Panel 1构图】
机位:high angle aerial约60至70度向下角度+焦段35mm wide+rectilinear lens projection+相机略带handheld呼吸感。
画面:整个沙漠战场+主角飞船@图1 居画面中央+完整长方形船体侧面与船底9条机械腿可见+机械腿全速狂奔向画面深处+船尾拖出大量#C49B5C沙脊赭金沙尘plume+三艘小型沙艇@图5 分散在主角船的左后/右后/正后三个位置+三艘沙艇都以引擎全速向主角船的位置收敛包夹+每艘沙艇上一个喽啰可见+三艘沙艇底部各喷起#C49B5C沙尘plume+#C8581F落日橙天空与#C49B5C沙脊赭金大漠地平线占据画面其余空间+整体构图传达三艘小艇向中央巨船收敛压制的包夹感。

【镜头2 三个喽啰同时抛钩索——按故事板Panel 2构图】
机位:medium-wide侧面框景+焦段35mm prime lens+相机随沙艇向前tracking+保持三艘沙艇在画面中横向并排可见。

背景:主角飞船的部分船身或机械腿剪影在画面上方边缘可见+#C0B299沙尘颗粒灰沙尘弥漫。
末段:三个#9B7E50青铜钩头同时在画面斜上方达到弧线顶点+正要向上方船腿位置咬合。

【镜头3 极低机位贴沙地仰拍三条钩索绷紧拽住三条不同船腿——按故事板Panel 3构图】
机位:extreme low angle贴沙地高度+焦段24mm wide-angle+rectilinear lens projection+相机基本固定+视角向上仰望船腿。
画面:画面前景与中景被三条主角船的机械腿占据大部分+三条机械腿是@图1 飞船底部9条腿中的不同三条+#7A6845铜青绿装甲+#846035古代暗金装饰+#4FC3D9失落科技青色关节缝隙发光+三条机械腿巨大占据画面竖向2/3+腿部从画面下方延伸冲出画面上方框外+三条机械腿仍galloping持续向画面深处方向迈步前进+三条腿的中部位置各被一个#9B7E50抛光青铜钩头紧紧咬合+从三条腿延伸出三条#4A2A18麻绳深棕色钩索+三条钩索绷成完全笔直rigid对角线状态+从船腿位置斜向画面下方边角延伸+三条钩索消失在画面框外（连接到画面外的沙艇位置）+三条钩索因极端张力震颤+绳索表面可见纤维微震+三条机械腿基部踏起的#B8862A拖拽摩擦热沙在画面下方扬起。

画面:画面横向展开整个战场+主角飞船@图1 居画面中央偏前方+船体侧面完整可见+9条机械腿全速galloping向画面深处方向+船尾后方与侧后方可见三艘沙艇@图5 拖在画面里+每艘沙艇通过一条#4A2A18麻绳深棕色钩索连接到主角船的不同机械腿+三条钩索全部绷成笔直对角线状态+三艘沙艇明显前倾向下tilted forward-downward+沙艇前部下沉+沙艇尾部略翘起+像被拖车拖拽的小车+沙艇底部喷起#B8862A拖拽摩擦热沙plume比正常沙尘plume更厚更深色+喽啰在沙艇上身体大幅后仰+双手紧握钩索末端+脚抵沙艇船舷+脸部狰狞用力+三个喽啰呈现一致的最大力度反向拉拽姿态+但沙艇本身仍被拖动持续向画面深处方向移动+整体视觉传达三艘小艇正尽全力对抗但失败被拖走的拔河僵持感。

【镜头5 一个喽啰medium close-up惊讶顺声转头——按故事板Panel 5构图】
机位:medium close-up中近景+焦段85mm slight telephoto+rectilinear lens projection+相机基本固定+仅微妙handheld呼吸感。
画面:画面中央一个喽啰胸部以上half-body入画+模型自定是三个喽啰中哪一个+喽啰仍在沙艇上+双手仍紧握#4A2A18麻绳深棕色钩索末端+身体仍后仰用力拉拽+但喽啰头部正快速向画面外某一侧（模型自定方向）转头+脖子转动幅度大+表情从狰狞用力切换为惊讶+眼睛睁大+嘴巴微张呈"啊"形状+脸部#8B5A3C晒红古铜肤色在#C8581F落日橙夕阳下泛光+喽啰头戴的#6B2419沙猎血红部落布条或头饰随转头动作飘动+背景虚化的钩索仍向画面外某方向延伸+喽啰转头方向暗示画外音的来源方向。
画外音:在喽啰转头瞬间从画面外某方向（与喽啰转头方向一致）传来一声中年男性嗓音爆喝"我来！"+焦急霸气+像反派首领看到手下被压制时介入战斗的宣告+爆喝有力+不沙哑+带回响感传达声音来自远处但响亮。

【声音设计】
镜头1:9条机械腿crash循环+三艘沙艇引擎咆哮closing in+大漠风声呼啸。
镜头2:三条钩索抛出rope whoosh破空声层+三个#9B7E50青铜钩头飞行空气震颤+三个喽啰用力呼喊声层+9条机械腿crash循环远景。
镜头3:三个青铜钩头同时clank-click咬合在#7A6845铜青绿机械腿装甲上的金属碰撞与锁紧声+三条钩索绷紧creak under tension层+9条机械腿持续crash循环+金属应变groan+#B8862A热沙crunch。
镜头4:三条钩索humming under load持续低频震颤+三艘沙艇引擎whining in reverse反向制动声+9条机械腿crash循环+三个喽啰持续用力呼喊+#B8862A拖拽热沙摩擦低频。

全程:背景大漠风声呼啸+仅生成动作音效与对白音效与环境音效+不要bgm+不要抒情背景音乐+sound design only ambient and action sounds NO background music。

【关键约束】
故事板反偷懒铁律: 仅作5个镜头的构图与机位参考+绝对禁止视频里出现故事板的任何视觉风格特征+绝对禁止铅笔素描质感+绝对禁止灰色marker底涂+绝对禁止米色或off-white纸张背景+绝对禁止草稿linework+绝对禁止红色铅笔箭头出现在画面里+绝对禁止任何手绘动画美学+视频必须是真实35mm胶片电影质感的实拍photorealistic film still。

钩索物理铁律:三条钩索分别套在主角船底部9条机械腿中的三条不同的腿上+绝对禁止三条钩索套同一条腿+绝对禁止钩索套船体而不是腿+#9B7E50青铜钩头必须可见地咬合在#7A6845铜青绿机械腿装甲上。

拔河僵持铁律:本段视觉核心是"三艘沙艇尽全力反向拉拽但仍被大船拖着前进"的双重物理状态+三条钩索必须绷成完全笔直rigid对角线+三艘沙艇必须明显前倾向下tilted forward-downward+绝对禁止沙艇平稳行驶+绝对禁止钩索松弛+绝对禁止主角船被拽停或减速+主角船在整段15秒内必须持续全速狂奔向画面深处方向。

船腿速度铁律:主角飞船的9条机械腿在整段15秒内持续保持全速独立galloping状态+每秒3-4步高速节奏+船底踏起大量沙尘plume+绝对禁止船腿缓慢移动+绝对禁止船腿同步行走+绝对禁止船静止或减速+即使被三条钩索拽住+船腿也必须保持狂奔速度感。

### 第55条｜medium-wide two-shot双人中景+焦段50mm prime

Shot on 35mm Kodak Vision3 500T film stock + analog film grain visible + practical light only + Christopher Doyle cinematography + Wong Kar-wai painterly desaturation + photochemical film texture + slight halation on highlights + organic film noise + heavy atmospheric haze + tactile real-world materials + film print look not digital cinema look + photorealistic film still aesthetic + real physical scene captured through a camera lens + not a 3D render.

Aftermath quiet reaction beats + Hulk-style explosive standing leap + Mad Max post-impact dust settling + 16:9 anamorphic + golden hour lighting.

用白噪音和动作音效作为整段声学底层+不要配抒情背景音乐+sound design only ambient and action sounds NO background music。

### 第56条｜Layout: 2 columns x 2 rows grid on age

A hand-drawn pencil sketch storyboard on aged off-white paper, in the style of professional film production storyboards. Total image aspect ratio strictly 21:9.

Layout: 2 columns x 2 rows grid on aged paper background. Each of the 4 cells contains one element. Top row (left to right): Panel 1, Panel 2. Bottom row (left to right): Panel 3, Shooting Plan diagram. Each of Panels 1-3 is strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape format with thick hand-drawn black ink frame border. The Shooting Plan cell in the bottom-right is a top-down bird's eye view of the desert battlefield aftermath with camera positions and 180-degree axis line marked.

Beneath each storyboard panel inner frame is a hand-lettered caption strip showing panel number, lens specification, shot type, and brief SFX/action notes in serif block letters slightly uneven.

The drawing technique is loose graphite pencil with confident architectural perspective and clear cinematic composition. Sketches are deliberately gestural but every spatial relationship and prop detail is clearly readable. Selective red colored pencil arrows and emphasis marks indicate movement direction, force vectors, and key dramatic beats.

LIGHTING NOTE FOR ALL PANELS: late afternoon desert golden hour, low sun key light from upper-left 30 degrees. Dense cross-hatching dominates most surfaces representing the shadow base of the warm desert atmosphere. Selective bright untouched paper zones represent the directional sun-key highlights on metal armor surfaces and rim-lit silhouettes. Strong tonal contrast throughout — deep blacks in shadow zones, bright untouched paper for highlights, NOT evenly lit.

Style references: Wes Anderson production storyboards, traditional Hollywood film storyboard aesthetic, animator's pre-visualization sketches, Mad Max Fury Road action sequence boards, Coen Brothers character beat storyboards.

Drawing rules:
- Pencil graphite on warm off-white aged paper background with slight edge wear and faint smudges suggesting heavy production use
- Black ink for panel borders and hand-lettered captions
- Red colored pencil ONLY for arrows, emphasis marks, camera position triangles, force vectors, and 180-degree axis line
- Cross-hatching for shading; bright highlight zones use untouched paper
- Hand-lettered captions in serif-style block letters, slightly uneven

DIRECTIONAL AXIS RULE: continuing from prior segment 9B, the protagonist ship has fallen into the deep distance of the battlefield (toward one consistent direction, henceforth "deep direction"). Liu Hui stands as the anchor in the foreground area (opposite the ship's direction). Three sand skimmers have all been thrown off in the aftermath chaos — two of them lie tipped over in the foreground-middle area near Liu Hui. The action axis runs from Liu Hui through the fallen ship; cameras stay on one consistent side of this axis throughout the sequence.

Title at top center of entire image, hand-lettered in confident block serif: "WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 10A — AFTERMATH & LEAP"

Below title, hand-lettered subtitle in smaller letters: "MULTI-SHOT HARD CUT / 3 SHOTS / 3 STORYBOARD PANELS + SHOOTING PLAN / RAIDER REACTION BEATS + LIU HUI'S LEAP"

PANEL 1 (top-left cell) — SHOT 1: OLD RAIDER + YOUNG RAIDER PROPPED AGAINST OVERTURNED SKIMMER, SHARED LOOK:
Scene composition: medium-wide two-shot framing. In foreground-middle of frame: a sand skimmer lies tipped on its side on the desert ground — the triangular streamlined hull is rolled roughly 80-90 degrees onto one side, the bottom of the skimmer with its jet-propulsion vents now exposed to the side facing the camera, the upper edge of the hull pointing skyward. Two Sand Hunter raiders sit on the desert sand leaning their backs against the upturned underside of this skimmer:

- LEFT raider — old raider — a middle-aged man with a long braided ponytail of dark hair hanging over one shoulder, a long full dark beard, a weathered scarf wrapped around his neck, a leather and brown fabric tunic, his back propped against the skimmer's underside, knees drawn up, his bow slung across his back partly visible, one hand resting on his knee. Face streaked with sand and dust. Expression: stunned blank stare into middle distance.

- RIGHT raider (sitting slightly to the right of the old raider, both leaning against the same overturned skimmer) — young raider — a younger man with short cropped dark hair, short stubble beard, a scarf around his neck, leather vest with crossed strap rigging, his mechanical bronze lasso-launcher gauntlet visible on one forearm, back propped against the skimmer, sand and dust on his face and clothing. Expression: stunned blank stare into middle distance, same direction as the old raider.

Both raiders are looking off-frame toward the upper-right direction (toward the unseen fallen ship in the deep distance, but the ship itself is NOT in this panel's frame — the panel is tightly composed on just the two characters and the overturned skimmer). Their two heads are at slightly different heights but their eye-lines are aimed at the same off-frame point. They have just witnessed something enormous.

Background: desert sand floor extends behind them with some scattered debris pieces (a snapped piece of rope, a torn fragment of fabric, small sand-dust still hovering in the air). In the far distance behind the raiders the horizon line is visible with golden hour atmospheric haze cross-hatched densely. The background middle-distance contains a faint suggestion of the second tipped-over skimmer (heavily abstracted, partial sketch only — just a triangular shape lying on its side).

Late afternoon sun comes from upper-left at low angle, casting strong rim-lighting on the right edge of both raiders' silhouettes, leaving the left side of their faces in deep cross-hatched shadow. The overturned skimmer's exposed underside catches the sun on its upper edge.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow indicating the static medium-wide camera framing. Red pencil dashed eye-line vectors from BOTH raiders extending toward the upper-right edge of frame, converging on a single off-frame point — labeled "shared off-frame eye-line." A small red pencil curved arrow on each raider's head indicating a slow simultaneous turn motion at the end of the shot — labeled "slow simultaneous head-turn / look at each other."

Caption strip below panel: "SHOT 1 / PANEL 1 — TWO-SHOT MEDIUM-WIDE / OLD + YOUNG RAIDER PROPPED AGAINST TIPPED SKIMMER / 50mm prime / static framing with handheld micro-shake / SFX: distant settling dust, residual far-off creaking from fallen ship, soft desert wind, two simultaneous synchronized exhales / ACTION: both look off-frame in shared direction, then slow simultaneous head-turn to look at each other"

**HARD CUT.**

PANEL 2 (top-right cell) — SHOT 2: FEMALE RAIDER CRAWLS OUT OF FULLY OVERTURNED SKIMMER, COUGHING:
Scene composition: medium-wide framing. In the middle of frame: a sand skimmer lies completely overturned on the desert sand — fully inverted, the bottom-side now pointing skyward, the open-top deck pressed down into the sand. One side of the skimmer's hull has crumpled inward from the impact, creating an opening near the sand level. Through this opening at the lower edge of the skimmer's hull, the female raider is in the process of crawling out — her upper body is already free of the skimmer, propping herself up on one forearm on the sand, her other hand pushing against the skimmer's hull edge for leverage. Her lower body is still partly inside the wreck.

The female raider has dark hair pulled back in a high ponytail with steampunk-style brass goggles pushed up on her forehead, her face and hair are heavily covered in sand and dust, sand streaks down her face, her leather vest is dirty and partly torn at the shoulder, the cartridge-belt rigging across her chest is rumpled. Her face is in the act of coughing — head turned slightly down and to one side, mouth open, eyes squeezed shut, one shoulder hunched up as her body shakes from the cough.

Around the female raider's emergence point: a thin cloud of sand-dust hangs in the air from the recent impact. Small debris scattered nearby — a broken piece of skimmer hull, some scattered cartridge shells, a strap of leather. The desert sand under the wreck shows deep furrows from where the skimmer skidded before tipping fully.

Background: desert floor extends behind the wreck. In the deep distance, far off-frame to the upper edge of the panel, the silhouette of the fallen protagonist ship is faintly suggested — a low collapsed shape on the horizon, partially obscured by lingering dust plume rising. Dense cross-hatched golden hour sky.

Late afternoon sun from upper-left rim-lights the curve of the overturned skimmer's hull and the female raider's shoulder and the loose strands of her hair. Her face is mostly in cross-hatched shadow with the rim-lit edge catching the highlight.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow indicating low-medium camera angle (slightly lower than the female raider's torso level — looking slightly up at her emergence). Red pencil emphasis marks around the cough — short radial lines around her mouth labeled "cough burst + dust puff." Red pencil arrow on her arm-push gesture labeled "leverage out of wreck."

Caption strip below panel: "SHOT 2 / PANEL 2 — MEDIUM-WIDE / FEMALE RAIDER CLIMBS OUT OF FULLY OVERTURNED SKIMMER / 50mm prime / handheld with light shake / SFX: muffled metallic creak from skimmer hull settling, fabric scrape, sharp coughing burst from female raider, sand grains pattering off her hair and clothes / ACTION: pushes upper body free of wreck, coughs hard, dust briefly visible around her face"

**HARD CUT.**

PANEL 3 (bottom-left cell) — SHOT 3: LIU HUI'S CROUCH-AND-LEAP, LOW-ANGLE GROUND-LEVEL TO OVERHEAD VANISH:
Scene composition: extreme low-angle ground-level shot — the camera is placed flat on the desert sand, looking straight up. Liu Hui stands directly above the camera, looking down toward it with the lens. The entire frame is dominated by the underside view of Liu Hui's massive fusion-form armored body — the camera sees up between his legs and up at his chest, neck, and the underside of his jaw and chin.

The framing shows: the desert sand floor at the very bottom edge of frame extending outward (the ground around Liu Hui's planted feet). Liu Hui's massive bronze-mechanized armored boots planted on either side of the camera position, occupying the lower-left and lower-right corners of the frame. His armored legs rise upward through the frame in dramatic foreshortening — the legs' bronze armor segments visible getting wider near the hips as they recede into perspective. His armored torso, chest, and the bronze spiral horns on his shoulders are visible higher up in the frame, dramatically foreshortened with the chest mass dominating the middle of the frame. His face — looking downward toward the camera — is visible at the top of the frame, the chin and lower jaw most prominent, eye-patch over the right eye, the un-patched left eye narrowed in concentration, his face only partially visible due to the extreme perspective.

Liu Hui is in the crouch-prep position: knees bent, body lowered into a deep loaded crouch — like a standing-long-jump pre-leap stance, both feet planted shoulder-width apart, weight loaded into the legs, arms lowered to his sides slightly behind his body. The cyan core at his chest is visible from below as a strong glow point on the underside of his chest plate. The cyan cores at his shoulders also glow.

This is the MOMENT BEFORE the leap — the storyboard captures the crouch-prep pose. A small inset diagram in the upper-right corner of the panel (drawn as a tiny secondary sketch, smaller than 1/5 of the panel area) shows the SAME camera looking up an instant later — Liu Hui has launched into the air, leaving only the empty sky frame with his receding silhouette small and going up toward the upper portion of the inset's frame, with a massive sand-dust eruption blooming from where his feet just left the ground. Below the inset's frame is a tiny caption: "AFTER LEAP — VANISHES UPWARD."

Background sky visible in the gaps between Liu Hui's body and limbs: dense cross-hatched golden hour atmosphere, the late afternoon sun visible as a small bright disc in the upper-left region of the sky where his shoulder doesn't block it.

Camera annotation: red pencil triangle at the very bottom of the panel indicating "camera planted flat on sand / looking straight up." Red pencil downward arrow on Liu Hui's loaded crouch labeled "weight loaded / pre-leap crouch / Hulk-style standing jump prep." Red pencil upward arrows around Liu Hui's body indicating the imminent leap direction labeled "leap vector upward + toward fallen ship direction." Red pencil radial burst marks around Liu Hui's feet labeled "imminent sand eruption."

Caption strip below panel: "SHOT 3 / PANEL 3 — EXTREME LOW-ANGLE GROUND-LEVEL / LIU HUI'S CROUCH-AND-LEAP / 18mm ultra-wide / camera planted flat on sand looking straight up / SFX: deep loaded armor compression groan, low gear-grinding from shoulder spiral horns, cyan core building hum, then explosive sand THOOM burst as legs release, body whoosh upward / ACTION: deep crouch loaded prep, then leap straight up — Liu Hui vanishes out the top of the frame, leaving sand eruption"

SHOOTING PLAN (bottom-right cell) — TOP-DOWN BIRD'S EYE OVERVIEW:
Drawn as a clean top-down schematic diagram on the aged paper. Layout uses the entire cell width effectively. Upper portion of the diagram (deep distance direction): the fallen protagonist ship drawn as a rectangular schematic silhouette tilted forward with several leg-marks splayed in collapsed kneeling positions — marked with a small dust-plume cross-hatched cloud above it labeled "FALLEN SHIP / SETTLING DUST." 

Middle-foreground area of the diagram: Liu Hui drawn as a small figure-outline standing firm on the sand, his orientation facing toward the fallen ship in the upper direction. Liu Hui's position labeled "LIU HUI ANCHOR / CROUCH-LEAP POSITION."

Around Liu Hui's position, scattered in the middle-foreground area: three small schematic outlines of tipped-over sand skimmers. One labeled "TIPPED SKIMMER A / OLD + YOUNG RAIDER PROPPED HERE" with two tiny figure-outlines beside it. Another labeled "FULLY OVERTURNED SKIMMER / FEMALE RAIDER CRAWLING OUT" with one tiny figure-outline at its edge. A third labeled "TIPPED SKIMMER B (FAR)" drawn smaller and further back.

Three red pencil triangles around the action area marking the camera positions for each of the 3 shots: 
- Shot 1 (medium-wide on tipped skimmer with old + young raider) positioned facing the raiders on the side opposite their eye-line direction
- Shot 2 (medium-wide on fully overturned skimmer with female raider) positioned at the side of her emergence point
- Shot 3 (extreme low-angle ground-level under Liu Hui) marked as a red triangle pointing straight up from a position directly underneath Liu Hui's standing point
Each red triangle is labeled with the shot number. 

A dashed red pencil straight line running from Liu Hui upward through the diagram toward the fallen ship — this is the 180-degree axis line, labeled "180° AXIS LINE / LIU HUI → FALLEN SHIP DIRECTION."

A red pencil curved arrow from Liu Hui's position curving upward and toward the fallen ship direction labeled "LIU HUI LEAP TRAJECTORY → toward ship deck (next segment)."

Hand-lettered labels on the diagram: "180° AXIS LINE", "S1 / S2 / S3 CAMERA POSITIONS", "LIU HUI ANCHOR", "FALLEN SHIP DEEP DIRECTION", "3 TIPPED/OVERTURNED SKIMMERS WITH 3 RAIDERS", "LEAP TRAJECTORY OUTGOING."

Caption strip below the shooting plan cell: "SHOOTING PLAN — 180° AXIS PRESERVED FROM SEG 9B / 3 hard-cut shot positions / S1 + S2 are reaction beats on raiders / S3 is Liu Hui's transition into next segment"

PRODUCTION NOTES at bottom edge of overall image (hand-lettered serif block letters along the bottom margin of the aged paper, outside the panel grid): "WANWU SHENG / EPISODE 1 / SEGMENT 10A — AFTERMATH & LEAP / multi-shot hard cut sequence / continuity: prior segment 9B closes on the fallen ship and Liu Hui standing firm after release / this segment opens on the raider aftermath beats and ends with Liu Hui's crouch-leap toward the fallen ship / next segment 10B opens on the ship deck POV as Liu Hui lands"

Hard constraints:
- NO digital painting style, NO 3D render, NO color overlays except red colored pencil for arrows and annotations
- NO photographic elements, NO AI-style smooth gradients
- Strong golden-hour desert high-contrast lighting in all panels — dense cross-hatch shadows + selective bright untouched paper highlights, NOT evenly lit
- Each storyboard panel's inner frame strictly 16:9 aspect ratio
- Total image strictly 21:9 aspect ratio
- All hand-lettered text in confident serif block letters with slight unevenness — readable but not machine-printed
- Aged off-white paper texture throughout with subtle edge wear
- Old raider has long braided ponytail + long full beard; Young raider has short cropped hair + short stubble — these two are visually distinct from each other in PANEL 1
- Female raider has high ponytail + brass goggles on forehead — clearly different from the other two
- Liu Hui's crouch in PANEL 3 must read as Hulk-style standing-long-jump preparation — loaded but not strained or laboring
- PANEL 3's extreme low-angle ground-level perspective must be unambiguous — the camera is on the sand looking straight up, Liu Hui towers over the camera


### 第57条｜Layout: top-down schematic floor plan

A hand-drawn top-down bird's eye view blocking diagram on aged off-white paper, in the style of a professional film production staging diagram. Image aspect ratio strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape format.

Layout: top-down schematic floor plan of a ship deck interior local area, drawn in confident graphite pencil with selective red colored pencil annotations. Aged off-white paper background with slight edge wear and faint smudges suggesting heavy production use.

Title at top center, hand-lettered confident block serif: "WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 10F — STAGING DIAGRAM"

Below title, smaller hand-lettered subtitle: "SHIP DECK BLOCKING / 5 CHARACTERS + ENVIRONMENT ANCHORS / CAMERA REVEAL PATH"

DRAWING TECHNIQUE: confident graphite pencil with clear top-down perspective. Cross-hatching for shading where appropriate. Selective red colored pencil ONLY for camera positions, motion vectors, character labels, and reveal path annotations. Hand-lettered labels in slightly uneven serif block letters.

THE DIAGRAM CONTENT:

Main composition: top-down bird's eye view of a section of the ship deck. The deck floor occupies most of the diagram space, drawn with subtle wood plank lines suggesting deck planking. Environmental elements are drawn as labeled top-down outlines.

Environmental anchors (drawn as top-down silhouettes with labels):
- A large vertical mast pillar drawn as a thick circle/octagon shape near the left-center area of the deck, labeled "MAIN MAST / 主桅杆"
- The bronze ship's wheel drawn as a circular outline with spokes radiating outward, positioned in the upper-right area of the deck, labeled "BRONZE SHIP WHEEL / 青铜船舵"
- Some scattered deck elements drawn as small outlines: a coil of rope labeled "rope coil," a deck cleat labeled "cleat," a hatch labeled "grating hatch"
- The deck's outer edge / railing drawn along one side of the diagram, labeled "DECK RAIL / OUTSIDE (DESERT VIEW BEYOND)"

Character positions (each character drawn as a top-down figure-outline silhouette with name label):
- LIU HUI: standing figure outline positioned right next to the main mast pillar in the LEFT-FOREGROUND area of the diagram (closest to the viewer's bottom edge), facing the mast direction. Label: "LIU HUI / 刘回 (foreground)"
- JIANG LIU: horizontal figure outline lying FLAT ON HIS BACK on the deck floor in the LEFT-MIDDLE area of the diagram, body axis roughly perpendicular to the viewer, his head pointing toward one direction (mark "head" and "feet" on the figure). Label: "JIANG LIU / 江流 (unconscious supine)"
- BIRD-DRAGON: standing figure outline positioned in the RIGHT-MIDDLE area of the diagram, with a small arrow on its head showing the bird-dragon's head looking up and around (label "looking up + glancing left/right / 仰脖东张西望"). Label: "BIRD-DRAGON / 机械鸟龙"
- MECHANICAL SAIGA: standing figure outline positioned in the UPPER-RIGHT area of the diagram, drawn as a smaller four-legged silhouette. The Saiga is roughly the size of a large dog or small pony — DRAWN SMALLER than the human figure outlines to emphasize its size. A small arrow at its head indicates chewing motion (label "chewing motion / 咀嚼中"). Label: "MECHANICAL SAIGA / 机械赛加 (to lieutenant's chest height)"
- LIEUTENANT: standing figure outline positioned right next to the Saiga in the UPPER-RIGHT area, facing the Saiga direction with arms crossed. Label: "LIEUTENANT / 副手 (arms crossed)"

Camera positions and reveal path:
- A red colored pencil camera triangle marked at the STARTING POSITION: positioned very close to Liu Hui's figure in the left-foreground, with its sight-line cone aimed at Liu Hui's side face (the close-up starting frame). Label: "CAMERA START / close-up on Liu Hui's side face / 50mm"

- A red colored pencil camera triangle marked at the ENDING POSITION: positioned further back and slightly elevated relative to the start position, drawn larger to indicate wider lens, with its sight-line cone widening to encompass the entire deck area including all 5 characters. Label: "CAMERA END / master wide shot / framing reveals all 5 characters"

- A red colored pencil CURVED ARROW connecting the start position to the end position, drawn as a smooth curve showing the camera's reveal path. The arrow includes annotations along its length: "slow dolly back," "slow pan," "slow tilt" — labeled "SPIELBERG REVEAL PATH / steady combined motion."

Sight-line annotations:
- A red dashed line from Liu Hui's figure to the mast labeled "Liu Hui's attention / inspecting mast like new property owner"
- A red dashed line from Lieutenant's figure pointing toward Liu Hui's direction across the diagram labeled "Lieutenant's dialogue direction → '老大,那小子怎么办?'"
- A red dashed line from Liu Hui's figure pointing toward Lieutenant labeled "Liu Hui's response → '老样子呗,芯片丢进沙子里,零件全部拆了卖掉。'"

Final master shot composition annotation:
- A red colored pencil dashed rectangle drawn around the entire diagram representing the final master wide shot frame, labeled "FINAL MASTER SHOT FRAME / off-axis composition / NOT centered"

Hand-lettered legend in the lower-left corner of the diagram:
- "S = Start camera position"
- "E = End camera position"
- "Dashed red lines = sight-lines + dialogue directions"
- "Curved red arrow = camera reveal path"

Production note at the bottom margin of the image (hand-lettered along bottom edge): "WANWU SHENG / EPISODE 1 / SEGMENT 10F — DECK MASTER REVEAL / Spielberg-style single take reveal cinematography / camera opens on Liu Hui's intimate close-up then dollies back + pans + tilts to reveal full deck master shot during dialogue exchange / continuity: prior segment 10E closes with Saiga swallowing crab ball"

Hard constraints:

- Pencil graphite for the diagram base
- Black ink for the title and main labels

### 第59条｜Looney Tunes Tom-and-Jerry comedic cha

Shot on 35mm Kodak Vision3 500T film stock + analog film grain visible + practical light only + Christopher Doyle cinematography + Wong Kar-wai painterly desaturation + photochemical film texture + slight halation on highlights + organic film noise + heavy atmospheric haze + tactile real-world materials + film print look not digital cinema look + photorealistic film still aesthetic + real physical scene captured through a camera lens + not a 3D render.

Looney Tunes Tom-and-Jerry comedic chase pacing + Coen Brothers timing + Wes Anderson dry deadpan deck setting + 16:9 anamorphic + golden hour lighting with strong light/shadow contrast on ship deck.

整段约9秒一镜到底single continuous take+无硬切。

用白噪音和动作音效作为整段声学底层+不要配抒情背景音乐+sound design only ambient and action sounds NO background music。

整段画面节拍分两段:

前半段画面节拍:
@图2 独立鸟龙在画面一侧出现+鸟龙的青铜机械身体+鸟头机械头盔+前额#4FC3D9青色发光机械眼锁定地面上某个位置+#6B4A2E赭红羽毛翅膀部分收拢+#8E6F3D磨光铜高光的机械喙朝向地面方向。

地面上@图3 萌螃蟹球体静止地停在甲板木板上+球体表面紧密合拢+#7A6845铜青绿外壳与#846035古代暗金符号刻面在#F0B670金色直射光下泛光+#4FC3D9青色关节缝隙微微发光。

鸟龙低下头部+伸出机械喙朝萌螃蟹球啄出第一下+鸟龙的青铜机械喙以鸟啄食的动作精准戳向螃蟹球+伴随metallic peck sharp tap金属喙对金属球的清脆敲击声"叮——"。

萌螃蟹球被喙戳中的瞬间被弹开+在甲板木板上滚动滑出一段距离+伴随bronze ball rolling on wood deck青铜球在木质甲板上滚动的低频rolling声+滚动方向是画面一侧+萌螃蟹球同时发出滑稽哀嚎拟声"啊啊——"参考@音频1 音色但是Tom and Jerry被惨揍时的卡通哀嚎风格。

鸟龙快速调整身位+移动到萌螃蟹球的新位置旁边+鸟龙的爪足在甲板木板上发出claw scratching on wood deck爪子刮木地板的声音+鸟龙再次低头伸喙朝萌螃蟹球啄出第二下+伴随第二次metallic peck金属敲击声"叮——"。

后半段画面节拍:

萌螃蟹球被啄中弹起+这次以更急速的方向滚动+滚向画面相反一侧+鸟龙紧跟着追+鸟龙的羽毛翅膀稍微张开half-open posture+赭红羽毛在金色直射光下泛光+鸟龙的爪足踏在甲板上的thud声+萌螃蟹球持续发出滑稽哀嚎拟声"哦哦——""呃啊——"的Tom and Jerry风格卡通哀嚎。

萌螃蟹球加速滚动+撞到甲板上的某个物体+可能撞到桅杆基座或绳索盘+发生反弹+萌螃蟹球弹起一下然后落地继续滚+伴随bronze ball hits wood post青铜球撞木质柱状物的咚响"咚"+萌螃蟹球的滑稽哀嚎在撞击瞬间提高一个音阶"哎哟——"。

萌螃蟹球继续滚动+从画面一侧的边缘滚出画外+鸟龙也跟着冲到画面边缘但因为体型太大没法完全跟上+鸟龙的头部探出画面边缘追+萌螃蟹球从短暂消失在画面外约一秒+然后再从画面另一侧或者同一侧的边缘滚回画内+伴随萌螃蟹球继续发出哀嚎拟声+萌螃蟹球的滚回方向像是它撞到什么又反弹回来的物理逻辑+滚回画面后鸟龙立刻转身追+爪足在甲板上发出快速thud thud thud声+萌螃蟹球再次撞到甲板上另一个物体+发出再次的青铜撞响"叮咚"。

整段背景音轨:
- 背景大漠风声远处呼啸
- 背景甲板木板slight creak远处偶尔
- 萌螃蟹哀嚎+鸟龙啄击+滚动撞击+爪足踏木声构成主导前景音效层
- 全段不要任何人声对白+不要任何画外音+不要任何human dialogue+only ambient and action sound effects

末帧:鸟龙再次伸喙朝萌螃蟹球啄过去+萌螃蟹球持续发出哀嚎拟声+画面在此节拍上自然结束+所有声音不切断保持持续状态。

NOT slow motion+NOT speed ramping+NOT居中构图+NOT萌螃蟹弹开变形为螃蟹形态(本段萌螃蟹严格保持球体形态全程)+NOT萌螃蟹露出蓝色眼睛或八条腿(本段萌螃蟹严格球体外观)+NOT硬切多镜头(本段一镜到底)+无字幕+无水印。


### 第60条｜Layout: 2x2 grid, 4 cells, each cell s

A hand-drawn pencil sketch storyboard on aged off-white paper, professional film production style. Image aspect ratio strictly 16:9.

Layout: 2x2 grid, 4 cells, each cell strictly 16:9 landscape with thick hand-drawn black ink frame border. Top row: Panel 1, Panel 2. Bottom row: Panel 3, Shooting Plan diagram. Beneath each cell a hand-lettered caption strip in serif block letters.

DRAWING TECHNIQUE: loose graphite pencil with confident architectural perspective. Cross-hatching for shading. Selective red colored pencil ONLY for arrows, emphasis marks, camera positions, motion vectors, annotations. Aged off-white paper with slight edge wear. Hand-lettered captions in slightly uneven serif block letters.

GLOBAL LIGHTING: late afternoon golden hour, low sun key light from one consistent direction. Strong rim-lighting on character silhouettes, deep shadow zones opposite. Dense cross-hatching for shadows, bright untouched paper for highlights. Strong tonal contrast — NOT evenly lit.

GLOBAL COMPOSITION RULE: avoid centered symmetric framing. All 3 panels use off-axis, asymmetric, rule-of-thirds, or diagonal compositions. NO panel has the main subject dead-center.

Style references: Marvel Studios action board, Hollywood film storyboards, Mad Max Fury Road character beat boards, Looney Tunes whip pan transition boards.

Title at top center, hand-lettered confident block serif: "WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 10D — INVITATION FOUND + LIU HUI'S ORDER + WHIP PAN TO STUNNED PAIR"

Below title, smaller hand-lettered subtitle: "3 SHOTS / 2 HARD CUTS + 1 WHIP PAN / SHIP DECK / LIEUTENANT FINDS INVITATION → LIU HUI INSPECTS → ORDERS BIRD-DRAGON DISMANTLED → WHIP PAN REVEALS COMEDIC FREEZE"

PANEL 1 (top-left) — SHOT 1: SLIGHTLY LOW-OVERHEAD ANGLE / LIEUTENANT KNEELS BESIDE UNCONSCIOUS JIANG LIU, PULLS INVITATION FROM HIS GARMENT:
Slightly low overhead angle (camera positioned ~20-30 degrees above ground level, looking slightly down at the deck floor area). Tight medium shot focused on Lieutenant's kneeling body. Off-axis composition: Lieutenant occupies center-left to right portion of frame, Jiang Liu only partially visible at the LEFT edge of frame showing localized body parts (torso, part of upper arm, edge of his cream-colored billowing sleeve) — Jiang Liu's face and head NOT in frame.

JIANG LIU LOCAL VISIBLE: lying FLAT ON HIS BACK on the deck planks (face-up supine position), wine-red leather vest over cream loose-sleeved billowing shirt, wine-red trousers — only partial torso and arm visible at the left edge of frame, not the head.

LIEUTENANT (kneeling beside Jiang Liu, occupying center-to-right of frame): middle-aged woman with dark hair in high braided ponytail wrapped with tribal cloth headband decorated with beads and small charms, scar across face, sun-tanned copper skin, worn deep-brown leather sleeveless top, cream neck scarf, tribal blood-red ribbon and crimson cloth accents on arms and waist, bronze mechanical prosthetic right leg from hip to foot. Twin curved blades on hip belt (not drawn). Kneeling on one knee beside Jiang Liu.

ACTION CAPTURED: Lieutenant's hand has just pulled an invitation letter object out from Jiang Liu's garment, mid-action. She holds the invitation in her hand, about to raise her arm up. Her other hand may be steadying on Jiang Liu's chest area.

INVITATION LETTER: drawn as a small rectangular ornate object in Lieutenant's hand — its exact visual design will be specified by the asset reference at video production stage, but for storyboard purposes draw as a small flat rectangular letter-shaped item, roughly the size of a playing card, with subtle ornamental edges suggesting an imperial document.

In the final beat of this panel: Lieutenant's hand is raising up to extend the invitation toward the UPPER-RIGHT direction of the off-frame space (where Liu Hui stands off-frame). Lieutenant's eye-line follows her raised hand looking up toward the upper-right off-frame direction.

Background: deck planks #6B4830 receding in slight perspective. Dust motes in the air. Some scattered debris (loose fabric scrap, broken piece). Ship's structural elements (rope coil, mast base, deck cleats) visible in background depths.

Lighting: golden hour direct light from one direction rim-lighting Lieutenant's shoulder and the raised hand holding the invitation. Cross-hatched shadow on the opposite side.

Red pencil annotations: triangle in lower-left indicating slight low-overhead camera position. Arrow on Lieutenant's hand showing the pull-and-raise motion labeled "pulls invitation from garment → raises toward off-frame Liu Hui." Eye-line dashed arrow from Lieutenant up to upper-right off-frame labeled "Lieutenant looks up at Liu Hui."

Caption: "SHOT 1 / PANEL 1 — SLIGHT LOW-OVERHEAD ANGLE / LIEUTENANT FINDS INVITATION / 50mm prime / camera mostly static with handheld micro-shake / SFX: fabric rustle as letter is pulled, Lieutenant: '老大,找到了,皇帝的邀请函。' / ACTION: Lieutenant pulls invitation from Jiang Liu's garment, raises arm to extend it toward off-frame Liu Hui, eye-line follows hand"

PANEL 2 (top-right) — SHOT 2: SHALLOW DOF CLOSE-UP / LIU HUI EXAMINES INVITATION + TURNS HEAD TO YELL:
Close-up framing, upper body only (waist-up to head). Shallow depth of field — Liu Hui sharp in focus, background heavily blurred bokeh. Off-axis composition: Liu Hui occupies the right 2/3 of frame, body angled at 3/4 view, his face slightly turned toward the invitation in his hand (which he holds at chest level).

LIU HUI in DEFAULT FORM upper body: bare upper torso with tribal tattoos on shoulders and chest, cream tribal scarf wrapped loosely around neck trailing over one shoulder, tribal blood-red scarf accents, short spiky black hair with forehead braid cord, eye-patch over right eye with scar through brow, bronze mechanical prosthetic right arm visible from shoulder down.

ACTION CAPTURED: Liu Hui holds the invitation letter at chest level with both hands (flesh left hand + prosthetic right hand jointly), his single visible eye narrowed in concentrated examination, his head slightly tilted down to look at the invitation. Expression: focused, scrutinizing.

BACKGROUND BLUR: heavily out-of-focus bokeh. From the bottom of frame to the top, the background reads as follows (all in shallow DOF blur):
- Bottom edge of frame: out-of-focus ship's inner railing or deck edge structure (rendered as soft pencil strokes with reduced contrast)
- Middle band: out-of-focus desert horizon line beyond the ship (warm pale tan band)
- Upper band: out-of-focus golden hour sky in #C8581F sunset orange (rendered with soft cross-hatching)

The deep depth of background allows the viewer to read "Liu Hui is standing on a ship deck with outside desert view beyond" without needing sharp background detail.

TRANSITION BEAT IN THIS PANEL: in the final beat, Liu Hui suddenly snaps his head to the LEFT (toward another off-frame direction where the bird-dragon + crab chase is happening). Expression shifts from focused-scrutinizing to irritated-impatient. He's about to yell.

Lighting: golden hour direct light rim-lighting Liu Hui's shoulder, edge of face, and the invitation letter. Cross-hatched shadow on the side of his face away from the light.

Red pencil annotations: triangle indicating close-up camera position with shallow DOF marker labeled "shallow DOF / bg blur." Arrow showing Liu Hui's head snap-turn labeled "head snaps left → irritated." Annotation near mouth labeled "yells: 够了!把那只青铜兽拆了!"

Caption: "SHOT 2 / PANEL 2 — SHALLOW DOF CLOSE-UP / LIU HUI EXAMINES + ORDERS / 85mm prime shallow DOF / camera static with handheld micro-shake / SFX: invitation rustle as it changes hands, then off-frame distant bird-dragon pecking + crab whining continues throughout, Liu Hui: '够了!把那只青铜兽拆了!' / ACTION: receives invitation, examines closely with narrowed eye, then suddenly snaps head left and yells the order"

PANEL 3 (bottom-left) — SHOT 3: POST-WHIP-PAN REVEAL / BIRD-DRAGON + CRAB BALL FREEZE STARING AT CAMERA:
Medium-wide shot of the ship deck open area. Off-axis composition: bird-dragon and crab ball positioned in the left 2/3 of frame, the rest of the deck space + ship's wheel (bronze ship wheel) visible in the right portion as deep background context.

This panel is the END of a fast WHIP PAN from Panel 2's framing — the camera has just whipped from Liu Hui's close-up position over to this new deck area framing in roughly 0.5 seconds. The whip pan transition is indicated in the captions and shooting plan.

BIRD-DRAGON (positioned in middle-left of frame): the independent mechanical bird-dragon, war-mount sized mechanical raptor form, full bronze mech armor, mechanical raptor head helmet, single #4FC3D9 cyan-glowing eye on forehead, mechanical beak with #8E6F3D polished bronze highlight, partly extended wings showing #6B4A2E ochre crimson feathers with #2E1F15 dark brown feather tips. Posed in mid-chase posture: body leaning forward, neck extended, beak forward, claws planted on deck with slight forward weight. Body language reads as "about to peck/chase." BUT — head and eye are turned toward the CAMERA, eye locked on camera, completely frozen mid-action.

CRAB BALL (positioned slightly to the right of bird-dragon on the deck, lower in frame): the bronze crab ball in its closed sphere form, roughly head-sized sphere, #7A6845 bronze olive shell with #846035 ancient gold etched glyph facets and #4FC3D9 cyan joint seam glow. Strictly maintained in ball form — no legs, no eyes visible. Positioned on the deck where it was just rolling. Frozen completely still, no rolling motion. Although crab ball has no visible face, its overall posture/orientation reads as "staring at camera" by slight tilt toward the camera direction.

BOTH bird-dragon and crab ball are completely FROZEN — caught mid-action by the sudden whip pan — they both look toward the camera in absurd comedic stillness. This is the comedy peak beat — chase-and-prey freeze together in Tom-and-Jerry style.

Background: deep deck space visible behind the pair, with the BRONZE SHIP WHEEL visible in the deep right portion of frame as a clear environmental landmark. Some other deck details (rope coils, deck cleats, perhaps part of mast base) visible. Background recedes in slight perspective.

Lighting: golden hour direct light rim-lighting bird-dragon's wing feathers and crab ball's curve. Cross-hatched shadow on opposite sides. The bronze ship wheel in deep background catches some light highlights.

Red pencil annotations: triangle indicating medium-wide camera position post-whip-pan. Whip-pan motion indicator at the panel's left edge labeled "WHIP PAN ENDS HERE from Liu Hui's POV direction." Frozen-still emphasis lines around bird-dragon and crab ball labeled "FREEZE BEAT / both stare at camera." Arrow pointing at the bronze ship wheel in deep background labeled "Bronze Ship Wheel (continuity anchor)."

Caption: "SHOT 3 / PANEL 3 — POST-WHIP-PAN MEDIUM-WIDE / COMEDIC FREEZE / 35mm wide / whip pan from Panel 2 in ~0.5 seconds settles into static framing with handheld micro-shake / SFX: whip pan whoosh + sudden silence as both freeze, no more pecking, no more crab whining — comedic dead silence / ACTION: post-whip-pan reveal of bird-dragon mid-chase + crab ball mid-roll, both freeze and stare at camera, holds for beat"

SHOOTING PLAN (bottom-right) — TOP-DOWN BIRD'S EYE OVERVIEW:
Top-down schematic diagram of the ship deck local area on the aged paper.

Layout shows the deck floor space. Schematic outlines from top to bottom:
- Upper portion: ship's outer railing edge + beyond it a label "DESERT VIEW BEYOND"
- Middle portion (deck open area): on one side, a small figure-outline of LIEUTENANT kneeling beside a horizontal outline of JIANG LIU lying flat on his back; on the OTHER side a small figure-outline of LIU HUI standing upright
- Bottom-middle portion (other deck area): two small schematic outlines marked "BIRD-DRAGON" and "CRAB BALL" positioned near a small circular outline labeled "BRONZE SHIP WHEEL"

Three red pencil triangles marking camera positions for the 3 shots:
- Shot 1 (slight low-overhead on Lieutenant + Jiang Liu) — positioned looking down at Lieutenant's kneeling area
- Shot 2 (shallow DOF close-up on Liu Hui) — positioned facing Liu Hui's standing position with arrow indicating shallow DOF cone
- Shot 3 (post-whip-pan medium-wide on bird-dragon + crab) — positioned facing the bird-dragon + crab area

Red pencil curved arrow connecting Shot 2's camera position to Shot 3's camera position, labeled "WHIP PAN ~0.5s — Liu Hui's POV swings from invitation examination to the chase area."

Red pencil dashed eye-line from Liu Hui's figure-outline to the bird-dragon + crab area labeled "Liu Hui's POV direction → triggers whip pan."

Hand-lettered labels: "S1 / S2 / S3 CAMERA POSITIONS," "JIANG LIU FLAT ON BACK," "LIEUTENANT KNEELS," "LIU HUI STANDS," "WHIP PAN PATH," "BRONZE SHIP WHEEL."

Caption: "SHOOTING PLAN — 3 shots on ship deck / S1+S2 are hard-cut on Lieutenant→Liu Hui exchange / S2→S3 is whip pan triggered by Liu Hui's head-turn yell / S3 lands on comedic freeze with bird-dragon + crab ball"

Hard constraints:
- NO digital painting, NO 3D render, NO color overlays except red pencil for annotations
- NO photographic elements, NO smooth gradients
- Strong golden-hour high-contrast — dense cross-hatch shadows + bright untouched paper highlights, NOT evenly lit
- Each panel strictly 16:9, total image strictly 16:9 with 2x2 grid
- Aged off-white paper texture with subtle edge wear
- NONE of 3 panels use centered symmetric composition
- Jiang Liu in PANEL 1: lying FLAT ON HIS BACK (face-up supine position), NOT side-lying, only partial body visible at left edge of frame (head and face NOT in frame)
- Lieutenant in PANEL 1: high braided ponytail with beads, scar across face, bronze mechanical prosthetic right leg
- Liu Hui in PANEL 2: short spiky black hair with forehead braid cord, eye-patch over right eye, bronze mechanical prosthetic right arm, bare upper torso with tribal tattoos, cream tribal scarf
- Liu Hui in PANEL 2 background must be shallow DOF / heavily blurred bokeh — bottom band reads as out-of-focus ship railing, middle band as desert horizon, upper band as sunset sky #C8581F
- Bird-dragon in PANEL 3: mechanical raptor form, ochre crimson wing feathers, dark brown feather tips, cyan eye, polished bronze beak — FROZEN mid-chase posture but head turned toward camera
- Crab ball in PANEL 3: strictly bronze sphere form (NO legs, NO eyes visible), with ancient gold facets and cyan joint seam glow — FROZEN, no rolling motion
- Bronze Ship Wheel must be visible in PANEL 3 deep background as environmental landmark
- The WHIP PAN motion from PANEL 2 to PANEL 3 is indicated via red pencil annotations in both panels and the shooting plan


### 第61条｜medium中景+焦段50mm prime lens+rectilinear

Shot on 35mm Kodak Vision3 500T film stock + analog film grain visible + practical light only + Christopher Doyle cinematography + Wong Kar-wai painterly desaturation + photochemical film texture + slight halation on highlights + organic film noise + heavy atmospheric haze + tactile real-world materials + film print look not digital cinema look + photorealistic film still aesthetic + real physical scene captured through a camera lens + not a 3D render.

Quiet investigation beat + sudden command moment + Looney Tunes whip pan to comedic freeze + Coen Brothers timing + 16:9 anamorphic + golden hour lighting on ship deck.

用白噪音和动作音效作为整段声学底层+不要配抒情背景音乐+sound design only ambient and action sounds NO background music+不要任何鸟啄声或螃蟹哀嚎声(后期音效自行叠加)。

镜头中段到末段阶段:鸟龙与螃蟹球继续保持完全静止状态+frozen completely still+定格在被刘回呼喊吓到的呆滞状态+整个画面保持静止+绝对dead silence死寂+只有非常微弱的大漠风声呼啸残留+暗示这是一个完整的Tom-and-Jerry风格的喜剧呆愣beat:被骂的鸟+被啄的螃蟹一起呆滞看向镜头+定格在荒诞的对视瞬间。


### 第62条｜Tom and Jerry comedic peek + strugglin

Shot on 35mm Kodak Vision3 500T film stock + analog film grain visible + practical light only + Christopher Doyle cinematography + Wong Kar-wai painterly desaturation + photochemical film texture + slight halation on highlights + organic film noise + heavy atmospheric haze + tactile real-world materials + film print look not digital cinema look + photorealistic film still aesthetic + real physical scene captured through a camera lens + not a 3D render.

Tom and Jerry comedic peek + struggling captive + Wes Anderson deadpan executioner + reluctant fate beat + 16:9 anamorphic + golden hour lighting on ship deck.

用白噪音和动作音效作为整段声学底层+不要配抒情背景音乐+sound design only ambient and action sounds NO background music+不要任何鸟啄声(后期音效自行叠加)。

后段末段阶段:副手抱着萌螃蟹走到画面深处某位置+这一刻机械赛加@图3 从画面侧方入画+赛加的体型仅到副手胸口高度+赛加站立在副手前方一步距离的位置+赛加的长喙赛加头朝向副手与萌螃蟹的方向+赛加的前额#4FC3D9青色发光机械眼锁定萌螃蟹。

赛加入画的瞬间+相机开始smooth dolly back丝滑拉远运动+从medium close-up逐步拉远到medium wide+画面范围从原本只拍副手腰部以上扩大到能容纳副手全身+怀中挣扎萌螃蟹+面前站立的赛加三者+相机拉远过程平稳流畅而不是抖动+handheld抖动感在拉远阶段降低保持稳定。

吞下瞬间:赛加张开机械喙mechanical beak opens wide+露出内部#3A2A1F青铜阴影深处的喉咙结构+伴随mechanical jaw hinge opens机械喙张开的金属铰链声"嘎吱——"+副手将怀中的萌螃蟹送向赛加张开的喙中+萌螃蟹被送入赛加喙内的瞬间台词"别别别别别——"急剧拉高音调到最高+然后赛加的机械喙猛地合拢bite closes+伴随mechanical jaw snap shut机械喙合拢的脆响"咔!"+萌螃蟹的求饶声瞬间被切断完全消失+赛加的喉咙处可见鼓动一下表示吞咽gulp swallow motion+伴随mechanical throat swallow gulp机械喉咙吞咽的低频声"咕咚"。

末帧:赛加站立姿态+机械喙合拢恢复闭嘴状态+前额#4FC3D9青色发光机械眼平静看向画面外某方向+副手抱萌螃蟹的双手已经空了+副手站在赛加旁边保持冷淡面无表情+画面整体进入brief silence beat短暂的静默节拍+只有背景大漠风声+背景甲板木板slight creak。

NOT slow motion+NOT speed ramping+NOT居中构图+NOT副手对萌螃蟹有怜悯或犹豫表情(本段副手严格冷淡面无表情deadpan)+NOT萌螃蟹台词语速过慢(本段萌螃蟹严格超极速天津话约每秒7字以上)+NOT赛加表现出凶猛或攻击性(本段赛加张嘴吞下是平静执行命令的动作而非攻击)+无字幕+无水印。

Below each swatch, the HEX color code is printed in small clean monospace black typography, centered under the swatch.

Above each HEX code (between swatch and HEX), a brief descriptive label of what this color represents in the scene, in small sans-serif black typography.

### 第64条｜Layout: 2 columns x 2 rows grid on age

A hand-drawn pencil sketch storyboard on aged off-white paper, in the style of professional film production storyboards. Total image aspect ratio strictly 21:9.

Layout: 2 columns x 2 rows grid on aged paper background. Top-left and top-right and bottom-left cells are storyboard panels each strictly 16:9 horizontal landscape format with thick hand-drawn black ink frame border. Bottom-right cell is a shooting plan diagram (top-down bird's eye view of the desert with camera positions and 180-degree axis line marked).

Beneath each storyboard panel inner frame is a hand-lettered caption strip showing panel number, lens specification, shot type, and brief SFX/action notes in serif block letters slightly uneven.

The drawing technique is loose graphite pencil with confident architectural perspective and clear cinematic composition. Sketches are deliberately gestural but every spatial relationship and prop detail is clearly readable. Selective red colored pencil arrows and emphasis marks indicate movement direction, force vectors, and key dramatic beats.

LIGHTING NOTE FOR ALL PANELS: late afternoon desert golden hour, low sun key light from upper-left 30 degrees. Dense cross-hatching dominates most surfaces representing the shadow base of the warm desert atmosphere. Selective bright untouched paper zones represent the directional sun-key highlights on metal armor surfaces and rim-lit silhouettes. Strong tonal contrast throughout — deep blacks in shadow zones, bright untouched paper for highlights, NOT evenly lit.

Style references: Wes Anderson production storyboards, traditional Hollywood film storyboard aesthetic, animator's pre-visualization sketches, Mad Max Fury Road action sequence boards.

Drawing rules:
- Pencil graphite on warm off-white aged paper background with slight edge wear and faint smudges suggesting heavy production use
- Black ink for panel borders and hand-lettered captions
- Red colored pencil ONLY for arrows, emphasis marks, camera position triangles, and 180-degree axis line
- Cross-hatching for shading; bright highlight zones use untouched paper
- Hand-lettered captions in serif-style block letters, slightly uneven

DIRECTIONAL AXIS RULE: the protagonist ship is moving deep into frame (forward vector toward upper frame edge). The buried Liu Hui erupts from the desert sand ahead of and to the side of the ship's forward path, between the ship's current position and its destination. The three sand skimmers are dragged behind the ship trailing in the rear. Cameras stay on one consistent side of this action axis throughout the sequence to maintain spatial continuity.

Title at top center of entire image, hand-lettered in confident block serif: "WANWU SHENG EP1 — SEG 9A — CHIEFTAIN ENTRANCE & POWER RUN"

Below title, hand-lettered subtitle in smaller letters: "12 SECONDS / 3 PANELS / SHOOTING PLAN"

PANEL 1 (top-left cell) — POV FROM SAND HUNTERS LOOKING AT DISTANT SAND MOUND:
Scene composition: this is a point-of-view shot from inside one of the sand skimmers looking out across the desert toward the horizon. Lower foreground edge of frame: the sketched silhouette of a sand hunter's shoulder and the rear-end of the taut rope still connected off-frame to the protagonist ship's leg — silhouette of skimmer rim faintly visible bottom corners. Mid-ground and background: vast empty desert dunes stretching to the horizon line at roughly upper third of panel. Center of frame, in middle distance: a localized sand mound is rising up from the otherwise flat desert surface — sand mound is drawn with crisp ink lines for the rising silhouette, surrounded by spreading radial crack lines on the desert surface fanning outward from the mound's base, and small puffs of sand dust drawn as cross-hatched clouds at the mound's perimeter. Above the mound, faint sketched vibration lines indicate the ground is trembling. Sky: late afternoon golden hour, dense cross-hatching for the warm-toned upper atmosphere, with selective bright zones near the horizon for sun glow.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow inside the panel pointing from the hunter silhouette outward toward the mound, labeled "POV / hunter's eyeline."

Caption strip below panel: "PANEL 1 — POV SHOT FROM SKIMMER / 50mm telephoto / hand-held with breathing micro-shake / SFX: distant ground rumble, sand cracking, low sub-bass swell"

PANEL 2 (top-right cell) — LOW ANGLE HERO LANDING — CHIEFTAIN ERUPTS FROM SAND:
Scene composition: extreme low angle ground-level camera looking up at a fusion-form mechanical chieftain figure who has just erupted from the desert floor. The figure occupies the central two-thirds of the frame, towering up out of frame top — dense pencil-sketched massive bronze mechanical armor covering torso, arms, legs; two tall spiraling tower-like horns rising from the shoulders piercing into the upper sky; a small human face visible at the top of the chest armor (peering out from the armor collar). Glowing cyan core circle is visible at the chest center — drawn as untouched paper with radiating bright lines. The figure's landing pose: hero-landing classic — both knees slightly bent, both armored fists planted into the sand ground, head lowered, body coiled like a compressed spring just before standing. At the base around the figure: massive sand particles and debris explode outward and upward in radial bursts — drawn as scattered ink dots and short streaks fanning radially around the impact point. The desert sand floor is cratered with deep cracks radiating outward from beneath the figure. Background sky: dense cross-hatching for shadow underside of the sky as seen from below, with bright untouched paper zones at the upper edges representing the sun behind the figure creating a strong backlight rim around the figure's silhouette.

Camera annotation: red pencil arrow rising from ground level pointing upward toward the chieftain figure, labeled "extreme low angle / hero landing." Additional red pencil emphasis marks: short impact-burst lines around the kneeling pose, and small "POW" emphasis marks (no English text) drawn as radial impact rays at the contact points between fists and ground.

Caption strip below panel: "PANEL 2 — LOW ANGLE HERO LANDING / 24mm wide / hand-held with sharp impact-frame stabilize / SFX: ground-shattering THOOM, sand-explosion roar, cyan-core power-up surge"

PANEL 3 (bottom-left cell) — ORBIT TRACKING POWER RUN — CHIEFTAIN CHARGES FORWARD:
Scene composition: this panel depicts a single moment in an orbit tracking shot — camera is positioned approximately 3/4 angle relative to the running chieftain figure, capturing him from front-quarter view as he charges forward in a power run. The chieftain figure occupies the center-right portion of the frame, mid-stride with one massive armored leg planted hard into the sand creating a visible deep crater impact, the other leg lifted high in mid-step. His upper body is upright and stable, NOT bobbing — the heavy armor stays balanced while his lower body powers through the ground. His face is set in stern focused expression (single eye-patched, the visible eye narrowed in determination), NOT shouting. The two shoulder spiral horns trail slightly behind from running momentum. The cyan chest core glows. Behind the chieftain figure, partial background visible: the sand desert with skimmer silhouettes and the protagonist ship trailing in the far distance ahead of him — the chieftain is catching up to them rapidly. At the chieftain's planted foot: massive sand plume erupting upward in cross-hatched dust cloud, with deep ground-crater impact lines radiating outward. Sweeping orbit-motion blur lines drawn around the figure to indicate the camera arc.

Camera annotation: a large red pencil curved arrow drawn arcing around the chieftain figure indicating the orbit camera path, labeled "orbit / half-rotation / front to back." Red pencil straight arrow pointing forward from the chieftain in the direction of his charge toward the off-frame ship.

Caption strip below panel: "PANEL 3 — ORBIT TRACKING POWER RUN / 35mm prime / orbit at 3/4 angle, half-rotation / SFX: thunderous footfalls, sand-impact booms, armor mechanism rhythmic clanks, growing low-frequency engine-like power throb"

SHOOTING PLAN (bottom-right cell) — TOP-DOWN BIRD'S EYE OVERVIEW OF THE DESERT BATTLEFIELD:
Drawn as a clean top-down schematic diagram on the aged paper. Center of cell: a sketched simplified outline of the desert terrain as seen from above. Upper portion of cell: the protagonist ship drawn as a rectangular schematic silhouette with nine small leg-marks underneath, moving toward the upper edge of the diagram (with a red pencil directional arrow showing the forward vector). Behind the ship trailing in lower-middle area of cell: three small triangular schematic outlines representing the three sand skimmers, each connected by a thin line (rope) to one of the ship's leg-marks. Lower-middle area of cell, slightly off to one side from directly behind the ship: a small starburst symbol marking the sand mound where Liu Hui erupts from underground (with a label "POINT OF ERUPTION"). Around this starburst, three small red pencil triangles marking the camera positions for each of the three panels (PANEL 1 camera positioned inside one skimmer pointing at the starburst; PANEL 2 camera positioned at ground level just outside the starburst pointing up; PANEL 3 camera positioned several meters away from the starburst with a curved orbit-arc red line indicating the half-rotation arc). A dashed red pencil straight line running across the diagram from the ship's forward vector through the eruption starburst — this is the 180-degree axis line. Labels in hand-lettered serif block letters: "180° AXIS LINE", "P1 / P2 / P3 CAMERA POSITIONS", "POINT OF ERUPTION", "PROTAGONIST SHIP HEADING", "SKIMMERS DRAGGED".

Caption strip below the shooting plan cell: "SHOOTING PLAN — 180° AXIS PRESERVED / cameras stay on one side of action / spatial continuity maintained across all 3 panels"

PRODUCTION NOTES at bottom edge of overall image (hand-lettered serif block letters along the bottom margin of the aged paper, outside the panel grid): "WANWU SHENG / EPISODE 1 / SEGMENT 9A — CHIEFTAIN ENTRANCE / continuity: prior segment closes with sand hunter head-turn at off-screen 'WO LAI!' shout / this segment opens with their POV revealing the source / 12 second total runtime / 3 shots cut internally as one continuous Seedance generation / next segment 9B begins with chieftain arriving at female sand hunter's skimmer to take the rope"

Hard constraints:
- NO digital painting style, NO 3D render, NO color overlays except red colored pencil for arrows and annotations
- NO photographic elements, NO AI-style smooth gradients
- Strong golden-hour desert high-contrast lighting in all panels — dense cross-hatch shadows + selective bright untouched paper highlights, NOT evenly lit
- Each storyboard panel's inner frame strictly 16:9 aspect ratio
- Total image strictly 21:9 aspect ratio
- All hand-lettered text in confident serif block letters with slight unevenness — readable but not machine-printed
- Aged off-white paper texture throughout with subtle edge wear
- Maintain consistent fusion-form chieftain character appearance across PANEL 2 and PANEL 3 — both show same character with bronze mechanical armor, shoulder spiral horns, cyan chest core, eye patch on right eye
