### 第76条｜The subject is the character Danmei  i

A single full-body character concept art illustration in 3:4 vertical portrait aspect ratio, displayed on a clean off-white studio background #F0EDE7, high-fashion character design photography aesthetic, hyperrealistic costume photography, real material textures captured through a camera lens, not 3D rendering, not CG, soft diffused studio lighting from upper left with subtle warm rim light from upper right, subtle drop shadow on background, subtle film grain.

The subject is the character Danmei @图1 in her awakened battle form, full-body standing pose with elegant catwalk-style contrapposto. She stands centered in the frame, weight on her right leg, left leg extended outward and slightly to the side, hips strongly rotated to the screen left forming a dramatic S-curve, shoulders counter-rotated to the screen right, the pose more exaggerated than a typical character portrait — closer to a fashion magazine cover stance with attitude. Her head tilts slightly to the screen right with chin pressed down, gaze directed sideways toward the front of the frame with cold imperious eyes and the faintest hint of a knowing smirk — a young Taoist trickster who clearly knows trouble is about to happen. Camera angle slightly low looking up at her for power.

Her short black hair with full straight bangs is preserved exactly as in her base reference. The dark red braided cord pendants hanging at both sides near her ears are preserved exactly. A small bronze segmented hairpin inserted diagonally into her hair on the right side, resembling a miniature crab leg segment in antique bronze #7A6845 with a tiny cyan #4FC3D9 glowing joint.

Her costume preserves her base color palette of white + black + red + bronze, but the silhouettes have been radically restructured into a more battle-ready, street-fashion-inflected outfit.

Top half: a cropped version of her original white stand-collar diagonal-button cheongsam-style blouse, the blouse now cut short to end just below her ribs, exposing her entire midriff and waist as bare skin with healthy young pale complexion. The hem of the cropped blouse has a torn-edge fraying effect with a few thin strips of white fabric trailing downward. The original stand-collar and diagonal-button design at the top are preserved exactly. The blouse sleeves are preserved as long sleeves to her wrists.

Over the cropped blouse, a black short cape/half-coat is draped over her left shoulder only, the right shoulder bare of cape, the cape is a single asymmetric panel of black matte fabric falling down her left side to her hip, lined on the inside with deep blood-red silk that just barely shows at the edges, the cape has a tall popped collar around the back of her neck. The cape gives her silhouette a strong asymmetric profile and adds the streetwear/jacket feel.

Lower half: a short black wraparound skirt with deep right-side slit, much shorter than her base reference's long skirt, the new skirt ends mid-thigh on her left side and is slit completely open on her right side exposing her entire right leg, the skirt is wrapped with multiple layered belts at her waist — three to four overlapping belts of different materials including a red braided cord sash (matching her base reference), a thin black leather belt, and a dark bronze chain belt with small talisman charms hanging from it. The bronze talisman pouch from her base reference is preserved and hangs from one of the belts, alongside two additional smaller pouches and several small bronze bells and a rolled paper talisman tucked into the belts — the waist area has high accessory density.

Legs: her right leg is fully exposed from hip to ankle through the deep skirt slit, the thigh wears a thick black leather strap wrapped twice around the upper thigh with small bronze talisman charms threaded onto it, the right knee has a small antique bronze segmented knee guard wrapping just the kneecap with cyan #4FC3D9 glow at the seams, from the knee down her right calf is bare skin to the ankle, her right foot in a black ankle-high boot with a small heel. Her left leg under the longer side of the skirt wears a black thigh-high stocking with a black leather garter strap at the top, the stocking ending in a similar black ankle boot.

Arms: black opera-length leather gloves extending from her fingertips up past her elbows nearly to her shoulders, the gloves fitting tightly to her arms. Over the gloves at each wrist sits a slim antique bronze segmented wrist cuff, jewelry-scale, three or four bronze segment plates with cyan glow at the joints.

Neck: a small bronze medallion hangs from a thin braided cord around her neck, sitting in the hollow between her collarbones, the medallion no larger than a coin, cast in bronze with concentric seal-script relief patterns and a tiny cyan #4FC3D9 glowing eye-stone at the center.

Shoulders: small antique bronze segmented shoulder ornaments hang down from the very top of each shoulder, palm-sized at most, three or four bronze segment links chained together with cyan glow at the joints, hanging like decorative tassels rather than armor pauldrons.

Her right hand reaches back behind her right shoulder and grips the reddish-copper spiral-threaded grip of a massive two-handed greatsword — the weapon called "渡" matches the design reference image exactly with all its structural and material details (bronze hexagonal plate blade body, top jet-venting array, segmented bronze tubing system, mid-blade bronze module, reddish-copper spiral grip, copper pommel sphere). The sword rests on her right shoulder behind her head, the blade extending diagonally upward to the left across the upper part of the frame, the full length of the sword visible cutting a strong diagonal line through the upper composition. The sword does not obscure her face or her body in front.

Her left hand hangs casually at her left side, the gloved fingers slightly curled, the left thumb hooked into one of the belts at her waist near the bronze talisman pouches.

Composition: Danmei occupies the central two thirds of the vertical frame from head to foot, the diagonal sword crossing the upper portion of the frame as a strong visual line, the off-white background empty around her with soft shadows beneath her feet.

Lighting: soft diffused key light from upper left, subtle warm rim light from upper right catching edges of bronze accessories, copper sword grip, and the bare midriff/right leg, the cyan glow joints on the bronze pieces soft and subtle, her bare skin on midriff and right leg showing healthy soft complexion, the contrast of pale bare skin against black leather gloves/stocking/cape and white cropped blouse is a key visual moment.

Material rendering: every surface real physical material — handcrafted bronze with authentic patina on the small armor pieces, real linen for the white cropped blouse with subtle wrinkles, real matte black wool/cotton for the wrap skirt and cape with real fabric drape, real deep red silk lining inside the cape, real black leather for the gloves and belts and boots with leather grain texture, real black silk/nylon for the thigh-high stocking, real reddish-copper for the sword grip, real human skin texture for the exposed midriff and right leg, real paper for the talismans, real braided cord for hair pendants and waist sash — no smooth CG surfaces.

Design philosophy: Zenless Zone Zero street-fashion meets Taoist trickster — accessory density at the waist (multiple belts, pouches, charms, bells), asymmetric silhouette via single-shoulder cape, exaggerated skin exposure (midriff fully bare, right leg fully bare), torn-edge fraying details on the cropped blouse hem, opera-length black leather gloves, thigh-high stocking on one leg, the body's curves emphasized through the cropped + wrap combination, bronze armor pieces reduced to small jewelry-scale accents only at contour nodes (shoulders, wrists, knee, neck), the massive sword as the dominant visual weight on her shoulder. The character's face, hair, and base color palette must match her base reference image. The sword must match its design reference image one-to-one.

### 第78条｜Photographic specifications: shot on A

A 3x3 grid of 9 cinematic black-and-white film stills, total canvas aspect ratio 16:9. Each of the 9 panels is itself a 16:9 cinematic frame, separated by clean thin black borders (2-3px) with small white margins between panels. Small panel numbers "01" through "09" rendered in clean sans-serif font in the top-left corner of each panel. Read order: left-to-right, top-to-bottom.

CRITICAL RENDERING DIRECTIVE: These are NOT line art, NOT manga panels, NOT sketches, NOT concept illustrations. These are PHOTOREALISTIC CINEMATIC STILL PHOTOGRAPHS rendered in pure black-and-white grayscale only - as if shot on a black-and-white digital cinema camera. Every panel must look like a frame extracted from a live-action martial arts film. Full continuous grayscale tonal range from pure black to pure white with all midtones. Real photographic depth of field, lens characteristics, motion blur where appropriate, atmospheric haze, sub-surface scattering on skin, accurate material reflectance on metal/leather/fabric/sand, fine film grain texture overlay. Color information is the ONLY thing missing - everything else must read as a real photograph from a real film production.

Photographic specifications: shot on ARRI Alexa Mini LF equivalent, anamorphic widescreen lenses, T2.8 aperture giving cinematic shallow depth of field where appropriate, Kodak Double-X 5222 black-and-white film stock aesthetic with subtle grain, natural directional daylight as primary lighting source (consistent direction across all 9 panels - sun from upper-left), no stylized hatching or graphic effects whatsoever.

MAIN CHARACTER (must be identical across all 9 panels - lock the design):
Lean young Asian female warrior. Short black bob haircut with straight blunt bangs covering forehead, fine hair strands visible. Hair ornament: a small red braided tassel hanging from the left side of her hair near a hairpin. Pale skin with realistic photographic texture. Sharp dark eyes.

Costume:
- White ragged cropped shirt with frayed torn hem, midriff exposed, fabric showing real cotton weave texture
- Long worn black leather coat (NOT a cloak) reaching ankle-length, with dark red interior lining visible at the lapels and when the coat flares in motion. Coat has heavy weathered texture, faded red Chinese calligraphy character printed on the back, metal chain decorations across the shoulder and back, multiple leather pouches and small buckles attached
- Tattered dark fabric short skirt with frayed lower edge, layered with deep red fabric beneath, metal chain belt at waist with hanging wooden charm tags inscribed with faded Chinese characters
- Black ripped fishnet stockings (the legs are bare otherwise - no greaves, no armor plating on the legs)
- Black thigh straps wrapping the upper thigh
- Long black leather gloves extending past the elbows
- Black leather high-heel ankle boots with thick stacked heels (do not invent additional details about boot construction - render them as realistic dark leather heeled boots)

Weapon: an oversized heavy sword roughly equal to her own height. The sword has an industrial-mechanical design - exposed metal pipes and tubing running along the spine, brass rivets, weathered dark metal surface with visible wear marks, the guard area is bulky with mechanical components, faded red painted text markings on the blade flat. The blade itself is broad and heavy-looking. This is a single signature weapon - keep its design absolutely consistent across all panels.

Overall aesthetic of the character: Eastern mechanical-punk, lived-in and weathered, real costume on a real actress - photoreal not stylized.

OPPONENTS (consistent design across all panels where they appear): 7 desert raiders, all male, lean and weathered. Faces fully wrapped in tattered dust-cloth with only narrow eye slits visible. Layered armor of stitched leather plates over coarse cloth wrappings, hanging bone amulets and small metal trinkets, weathered leather belts. Each wields a curved single-edged saber with crude wrapped grip and simple metal guard. Their builds are sinewy. Render them as real actors in real costumes, not as graphic designs.

ENVIRONMENT: vast windswept desert. Layered dune ridges receding to a distant horizon line. Scattered weathered rocks half-buried in sand. Distant rock formations as silhouettes on the horizon. Vast sky with thin high-altitude cirrus clouds. Strong directional sunlight from upper-left creating long hard-edged shadows consistent across all 9 panels. Wind blowing sand particles consistently from right to left across all panels. Real photographic atmospheric haze increasing with distance.

GRAYSCALE RENDERING REQUIREMENTS:
- Skin tones: midtone grays with subtle highlight gradients, realistic sub-surface light scattering, no graphic outlines
- Black leather coat / boots / gloves: deep blacks with realistic highlight rolloff where light catches the surface, visible leather grain texture in close-ups
- White cropped shirt: bright midtone to highlight range, realistic cotton fabric texture
- Heavy sword: dark metallic grays with sharp specular highlights along the blade edges, brass fittings rendered slightly lighter
- Red elements (coat lining, calligraphy on coat back, fabric beneath skirt): rendered as medium-dark grays but with enough tonal separation from black to remain readable
- Sand: bright midtone with fine granular texture in foreground, smooth gradient surfaces in midground, atmospheric haze in background
- Sky: lighter gray gradient with cloud detail rendered as soft tonal variations
- Shadows: deep but with detail retention - not crushed pure black
- Highlights: bright but with detail retention - not blown out pure white
- Motion blur on fast-moving elements (sword swings, sand, fabric in motion): realistic photographic motion blur, not stylized speed lines
- Fine film grain overlay across the entire image for cinematic texture

PANEL 01 [Extreme wide shot / High aerial angle]:
The warrior stands on the crest of a high dune, viewed from behind and slightly above, her full body silhouetted against the bright sky. Heavy sword stabbed vertically into the sand to her right, her left hand resting loosely on the pommel. The dark red interior lining of her coat is visible as the wind catches and lifts the left side of the coat outward. Below her, the dune slopes down dramatically into a wide valley. In the far distance at the valley floor, 7 tiny figures advance in spread formation - small but clearly readable as armed human silhouettes. The sky occupies the upper two-thirds of the frame with thin streaked cirrus clouds. Her long hard shadow stretches across the dune ridge. Airborne sand particles streak across the frame. Deep focus - everything sharp from foreground to horizon.

PANEL 02 [Medium shot / Eye level, very slight low angle]:
Tight medium shot of the lead opponent from chest up. His cloth-wrapped face fills the frame with only his narrow sharp eyes visible through the wrap. His curved saber rests across his right shoulder. His left hand is raised mid-gesture, fingers spread, signaling his men to flank. Strong backlight from the upper-left rim-lights the edges of his head wrap and shoulders with a bright highlight, his front in shadow but with detail retention. Behind him in soft-focused background, 4 other figures visible in spread formation as out-of-focus silhouettes. Dust streaks blow across the frame from right to left. Shallow depth of field isolates the leader.

PANEL 03 [Extreme low angle / Vertical upward composition]:
A very low camera angle, ground level. In the lower portion of the frame: an extreme close-up of the warrior's boots planted firmly in the sand, individual sand grains compressed and displaced visibly around the boot edges. The boots are dark and heeled (render based on the established character design - do not invent fabricated details). Rising vertically from beside the boots through the entire frame: the heavy sword, blade flat facing camera, every rivet and mechanical pipe along its length visible in sharp focus, surface wear and engraved markings catching a sharp specular highlight from the upper-left sun. At the top edge of the frame: the partial underside of the warrior's chin and lower face, mostly out of frame. Background: out-of-focus dune slope with atmospheric haze.

PANEL 04 [Wide shot / Behind-character three-quarter angle / Dynamic motion]:
The warrior viewed from a behind-right three-quarter angle, captured mid-charge, her body rotated through a powerful horizontal sword swing. The heavy sword is extended out to her left in a wide arc, the blade frozen at the moment of full extension with realistic photographic motion blur trailing the swing. The sword's path has thrown up a curving wall of sand particles - rendered as a dense cloud of individual airborne grains catching the light. Three opponents in the path of the swing are knocked backward by the displaced air and sand wall - their bodies tumbling away from the warrior, sabers knocked from their hands tumbling through the air alongside loose cloth fragments from their tattered wrappings. Her coat fully extended behind her with the red lining flared open, real fabric motion. Other opponents visible in the background reacting and backing away. Low horizon line, sky compressed to upper sliver. The visual focus is on the sand wall and the force of the swing, not on body damage - no wounds, no blood, no torn flesh, just opponents being knocked back by force.

PANEL 05 [Low angle / Strict profile tracking shot / Sharp diagonal composition]:
The warrior in strict profile from her left side, her body angled into a powerful diagonal sword swing. The heavy sword is swung from upper-right to lower-left of the frame, the blade caught at the exact moment of meeting a foreground opponent's raised saber that he holds up defensively across his chest. Sparks burst from the metal-on-metal contact point as individual radiating photographic particles. The opponent's saber is bending under the force of the blow, his body recoiling backward from the impact, his feet sliding through the sand. In the background in sharp focus: a second opponent rushing in with raised saber, his cloth face wraps fluttering with his motion. The warrior's face partially visible: cold expression, controlled, mouth slightly set, hair caught by motion. Low camera angle. The focus is metal-on-metal impact and force - no body damage, no wounds.

PANEL 06 [Extreme close-up / Eyes only / Horizontal letterbox composition within the 16:9 panel]:
An extreme close-up of just the warrior's eyes occupying the full width of the panel. Black bob hair strands fall across the upper forehead, individual strands rendered with photographic detail. Both eyes rendered in extreme detail: realistic iris texture and pattern, pupils, lower lash line, micro-tension at the outer eye corner, subtle skin texture under the eyes. The skin tone is rendered as soft midtone grays. In the right pupil, a tiny but clearly legible reflection: the silhouette of an opponent in mid-motion with raised saber, rendered with enough sharpness to be readable. Expression: absolute cold focus, no anger, no fear, no exertion - just precision. The background is soft-focused into abstract darkness, isolating the eyes completely.

PANEL 07 [Medium shot / Three-quarter front angle / Symmetrical pressure composition]:
The warrior in a deep kneeling stance on her right knee, left leg braced forward, the heavy sword held horizontally across her chest as a guard in both hands. Two curved sabers come down onto the flat of her blade from upper-left and upper-right simultaneously - she is blocking both strikes. Both contact points burst with photographic spark particles radiating outward. The two attacking opponents visible at the left and right edges of the frame, their bodies leaning into the strike with visible muscular effort, faces showing strain through their cloth wraps. Two more opponents compressing in from the extreme left and right edges of the frame with sabers raised, preparing to strike. The warrior's expression: focused, jaw set, controlled. Her coat pooled around her on the sand, real fabric weight and fold detail. The sand around her shows pressure displacement under her stance. Focus on the moment of defense, the sparks of metal blocking metal.

PANEL 08 [Extreme low angle / Ground-level upward / Explosive vertical composition]:
Camera positioned at sand level looking sharply upward. The warrior bursting upward from her kneeling position into a standing position, the heavy sword swung in a powerful upward semicircular arc, the blade tracing a path across the upper half of the frame with realistic motion blur. Two opponents who had been pressing down on her are now thrown upward and backward by the force of her rising counter-strike, their bodies tumbling backward through the air with arms and legs splayed in tumbling motion, their sabers knocked loose from their grips spinning away. Her coat fully extended behind her at the apex of the motion, the red lining flared wide. Her face visible from below: teeth slightly bared in effort, hair lifted by the upward movement, expression showing controlled intensity. Background: dramatic sky with cloud detail filling the upper frame. Sand particles thrown up around her feet in a cloud. The focus is the explosive force of her rising movement - opponents are tumbling, not damaged. No body injuries visible.

PANEL 09 [Extreme wide shot / High aerial angle / Mirror of Panel 01]:
EXACT same camera position, angle, and lens as Panel 01. The warrior now stands at the exact center of the frame on the dune crest, the heavy sword stabbed vertically into the sand to her right, her left hand on the pommel - identical posture to Panel 01 but mirrored in position within the frame. Her coat now hangs still, minimal wind motion. Surrounding her in a loose radius on the sand: 7 fallen opponents lying motionless on the dune in varied resting poses - they have been defeated and are lying still on the sand. Each fallen figure is rendered in clear photographic detail but with no visible injuries, no blood, no damage - they simply lie still on the sand as if asleep or unconscious, their loose clothing settled around them, their sabers fallen beside them. Long sunset shadows stretched dramatically across the dunes - the shadow direction has shifted to suggest end-of-day light (lower sun angle, longer shadows than Panel 01). The sky now compressed in the upper portion with denser cloud cover suggesting a lower sun. Atmospheric haze rendered with photographic depth. The visual rhyme with Panel 01 must be exact: same warrior posture, same sword position, same dune crest, same camera. The DIFFERENCE: she is now centered, the figures who were approaching her in Panel 01 are now still and fallen around her feet, and the light has changed.

CRITICAL FINAL DIRECTIVE: Every single panel must look like a real photograph from a real live-action martial arts film production. Pure black-and-white photographic grayscale only. Fine film grain texture across all panels. Consistent lighting direction across all panels. Consistent character design across all panels. The action is choreographed martial arts - emphasis on force, motion, and impact, NOT on injury or damage. No blood. No wounds. No torn flesh. No graphic injury detail. The opponents are knocked back, blocked, thrown - they are not visibly hurt. This is stylized cinematic action like classic wuxia films, not graphic violence.

Output: 16:9 final canvas, 3x3 grid of 9 equal panels, thin black grid borders with small white margins, panel numbers "01"-"09" visible in top-left corners.

### 第80条｜Title text at top center reading "渡 —

A character design reference sheet displayed on a clean off-white studio background with grid-divided panels, museum-quality product photography aesthetic, photographic still life of a real physical handcrafted weapon prop, real material textures captured through a camera lens, not 3D rendering, not CG, not digital painting, hyperrealistic industrial weapon photography, tactile bronze and copper metalwork, soft diffused studio lighting from upper left, subtle drop shadow on background.

Title text at top center reading "渡 — DU — COMPANION-WEAPON DESIGN SHEET" in clean modern sans-serif typography.

Four panels arranged in a 2x2 grid, each panel labeled at the bottom center with small capital letters.

[TOP LEFT PANEL — labeled "FULL FRONT"]
A massive two-handed industrial greatsword displayed vertically, blade pointing up, hilt pointing down, the weapon stands approximately the height of a human adult, the overall silhouette and structure is a faithful one-to-one replica of the Wyvern Ignition Greatsword from Monster Hunter World — the same imposing rectangular slab blade proportions, the same flared trapezoidal jet-engine-style venting array at the blade tip, the same broad rectangular blade body with industrial cutting profile, the same mid-blade information panel module, the same curving bronze tubing system running along both sides of the blade, the same small oval venting holes on the blade body, the same dramatically simple long grip ending in a counterweight sphere. The entire structural design is industrial weapon hard-surface, every edge is angular machined cutting profile, every joint shows visible rivets and structural seams.

The material language is the key transformation — instead of the original gunmetal steel and crimson lacquer, every armored surface of this blade is constructed from densely packed antique bronze hexagonal plates, the same hexagonal symbol-relief plate material as found on ancient Chinese bronze ritual artifacts, #7A6845 muted bronze-green base color with #846035 darker bronze recesses, each hexagonal plate cast with raised ancient seal-script and Taoist talisman symbols in the relief pattern, between every plate thin glowing cyan #4FC3D9 seam lines, oxidation patches and verdigris in the deeper crevices, real metal weathering not digital wear, surface scratches showing dull underlying bronze beneath the patina layer. The original crimson lacquer accent areas of the Wyvern Ignition design are translated into darker bronze recessed zones with concentrated verdigris green oxidation, providing the same color contrast role as the original crimson but in bronze-patina language.

The top venting structure is the flared trapezoidal array of parallel horizontal grille slots fanning outward from the blade body, six to eight grille slots stacked vertically in the iconic Wyvern Ignition jet-exhaust configuration, each slot framed in cast bronze with chamfered edges and rivet detailing, the slot interiors deep and dark with faint cyan #4FC3D9 glow visible deep inside indicating dormant energy chambers, no inscriptions or text on the vent surfaces, purely structural industrial design with bronze material language.

The mid-blade information panel module retains the exact shape and position of the original Wyvern Ignition design — a flat rectangular plate embedded in the blade center, but the original blue-grey panel with pink-purple circuit-pattern etching is here replaced with a darker bronze plate #5A4A35 framed in segmented bronze bezels with rivets, the panel surface is smooth bronze with subtle patina, no inscriptions, no eyes, no symbols added — just the clean dark bronze plate occupying the exact position of the original information module.

The curving tubing system that runs along both sides of the blade from the venting array down through the blade body is translated from the original brass hydraulic pipes into segmented antique bronze tubing matching the eye-stalk segment construction of crab-form bronze artifacts, each tube section connected by bronze ring couplings with visible rivets, between couplings small cyan #4FC3D9 glowing joints leak faint light, the tubing follows the exact curving path of the original Wyvern Ignition pipework.

Small oval venting holes on the blade body sides retain their original positions and shapes but are now cast in bronze with patina darkening at the edges, faint cyan glow visible deep inside each oval vent.

The blade base transitions through a tapered bronze segment into the grip guard — a small curving crescent guard piece in dark bronze with verdigris.

The grip retains the exact form of the original Wyvern Ignition handle — a long cylindrical grip with raised spiral ridges along its surface, but the material is reddish-copper #A85530 polished copper with spiral threading clearly visible, the copper shows authentic age patina with warmer red tones, this copper grip provides the warm color accent against the cooler bronze-green of the rest of the weapon, the spiral threading matches the original design exactly.

The pommel terminates in a small round copper sphere with a tiny pointed tip, exactly as the original Wyvern Ignition pommel structure, in matching reddish-copper material.

The entire weapon shows authentic real-metal handcrafted aging — bronze patina with natural verdigris, copper warm patina, no digital noise patterns, no procedural texturing, only the irregular natural beauty of physical aged metal.

[TOP RIGHT PANEL — labeled "FULL SIDE"]
The same greatsword viewed in pure profile from the right side, blade still vertical. The side view reveals the weapon's thickness profile — the blade is a thick industrial slab matching the original Wyvern Ignition side profile, showing the depth of the venting cavity at the top, the segmented bronze tubing now visible running prominently along the spine edge of the blade as three-dimensional pipes with bronze ring couplings, cyan #4FC3D9 glow leaking from segment joints. The mid-blade bronze information panel from this side appears as a slight protrusion from the blade body. The top venting structure from this angle reveals the depth of the parallel grille slots extending backward into the blade body like real jet exhaust manifolds, internal cyan glow visible. The reddish-copper spiral grip with the small copper pommel sphere visible at the base. Same hyperrealistic material rendering — antique bronze hexagonal plate work, copper grip, real metal weathering.

[BOTTOM LEFT PANEL — labeled "VENT DETAIL"]
A close-up macro shot of the top venting structure of the blade, framed tightly so the venting fills most of the panel with a small portion of the upper bronze hexagonal plates visible below for context. The shot reveals fine industrial detail of the bronze jet-exhaust array: each parallel grille slot is a deep clean rectangular cavity framed in cast bronze, the slot edges have chamfered industrial machining with small visible rivets along the structural frame, the bronze surface around the vents shows authentic patina texture with verdigris pooling in the recessed seams, scratches and oxidation showing the dull underlying bronze beneath, no inscriptions or characters anywhere on the venting structure, purely industrial machined bronze surfaces. Deep inside the grille slots cyan glow #4FC3D9 is visible as a soft volumetric haze with faint internal structure suggesting energy chambers, the cyan glow varies in intensity between slots indicating dormant pulsing energy, no particles, no paper fragments. The bronze hexagonal plates below the vent frame are visible showing their characteristic ancient symbol relief casting with cyan glow seam lines between plates. Adjacent to the vent frame: the upper terminus of the segmented bronze tubing system, showing the bronze pipe joining into a flared coupling at the vent base. The entire macro shot emphasizes that this is a real handcrafted bronze weapon prop photographed with a macro lens.

[BOTTOM RIGHT PANEL — labeled "GRIP DETAIL"]
A close-up macro shot of the grip and pommel region of the weapon, framed to show the lower portion of the bronze blade transitioning down through the bronze guard piece into the reddish-copper spiral grip and ending at the copper pommel sphere. The shot reveals the contrast of materials: the cooler bronze-green hexagonal plates of the blade base meeting the warmer reddish-copper #A85530 of the grip, the spiral threading on the copper grip clearly visible with raised ridges catching the studio key light, the copper surface shows authentic warm patina with subtle darkening in the spiral grooves, real metal oxidation. The small crescent bronze guard piece between blade and grip shows verdigris green patina in its curves. At the very bottom of the grip the small round copper pommel sphere with its tiny pointed tip is fully visible, matching warm copper material with age patina. No inscriptions, no symbols, no characters anywhere — purely material and structural detail. The entire detail shot emphasizes the warm-cool contrast of the two metals and the handcrafted physical material reality.

Overall presentation: clean off-white studio background #F0EDE7, soft diffused lighting from upper left creating gentle shadows, subtle film grain across the entire image, neutral color grading emphasizing the bronze and copper tones, photographic depth of field with each panel sharp throughout, no flashy CGI effects, this is a design reference sheet treating an imaginary bronze-and-copper weapon as if it were a real physical museum prop photographed by a product photographer, the consistency of antique bronze hexagonal plate material and warm copper grip material across all four panels is critical.

### 第82条｜The subject is the character Danmei  i

A single full-body character concept art illustration in 3:4 vertical portrait aspect ratio, displayed on a clean off-white studio background #F0EDE7, high-fashion character design photography aesthetic, hyperrealistic costume photography, real material textures captured through a real camera lens, photographic film still aesthetic, NOT 3D rendering, NOT CG, NOT digital painting, NOT anime illustration, real physical handcrafted costume captured on a physical human model in a photography studio, soft diffused studio lighting from upper left with subtle warm rim light from upper right, subtle drop shadow on background, subtle film grain.

The subject is the character Danmei @图1 in her awakened battle form. Her face, facial features, eye shape, eye color, hair color, hair style, and entire facial identity must match her base reference image with absolute precision — character identity consistency is critical, this must be unmistakably the same person as in the reference image, only her costume is changed.

Full-body standing pose with elegant catwalk-style contrapposto. She stands centered in the frame, weight on her right leg, left leg extended outward and slightly to the side, hips strongly rotated to the screen left forming a dramatic S-curve, shoulders counter-rotated to the screen right, the pose more exaggerated than a typical character portrait — closer to a fashion magazine cover stance with attitude. Her head tilts slightly to the screen right with chin pressed down, gaze directed sideways toward the front of the frame with cold imperious eyes and the faintest hint of a knowing smirk. Camera angle slightly low looking up at her.

CRITICAL PROPORTION ANCHOR: Danmei is 165cm tall. The weapon she holds is also approximately 165cm tall, almost exactly equal to her own height. In the composition, the weapon and Danmei must appear at nearly equal height, the weapon's full length from tip to pommel must measure the same as Danmei's full height from head to foot. The weapon stands vertically next to her body, parallel to her standing posture, no foreshortening, no perspective compression of the weapon length. Her right hand grips the weapon near where the blade meets the grip, the weapon's lower tip rests on the ground at her right side, the entire weapon body stands straight up alongside her body, the top of the weapon reaches up to the same height as the top of her head or slightly above. This is a one-to-one height comparison composition.

Her short black hair with full straight bangs is preserved exactly as in her base reference. The dark red braided cord pendants hanging at both sides near her ears are preserved exactly. A small bronze segmented hairpin inserted diagonally into her hair on the right side, antique bronze #7A6845 with cyan #4FC3D9 glow joint.

Her costume:

Top: a heavily modified sleeveless crop version of her original white stand-collar diagonal-button cheongsam-style blouse. The blouse is cut short to end just below her ribs exposing her bare midriff. The blouse is sleeveless on BOTH sides — both left and right shoulders are fully bare to bare skin, both arms emerge from the sleeveless armholes as bare uncovered skin. The original stand-collar and diagonal-button design at the top preserved. The blouse fabric is opaque thick white linen, properly tailored with full structural support underneath so the chest area shows only the smooth outer fabric surface with no visible underlying details — fully covered modest tailored fit at the bust. The crop hem at the bottom of the blouse has a torn-edge fraying effect with several thin strips of white linen fabric trailing downward in irregular lengths, the edge is ragged and uneven rather than clean tailored, the torn edges show frayed loose threads and fabric strips hanging down a few centimeters past the main hem line — the costume looks battle-worn and tattered at this hem.

Over the sleeveless cropped blouse, a black short cape draped over her LEFT shoulder only, the right shoulder has no cape over it. The cape is a single asymmetric panel of black matte fabric falling down her left side to her hip, lined on the inside with deep blood-red silk just barely visible at the inner edges, tall popped collar around the back of her neck. The cape drapes over her bare left shoulder skin but does not function as a sleeve — her left arm beneath the cape remains visible as bare skin from shoulder downward.

Bare midriff: the strip of bare midriff between the cropped blouse hem and the high-waisted skirt top shows a young woman's natural slim soft abdomen with healthy young pale complexion — flat and softly toned but absolutely no defined abdominal muscle ridges or six-pack lines visible, no athletic musculature, the abdomen reads as natural feminine soft skin only.

Bottom: a short black wraparound skirt with deep right-side slit, ending mid-thigh on her left side and fully slit open on her right side. The skirt is wrapped with multiple layered belts at her waist — three to four overlapping belts including a red braided cord sash, a thin black leather belt, and a dark bronze chain belt with small talisman charms. The original bronze talisman pouch is preserved hanging from one belt, alongside two additional smaller pouches and several small bronze bells and a rolled paper talisman tucked into the belts.

LEG SILHOUETTE — important: both legs are slim slender feminine youthful legs, the silhouette must read as graceful young woman not as muscular athletic build, no thick calves no muscular thighs, slim graceful slender feminine leg lines throughout, the kind of slim leg proportions seen in fashion editorial photography of young female models, soft tapering line from hip to ankle.

Right leg: fully exposed from hip to ankle through the deep skirt slit. The upper thigh wears a thick black leather strap wrapped twice around it with small bronze talisman charms threaded on. From the knee down her right calf is completely bare slim skin to the ankle, no armor, no knee guard, no greave anywhere on this leg. Right foot in a black ankle-high boot with a small heel. The exposed right leg's bare skin is slim slender feminine line.

Left leg: under the longer side of the skirt, the left leg wears a black thigh-high stocking with a black leather garter strap at the top, stocking ending in a similar black ankle boot. The stocking-clad left leg silhouette is also slim slender feminine.

Arms: both shoulders and upper arms are bare from the sleeveless blouse. Her RIGHT arm wears a black opera-length leather glove starting at her fingertips and extending up her forearm and past her elbow to mid-upper-arm, fitting tightly to her arm — the glove covers from her hand up to roughly two thirds of the way to her right shoulder, leaving the top portion of her right upper arm and her right shoulder as bare skin between the glove top and the sleeveless blouse opening. Over the right wrist sits a slim antique bronze segmented wrist cuff with cyan glow joints, layered on top of the glove at the wrist. Her LEFT arm is completely bare from shoulder to fingertips — no glove, no wrist cuff, no armor, no fabric of any kind, only bare skin from her left shoulder all the way down her left arm to her left fingertips. Asymmetric arm design — right arm has the long glove plus wrist cuff with a bare upper arm strip between glove top and shoulder, left arm completely bare throughout.

Neck: a small bronze medallion hangs from a thin braided cord around her neck, sitting in the hollow between her collarbones, coin-sized, bronze with concentric seal-script relief and a tiny cyan #4FC3D9 glowing eye-stone at center.

Shoulders: a small antique bronze segmented shoulder ornament hangs from her right shoulder strap of the blouse, palm-sized, three bronze segment links with cyan glow at joints, hanging like a decorative tassel against her bare right upper arm. Her left shoulder has no ornament, only the cape draped over bare skin.

Pose with the weapon: her right hand grips the weapon, the weapon stands vertically alongside her right side, the weapon's tip resting on the ground beside her right boot, the weapon body parallel to her standing posture, the top of the weapon reaching up to the level of the top of her head or slightly above, the weapon's full 165cm height visible without foreshortening matching Danmei's 165cm height in a one-to-one proportion comparison.

The weapon must match the design reference image of the weapon exactly — all structural details, all proportions, all colors, all materials of the weapon are determined entirely by the reference image and not by this text description.

Her left hand hangs relaxed at her left side, the bare ungloved fingers slightly curled, left thumb hooked into one of the belts at her waist near the bronze talisman pouches.

Composition: Danmei and the vertical weapon occupy the central two thirds of the vertical frame as two parallel vertical anchors of equal height, the off-white background empty around them with soft shadows beneath her feet and the weapon's tip. The frame is composed to show both at full height with the top of her head and the top of the weapon reaching the same horizontal level, both feet/tip reaching the bottom ground line at the same horizontal level.

Lighting: soft diffused key light from upper left, subtle warm rim light from upper right catching edges of bronze accessories, the weapon, and bare skin. Cyan glow joints subtle. Pale bare skin against black cape, black stocking on left leg, black skirt, and white cropped blouse — strong tonal contrast. Bare left shoulder and left arm visible with soft skin tones.

CHARACTER IDENTITY: This must be unmistakably the same Danmei as in her base reference image — same face, same eye shape, same nose, same mouth, same hair, same overall facial structure, same young Taoist girl identity, only her clothing has been changed. Her face must not be redesigned or restyled. Her body type must read as a slim slender young woman with feminine silhouette throughout.

CRITICAL RENDERING: This is photorealistic studio fashion photography of a real human model wearing a real handcrafted costume, captured through a real camera lens on real film. NOT 3D rendered, NOT CG game character, NOT anime illustration, NOT digital painting. Real human skin with natural soft pore texture and soft complexion, real linen fabric for the cropped blouse with natural wrinkles, real matte wool/cotton for the wrap skirt and cape with real fabric drape, real silk lining inside cape, real black leather for belts and boots, real black silk/nylon for the thigh-high stocking, real handcrafted bronze with authentic patina on the small accessories, real braided cord for hair pendants and waist sash, real paper for the talismans. The entire image must read as a single high-end fashion photography still from a real photoshoot, with photographic depth of field, real lens optical characteristics, real studio lighting falloff, real film grain. Avoid all CG cleanliness, avoid procedural texture patterns, avoid plastic skin shading, avoid stylized anime rendering.


### 第83条｜@素材1背对镜头站在沙丘顶端，重剑斜插入身旁沙地，左手轻搭剑柄。黑色大衣红色

@图1 为女主角形象参考。
@图2 为色卡参考，严格按色卡的HEX分配色彩。
@图3 为女主使用的武器。

15秒沙漠真人电影武术动作戏，全程实拍真实感，彩色成片。9个镜头硬切串联。武侠片式的动作美学，强调力量与气势。

对手为6-7名沙匪，破布缠面只露双眼。服饰主色#B08850土黄破布与#5C3A24焦褐皮甲，挂兽骨护符与小金属饰物，腰间皮带#7A4530铁锈色金属扣件，手持单刃弯刀。

环境为广袤起伏的沙丘地形：近景沙地#B8804A暖橙沙，远景沙地#C9A876偏黄沙，天空#8A95A0灰蓝带稀薄云带，沙丘投影#3D2E26深褐色。远处有岩石轮廓与零星沙漠遗迹。强烈定向阳光从画面左上方射入，正午偏后顶光。沙尘从右向左持续吹过，扬尘色调#E8DCC4米白奶色。

整体审美：真人电影实拍质感，ARRI Alexa拍摄质感，Kodak Vision3 35mm彩色胶片颗粒感，宽银幕变形宽镜头anamorphic widescreen，褪色油画色调（强光+低饱和度，红色与黑色清晰可辨但被胶片质感吃掉饱和度，不是灰扑扑），中等画面对比度，自然光源主导，真实景深，运动模糊符合摄影物理规律。武术动作经过严密设计，类似经典武侠片，强调力量、气势、招式之美。

【镜头1 / 极远景俯拍 / 约1.5秒】
@素材1背对镜头站在沙丘顶端，重剑斜插入身旁沙地，左手轻搭剑柄。黑色大衣红色内衬被风从左侧掀起，红色内衬#6B1F22在风中是画面唯一鲜明色彩点。她脚下沙丘大幅下倾，远处沙谷底有7个武装人员小剪影呈散开队形逼近。天空#8A95A0占画面上方三分之二。她的长影投在沙丘脊上。空气中飞沙颗粒清晰可见从右向左飘过。色调：天空灰蓝主导，沙地暖黄过渡，主角剪影黑红对比。配音：低频沙漠风声呜咽。

【镜头2 / 平视中景 / 约1.5秒】
硬切。对手首领胸部以上特写，破布缠面#B08850只露锐利双眼，弯刀架于右肩。左手抬起做散开手势。强烈左上方逆光勾出他的发布和肩部轮廓，正面在阴影中但保留细节。背景虚化中可见4名其他武装人员呈扇形散开。沙尘从右侧吹过画面，沙粒在光中可见。浅景深锁定首领。色调：焦褐#5C3A24与土黄#B08850主导，逆光形成深褐剪影#3D2E26。配音：弯刀金属出鞘的清脆刮擦声+压低的呼喝。

【镜头3 / 极仰角垂直构图 / 约1.5秒】
硬切。机位置于地面极低角度。画面下半部分：@素材1的黑色高跟皮靴特写踩在沙地上，靴下沙粒被压陷可见。从靴部起重剑沿画面垂直轴向上贯穿整个画面，剑刃面对镜头，剑身做旧金属#5C544A上每一个铆钉与机械管线都清晰可见，磨损痕迹与雕刻标记被左上方阳光勾出锐利高光。画面顶部隐约露出主角下颌剪影。背景虚化的沙丘斜坡有大气雾化感。色调：做旧金属#5C544A与前景暖沙#B8804A形成主导对比。配音：靴下沙粒压陷的咯吱声+剑身金属的低沉嗡鸣余韵。

【镜头4 / 主角后方广角动态 / 约2秒】
硬切。@素材1背影三分之四角度，正冲入对手群中，身体在水平挥剑中段旋转。重剑向左侧画弧极限延展，剑刃带出真实的摄影运动模糊轨迹。剑势扫起一道弧形沙幕——大量飞沙颗粒在空中形成扇形墙，扬尘色#E8DCC4弥漫画面中段。3个对手被剑势带起的气浪和沙墙击退，身体向后翻滚远离主角，手中弯刀被震飞在空中翻转，破布残片散落。大衣完全展开在身后，红色内衬#6B1F22大幅外翻。背景另有武装人员正在反应后退。低水平线，天空压在画面顶部一窄条。视觉重点是剑势的气浪与沙墙，对手只是被冲击力击退翻倒，无伤害细节。色调：扬尘奶白#E8DCC4弥漫，主角黑红#1A1518+#6B1F22色块切入。配音：剑刃破空的低沉嗡鸣+布料撕裂声。

【镜头5 / 低角度严格侧面跟拍 / 约1.5秒】
硬切。@素材1严格左侧侧面，身体压低做对角线挥剑。重剑从画面右上向左下挥落，剑刃在与前景对手举起防御的弯刀相遇的瞬间被定格——金属对金属的格挡。火星从接触点以辐射状真实摄影颗粒迸射。对手的弯刀在剑势重压下弯曲变形，身体被冲击力推得向后倾倒，双脚在沙地上被推出滑痕。背景清晰对焦中：第二个对手正举刀扑来，他的面部破布在动作中飘动。@素材1的面部局部可见：冷静控制的表情，嘴角微抿，发丝被运动带起。低角度机位带到她的靴部和倒退对手的身体在画面底部。视觉重点是金属相击的火星和力量对抗，无伤害。色调：做旧金属#5C544A剑身与对手铁锈色#7A4530武器对撞，火星橙色高光点缀。配音：金属重击的钝响+火星迸裂的脆响。

【镜头6 / 极近特写双眼横向 / 约1秒】
硬切。@素材1的双眼横向特写充满整个画面宽度。黑色刘海发丝散落在额头上方，每根发丝有摄影细节。双眼极致细节渲染：真实虹膜纹理、瞳孔、下睫毛线、眼角的微妙肌肉张力、眼下皮肤纹理。右瞳孔中映出微小但清晰可辨的影像：对手举刀扑来的剪影。表情：绝对的冷静专注，无愤怒无恐惧无费力。背景完全虚化为抽象的暗调，将焦点彻底锁在双眼上。色调：肤色#D9B89C与披风黑#1A1518的近距对比，瞳孔深处映出微小剪影。配音：风声压低至几乎只剩低频心跳节奏。

【镜头7 / 中景三分之四正面对称构图 / 约1.5秒】
硬切。@素材1单膝下跪深蹲（右膝触地左腿支撑），重剑横置胸前作格挡，双手紧握。两把弯刀同时从画面左上和右上砍落在剑面平面上——她在同时格挡两路攻击。两个接触点同时迸出辐射状火星颗粒。两个攻击的对手可见于画面左右两侧边缘，身体压向劈砍动作有可见的肌肉张力，面部破布下可见费力表情。另两名对手正从画面极左右两端举刀压上准备攻击。@素材1的表情：专注，下颌紧绷，控制——没有恐慌。大衣布料铺散在身周沙地上，真实布料厚重感与褶皱细节。她下蹲处沙地有真实压陷。视觉重点是双重格挡的瞬间，金属对金属的火星。色调：做旧金属#5C544A主导画面中央，焦褐#5C3A24对手从两侧压入。配音：双重金属重击同步炸响+四周对手压迫的呼喝声。

【镜头8 / 极仰角地面视角爆发上升 / 约2秒】
硬切。机位置于沙地极低视角向上仰拍。@素材1从下蹲位置猛然爆发性向上起身，重剑在向上半圆弧中挥起，剑刃在画面上半部分划过天空带出真实运动模糊轨迹。原本压制她的两个对手被她爆发的反击之势震飞向后上方，身体在空中翻滚后退，肢体散开做翻滚动作，他们的弯刀脱手在空中旋转飞舞。她的大衣在动作顶点完全展开在身后，红色内衬#6B1F22大幅外翻，真实重布料的物理运动。她的面部从下方仰角可见：微露牙齿用力，发丝被上扬的动作带起，表情从控制转为控制下的爆发力。背景：戏剧性的天空云带充满画面上半部。她脚边沙粒被带飞形成尘云。视觉重点是她爆发起身的力量与气势——对手是被震飞翻滚，不是被伤害，无任何受伤细节。色调：天空灰蓝#8A95A0作背景，披风绛红#6B1F22在画面顶部绽开成视觉爆点。配音：剑势破空的强烈嗡鸣+重靴蹬沙的爆发声。

【镜头9 / 极远景俯拍 / 与镜头1机位完全相同 / 约1.5秒】
硬切。机位、角度、镜头与镜头1完全一致。@素材1此刻站在画面正中心的沙丘顶端，重剑斜插入身旁沙地，左手轻搭剑柄——与镜头1完全相同的姿势但位置从画面侧面移到了正中。她身周沙地上躺着7个被击败的对手呈不规则分布，每个倒下的人都保持完整摄影细节但无任何伤害细节，他们只是静止地躺在沙地上像是失去意识，宽松的衣物自然散落，弯刀掉落在身边。极长的夕阳投影从她和倒下的对手身上向画面右下方拉出（光向从镜头1切换为低角度西斜阳光，影子明显更长）。天空中云层在画面顶部更密集，太阳已经西沉，天空仅余顶部一抹血锈红#4A1E1E夕照。大气雾化更明显。视觉回环必须精确：与镜头1同样的人物姿势/剑的位置/沙丘脊。差异：她从画面边缘移到了正中，远处逼近的人变成了脚下静止倒下的人，光线从正午变成了夕阳。色调：阴影深褐#3D2E26拉长，地面前景暖沙#B8804A，天空压成血锈红#4A1E1E与灰蓝过渡。配音：风声回归低频呜咽，但比镜头1更空旷——所有人声消失。

整体画面要求：每一镜都是真人电影武侠动作戏实拍彩色质感，35mm彩色胶片摄影，褪色油画色调，自然光主导，真实景深与运动模糊。9个镜头之间灯光方向统一（左上方光源，仅镜头9转为低角度西斜），色卡分配严格遵循@素材2，角色服装造型武器完全一致，沙尘飘动方向统一（右向左）。所有对手保持设计一致。武术动作美学：强调力量、气势、招式之美，对手是被气浪、冲击力、格挡之势所震退/翻滚/倒下，全程无伤害细节、无血、无伤口、无破损人体描写。

35mm Kodak Vision3 500T film stock, Christopher Doyle cinematography, Wong Kar-wai painterly desaturation, Mad Max post-action aftermath aesthetic, fine organic film grain, low contrast middle grey tonality, slightly soft cinematic focus, restrained color palette with slight gray cast.

Sound: ambient desert wind throughout, no music, diegetic creature vocalizations and dialogue only.

HARD CUT 5:超近景固定镜头,extreme close-up景别,丹眉@图1 的整张脸部完全填满画面,她侧脸贴在甲板木板上,头部朝向画面左侧,头发散落在额头和木板上,辫坠的红绳贴在脸颊旁边的木板上,光线从画面左侧斜射照亮她左半脸,右半脸冷蓝阴影,,飞船甲板木质纹理在脸颊旁边虚化作背景。丹眉眉头紧蹙，正在闭眼做噩梦。


### 第85条｜A weapon design reference sheet in 4x1

A weapon design reference sheet in 4x16:9 ultra-wide horizontal canvas format displayed on a clean off-white studio background #F0EDE7 with grid-divided panels, museum-quality product photography aesthetic, photographic still life of a real physical handcrafted weapon prop, real material textures captured through a camera lens, not 3D rendering, not CG, not digital painting, hyperrealistic industrial weapon photography, tactile bronze and copper metalwork, soft diffused studio lighting from upper left, subtle drop shadow on background, subtle film grain.

Title text at top center reading "渡 — DU — COMPANION-WEAPON DESIGN SHEET" in clean modern sans-serif typography.

Four panels arranged in a 2x2 grid layout across the ultra-wide canvas, each panel itself in horizontal wide proportion to accommodate the horizontal weapon layout, separated by thin grid lines, each panel labeled at the bottom center with small capital letters.

The weapon depicted in all four panels is the greatsword shown in the reference image — its silhouette, proportions, structural design, every mechanical detail, the venting array at the blade tip, the blade body construction, the mid-blade module, the tubing system, the grip form, and the pommel must all faithfully match the reference image. Do not redesign, do not stylize, do not add elements not present in the reference.

The material language of the weapon also follows the reference image exactly — densely packed antique bronze hexagonal plates with #7A6845 muted bronze-green base color, #846035 darker bronze recesses, ancient seal-script symbol relief casting on each hexagonal plate, thin glowing cyan #4FC3D9 seam lines between plates, oxidation patches and verdigris in deeper crevices, real metal weathering not digital wear, surface scratches showing dull underlying bronze beneath the patina, faint cyan glow inside the venting structure, segmented antique bronze tubing system with bronze ring couplings and cyan glow at joints, reddish-copper #A85530 spiral-threaded grip with warm copper patina, small reddish-copper pommel sphere at the grip base — all consistent with reference image.

[TOP LEFT PANEL — labeled "FULL FRONT"]
The full weapon shown from the front, displayed horizontally with the blade pointing to the left and the hilt pointing to the right, the entire weapon laid out horizontally across the full width of the panel filling the available horizontal space, blade tip near the left edge of the panel and pommel near the right edge. Front-facing view showing the full width and silhouette of the blade. All structural and material details exactly per the reference image.

[TOP RIGHT PANEL — labeled "FULL SIDE"]
The same weapon viewed from the side where the jet venting array and the tubing system are exposed — looking at the weapon from the back-of-blade direction so that the full jet exhaust manifold structure is visible head-on at one end, the segmented bronze tubing system running along the back spine of the blade is fully revealed in three-dimensional depth with all the bronze ring couplings and cyan glow joints clearly visible, all the mechanical engine components that face outward from the weapon's back are shown. The weapon is displayed horizontally with the venting end pointing to the left and the hilt pointing to the right, laid out horizontally across the full width of the panel. This is the engine-side reveal view, not the blade-edge profile view. All structural and material details exactly per the reference image.

[BOTTOM LEFT PANEL — labeled "VENT DETAIL"]
A close-up macro shot of the top venting structure of the blade, framed tightly so the venting fills most of the panel with a small portion of the adjacent hexagonal bronze plates visible for context. Macro lens detail revealing the bronze patina texture, verdigris in seams, faint cyan #4FC3D9 glow visible deep inside the vent slots. All structural and material details exactly per the reference image.

[BOTTOM RIGHT PANEL — labeled "GRIP DETAIL"]
A close-up macro shot of the grip and pommel region of the weapon, framed to show the lower portion of the bronze blade transitioning through the bronze guard piece into the reddish-copper spiral grip and ending at the copper pommel sphere. Macro lens detail revealing the warm-cool material contrast between the bronze blade base and the reddish-copper grip, the spiral threading on the grip catching the studio key light. All structural and material details exactly per the reference image.

Overall presentation: 4x16:9 ultra-wide horizontal canvas with off-white studio background, four panels in 2x2 grid layout, soft diffused lighting from upper left, subtle film grain across the entire image, neutral color grading emphasizing the bronze and copper tones, photographic depth of field with each panel sharp throughout, no flashy CGI effects, this is a design reference sheet treating the weapon as if it were a real physical museum prop photographed by a product photographer, the consistency of antique bronze hexagonal plate material and warm copper grip material across all four panels is critical, the visual identity of the weapon must match the reference image one-to-one.


### 第86条｜The top one-third of the frame is domi

A 4:3 aspect ratio cinematic film poster composition for a Chinese sci-fi mythology epic.

OVERALL COMPOSITION (top to bottom):
The top one-third of the frame is dominated by the colossal lower jaw, neck, and upper chest armor of an ancient towering mecha looming on the far horizon, so massive that even at great distance it fills the entire upper portion of the sky. The middle and lower two-thirds of the frame is the desert landscape with a young Daoist priestess standing in dynamic frozen pose at the center, framed from waist-up. The horizon line sits at roughly 60 percent up the frame.

THE COLOSSAL MECHA (background, upper third of frame, rendered as half-silhouette with edge-light only):
The hulking lower head, neck, and chest segment of a ruined ancient Chinese war-god mecha, partially obscured by atmospheric haze and rising sand-dust. The head itself is cropped out of frame above — only from the lower jaw downward is visible. Visible elements: a heavy mechanical jaw segment with gold-trimmed dark red lacquered armor plating, layered dragon-scale neck guard plates wrapping around a thick neck of exposed brass piping and dark cabling, broad shoulder pauldrons rising at the edges, the upper edge of an ornate breast plate inscribed with faded gold seal-script Chinese calligraphy and dragon-cloud motifs. Tattered red banner cloth fragments hang in shreds from the shoulders, frayed at the edges, gold embroidered characters barely visible. The mecha is DEAD — no glowing eyes (head not visible), no light pulses on the armor seals, the inscriptions weathered and dull, the metal heavily oxidized with rust-bloom and sand-pitting, faint sand-streaks running down the lacquered surfaces from centuries of desert wind. Its facing is straight ahead toward the camera, head pointing skyward beyond the frame, neither looking down nor turning aside — the posture of an enormous ancient statue that has stopped functioning but still stands. The entire mecha is rendered as a deep silhouette with light coming only from camera-left, catching the edge of the jaw, the rim of the shoulder pauldron, and the top edge of the breastplate in warm rim-light. The interior of the form falls into deep cool-blue shadow with only faintest hint of dark red lacquer color and oxidized brass undertones. Strong atmospheric haze and sand-particle dust diffuses the form, making it feel both enormous and distant.

THE YOUNG WOMAN (foreground center, mid-frame to lower-frame, waist-up):
A young East Asian woman in her late teens, framed from waist up, standing alone in the center of the frame against the desert. Her body is caught mid-pose in a dramatic dynamic posture: her torso arches backward as if struck by an unseen force or wind from above, her right arm raised high overhead with elbow bent and forearm crossing over her forehead in a partial shielding gesture, her left hand brought to her hip with fingers splayed slightly. Her face is angled upward toward the sky, chin lifted, mouth slightly open with a small gasp of breath, neck exposed in a long arc. Her eyes are open and glowing a vivid blood-crimson red, sharp and supernatural, the irises bright like lit coals while the whites of her eyes remain natural — this is the only fully saturated red in the entire image, drawing the eye immediately. Her short black bob haircut with straight blunt bangs is being whipped sideways by a strong upward wind, strands of jet-black hair fanning out to camera-right horizontally. From the left side of her hair dangles two thin dark-red braided cord tassels with small knotted ends, also flying outward in the wind. A small wooden hairpin is visible.

Her clothing: a high-collared cream-white linen mandarin-collar tunic top with diagonal cross-front cloth-knot closures, the long blousy sleeves billowing dramatically as wind catches them and pushes them upward, the cuffs gathered at her wrists. Visible at her waist line: the high black waistband of her long dark skirt with a crimson red braided cord sash tied at the front, the two long ends of the sash whipping sideways in the wind. A small worn brown canvas talisman satchel hangs at her right hip, with strips of yellow paper talismans bearing faded red brush-stroke Chinese characters stuck onto its flap, the paper edges fluttering in the wind. Her skin shows natural East Asian young woman texture with visible pores, faint imperfections, soft fine peach fuzz catching the warm side-light, no plastic smoothing, no beauty filter retouching.

THE DESERT ENVIRONMENT:
A vast expanse of golden ochre sand stretching to the horizon, with low wind-sculpted dunes catching warm light on one side and falling into cool shadow on the other. In the deep middle-ground, the silhouettes of the eroded ramparts of an ancient frontier fortress — Yumen Pass — stretch across the horizon as a low jagged line, partially obscured by the standing colossus and atmospheric haze. The sand surface near the camera is rippled by wind, scattered with small fragments of weathered mechanical debris — a half-buried curved blade tip, a corroded gear, a broken finger-joint of giant scale — hinting that more dead mechs lie beneath. Wind-blown dust and sand particles drift across the frame.

THE SKY:
A dramatic late-afternoon sky filling the entire upper portion of the frame around the colossal mecha silhouette. Towering cumulus thunderhead cloud columns rise behind the mecha, their tops painted in warm golden-amber where the lowering sun strikes them, their underbellies and recessed shadows in deep cool blue-grey. Patches of clear sky show a saturated but not oversaturated cobalt blue. The clouds have painterly depth and substance, not flat. Warm golden hour light streams from the camera-left direction at a low angle, casting long shadows from the dunes and from the figure across the sand.

TITLE TYPOGRAPHY:
Across the lower portion of the frame, below the figure and floating above the sand, the Chinese film title "万物生" (three Chinese characters meaning "All Things Born / All Things Arise") rendered in elegant calligraphic seal-script style, large but not overpowering, the characters arranged horizontally. The brushwork is bold and weathered, executed in deep ink-black tone with slight texture of dry-brush effect at the edges. The characters sit naturally in the lower-third sand area as if etched into the desert plane, scaled to read clearly from a distance but not crowding the figure. No other text on the poster.

STYLE LOCK:
35mm Kodak Vision3 500T film stock aesthetic, Christopher Doyle and Roger Deakins cinematography influence, Frank Frazetta fantasy poster painterly composition, Akira Kurosawa epic-scale landscape framing. Fine organic film grain. Low-medium contrast middle-grey tonality with restrained color palette: warm umber ochre sand (#A88554), dark blood-red lacquer accents (#7A2620), deep oxidized brass (#5A4838), warm golden cloud highlights (#D4A857), cool blue-grey cloud shadows (#3D5266), cobalt sky (#4A6F8A), brilliant crimson red eyes only on the figure (#B91C2A) as the sole high-saturation accent. Slight gray tone overall, NOT oversaturated, NOT HDR-pumped. Anamorphic widescreen lens character with subtle barrel curvature at edges and natural lens flare from the side-light.

Generate a 4:3 aspect ratio Chinese cinematic film poster combining these two references into a single new composition, in the visual language of a flat graphic-design poster with a single character figure over a patterned typographic background — similar in structural approach to a Neon Genesis Evangelion character poster where the character dominates the frame against a dense text-pattern backdrop.

COMPOSITION:
The young woman from Reference 1 is the dominant subject filling almost the entire frame. She occupies roughly the central 75-80 percent of the canvas vertically — from below her feet at the lower edge up to near the top of her raised hand at the upper edge of the frame. Her full body is visible (head to feet), centered horizontally. She is in the exact dynamic pose from Reference 2, with her hair, sleeves, sash ends, and talisman papers being blown by a strong upward wind.

Her eyes are the only modification from Reference 1: they now glow a vivid blood-crimson red (#C41E2E to #D32436), supernatural and luminous like lit coals, with subtle internal glow — the irises bright red while the whites of the eyes remain natural. Everything else in her appearance — face, hairstyle, hair tassels, hairpin, full clothing, satchel — is preserved exactly from Reference 1.

BACKGROUND — DEEP-TONED VERTICAL BAMBOO SLIP TEXT WALL:
The entire background behind the woman is a dense, flat, wall-like pattern of ancient Chinese vertical bamboo slip strips arranged side by side, completely filling the canvas from edge to edge. Each bamboo slip is a long vertical strip of weathered DEEP AGED bamboo, rendered in dark caramel-brown tones (#6B4F30 to #4A3520 range) — like ancient bamboo slips that have been buried for centuries, dust-coated, shadowed, and aged. The bamboo is significantly darker than fresh tan-beige bamboo, sitting closer to dark mahogany and aged leather tones. The slips are bound by faint horizontal cord lines, with vertical columns of Chinese seal-script (篆书) and clerical-script (隶书) characters brushed in deep ink-black down each strip, reading top-to-bottom, right-to-left in traditional Chinese book layout.

The text content on the bamboo slips is a dense mixture of project-thematic keywords, Daoist scripture fragments, and Shan Hai Jing (Classic of Mountains and Seas) excerpts, with the following keywords appearing prominently and repeatedly across the bamboo slip field, in legible large characters:

万物生・超度・机甲尸骨・盘古既死・九州破碎・道生一・一生二・二生三・三生万物・山海有兽・神兽既亡・关羽・玉门关・西出阳关・符箓・往生・尘归尘・土归土・血肉沉沙・钢骨为山・天地不仁・以万物为刍狗

Additional smaller bamboo slips between these keywords contain longer columns of unreadable but properly-formed seal-script and clerical-script Chinese characters resembling classical Daoist text and Shan Hai Jing-style ancient prose, creating a dense textural pattern of language.

The bamboo wall has heavy weathering and age — some slips lightly cracked, some characters faded, a faint patina of dust. It is rendered as a flat backdrop with NO perspective depth, NO three-dimensional space behind the woman — purely a wall of text-bamboo at the same depth across the canvas. Subtle warm side-light from camera-left gives the bamboo a gentle low-key gradient from slightly less-dark on the left to deeper-dark on the right, but the overall tone remains deep and shadowed throughout. The bamboo wall should read as a dark, moody, weathered backdrop — not as a bright surface competing with the figure.

COLOR & TONE STRATEGY:
The bamboo slip background is rendered in DEEP low-saturation aged earth tones — dark caramel, aged mahogany, dust-shadowed brown, with deep ink-black characters. It is the BACKGROUND PATTERN, deliberately muted and sitting in shadow.

The young woman is rendered with cinematic presence: her white tunic catches warm side-light from camera-left and stands out as a bright element against the dark background, her black skirt sits in cool shadow on its right side, her skin reads in natural mid-tone warm light. Her crimson red sash and red braided hair tassels are intensified to vivid, luminous crimson red (#A8202C to #C42435) — bold and saturated, reading as bright red brushstrokes against the dark bamboo. Her crimson red glowing eyes (#C41E2E to #D32436) are the single highest-saturation point in the entire image, with a subtle supernatural luminosity. The waist sash ribbons trailing through the center of the frame cut bold red lines across the dark background, leading the viewer's eye upward toward her face and eyes.

THE CONTRAST PRINCIPLE: A mid-to-bright tone figure (white tunic + warm skin + vivid red accents) stands forward against a DEEP dark text wall. The woman's silhouette must read instantly against the bamboo pattern, never blending. The red accents (eyes, sash, tassel) are the only fully saturated colors in the entire image — they pop as the visual hierarchy peak.

TITLE TYPOGRAPHY:
The Chinese film title "万物生" rendered in three large bold weathered ink-brushed seal-script characters, placed prominently — suggested position: vertically arranged down the right side of the frame (right margin), three characters stacked top-to-bottom, in deep ink-black with slight dry-brush edge texture, large enough to read instantly. The title sits on top of the bamboo slip background but is clearly distinct from the small bamboo-slip text — it is the dominant typographic element on the canvas. No other large text on the poster.

A dramatic late-afternoon sky filling the upper portion around the mecha silhouette. Towering cumulus thunderhead cloud columns rise behind the mecha, their tops painted in warm golden-amber where the lowering sun strikes them, their underbellies and recessed shadows in deep cool blue-grey. Patches of clear sky show a saturated but not oversaturated cobalt blue. Painterly cloud depth and substance, not flat. Warm golden hour light streams from the camera-left direction at a low angle, casting long shadows from the dunes and from the woman across the sand.

### 第90条｜— Reference: the male character. Prese

REFERENCE IMAGE:
@图1 — Reference: the male character. Preserve his identity, full body design, the black LED-panel face with two glowing teal-green light points serving as eyes, his messy black human hair falling over the panel, his mechanical segmented neck, his teal-green crystal earrings, his costume (burgundy/dark-red sleeveless long vest with leather and metal armor accents, cream-white billowing pirate sleeves, dark burgundy fitted pants, brown leather over-the-knee boots, brown leather messenger satchel, brass ring buckle on the chest, dark leather strap diagonally across torso), all material qualities, color palette, and overall aesthetic exactly from this reference. He is a young man, lean build, late teens to early twenties.

INSTRUCTION:
Generate a 16:9 aspect ratio editorial magazine spread for a male character introduction page, in the visual language of contemporary high-fashion editorial design (i-D Magazine, Dazed, AnOther Magazine reference). This is the second card in a series — the first card featured a female character in the same editorial style. This card must feel like a companion piece to the first card while introducing this male character as a separate identity.

COMPOSITION (16:9 landscape):

The young man from the Reference image stands in the center-LEFT portion of the frame, occupying approximately 35-40 percent of the canvas width horizontally and 70-75 percent of the canvas height vertically. He is rendered FULL BODY from feet to the top of his head, placed in an extreme dynamic JoJo-style pose described in detail below.

THE POSE (CRITICAL — this is a stylized exaggerated JoJo Hirohiko Araki pose, not a natural stance):

His body is bent backward in an extreme arching pose, like DIO's iconic backward-arch pose from JoJo's Bizarre Adventure — spine curved into a deep reverse C-shape, joints pushed beyond natural anatomical range to appear almost dislocated. Specifically:

LOWER BODY: Both legs planted firmly on the ground, knees bent and pushed forward aggressively, hips thrust forward toward the camera. The legs form a stable wide base while everything above the hips falls backward.

TORSO: His spine arches dramatically BACKWARD, the entire upper body tilted back so that his chest is angled upward toward the sky. The reverse C-curve through his spine is extreme — his back is nearly parallel to the ground at the shoulders. His burgundy vest stretches and bunches with the arch, the cream-white pirate sleeves billow with the extreme posture.

HEAD AND FACE PANEL: His head falls all the way back so that his black LED face panel points STRAIGHT UP toward the sky, the two teal-green light points facing the heavens. His messy black hair cascades downward away from the panel, hanging loose toward the ground behind him as gravity pulls it. The mechanical segmented neck is extended and exposed by this backward arch, the metal joints visibly stretched, the teal-green crystal earrings dangling outward from his ears.

RIGHT ARM: His right arm is flung backward and outward, reaching behind his body at an extreme angle as if grasping for something invisible behind him — the arm is fully extended, fingers splayed open and reaching, the wrist twisted at an awkward angle. The arm forms a sharp diagonal line cutting across the frame.

LEFT ARM: His left hand is brought up to touch his own mechanical neck — fingers curled and pressing against the metal segments of his throat, as if confirming he still exists, or as if he is about to remove his own panel face. The hand is delicate, the gesture intimate and strange.

OVERALL: The pose is theatrical, sculptural, exaggerated, deliberately impossible-looking. This is NOT a natural standing pose. This is a JoJo Bizarre Adventure pose — body language that defies gravity and anatomy, used to express character through silhouette alone. His silhouette must read as instantly dramatic and strange. The pose is reminiscent of DIO's "WRYYY" backward-arch but adapted for a melancholic, mysterious character rather than a triumphant villain.

THE CORE TENSION OF THE POSE:
His body is performing one of the most expressive, emotionally-exposed poses in visual language — head thrown back, body arched, arm flung wide as if howling at the sky or breaking apart in ecstasy/agony. But his face is a featureless black LED panel incapable of any expression, and the panel points uselessly at the sky showing nothing but two faint teal lights. The body screams; the face says nothing. Meanwhile his own hand touches his neck, as if asking "am I still here?" This contradiction — extreme emotional body posture + completely unreadable face — is the visual essence of this character.

COLOR PALETTE (60-30-10 RULE — calibrated specifically for this character's costume colors):

This character's costume features burgundy/dark-red vest, cream-white shirt, brown leather boots and satchel — a warm-leaning palette. To make these elements pop with clean figure-ground separation, this card uses a COOL-toned background palette that contrasts with his warm costume, while maintaining the structural design system of the series (60-30-10 with one accent color signature per character).

60% DOMINANT — Deep Stone Blue-Grey Background:
The dominant background filling most of the canvas is a deep stone blue-grey (#4A5A5C to #3D5560 range) — like weathered bronze, dark-wet slate, or an evening mountain silhouette. NOT bright teal, NOT navy, NOT pure grey — a muted cool tone with a subtle blue-green undertone that sits in the mid-to-deep range. The surface has subtle paper-grain or matte stone texture, slightly weathered, with very faint variation in tone across the canvas to avoid feeling flat. This cool deep background creates strong contrast with the character's warm costume: his burgundy vest reads darker but tonally separated against the cool blue-grey, his cream-white shirt pops brightly against it, his brown leather boots create warm-cool contrast against the cool background.

30% SECONDARY — Smoked Earth Brown Color Block:
A large rectangular deep smoked earth-brown color block (#5A3D2D to #4A3525 range — a rich dark brown with red undertones, like burnt umber or aged leather, NOT pure black, NOT light brown) occupies the upper-right portion of the canvas, roughly 25-30 percent of the canvas area. This warm-dark block contains the main editorial title and the introductory data, set in light cream-white type (#F0E8D8). The block has clean rectangular edges with slight rough/printed quality at the edges (like risograph or offset print).

The warm-brown color block on the cool blue-grey background creates a complementary warm-cool block contrast that gives the layout strong editorial structure.

10% ACCENT — Teal-Green Highlights:
The accent color is TEAL-GREEN (#4DD4C4 to #6BE0CC range — same teal-green as his eye lights and earrings). On the cool deep stone background, this saturated teal will pop dramatically like glowing lights in a dim room. The teal-green appears in exactly four places: (1) his two glowing eye light points on the face panel, (2) his teal-green crystal earrings, (3) one small teal-green ink stamp on the editorial collage area, (4) one small teal-green brushstroke or label accent on one of the collage elements. NO other teal-green anywhere else.

SERIES DESIGN SYSTEM NOTE:
The first character card in this series used: warm beige background + charcoal-green block + crimson red accent (a warm-base palette suited to that character's white/black/red costume).
This second card uses: cool stone blue-grey background + smoked earth-brown block + teal-green accent (a cool-base palette suited to this character's burgundy/cream/brown costume).
The two cards form a warm/cool diptych, with the same 60-30-10 structural system applied with character-specific palette calibration.

EDITORIAL COLLAGE ELEMENTS (arranged asymmetrically in the right 55-60% of canvas):

1. MAIN TITLE BLOCK (top right, inside the charcoal dark block):
At the top of the charcoal block, the character name "江流" rendered in two large bold contemporary Chinese typeface characters (sans-serif modernist style, NOT brush calligraphy — contemporary fashion magazine title typography), in cream-white color (#F0E8D8) on the charcoal background. Two or three small torn paper note fragments, off-white/aged paper, with question marks "?" scrawled on them in different sizes and styles — some printed, some handwritten, some smudged. Tucked into the page at slight angles, some edges curling, some partially overlapping.

6. TEAL-GREEN INK STAMP (one prominent location):
